import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { AuditLog } from "@/lib/models/AuditLog";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";
import {
    AUDIT_LOG_PUBLIC_FIELDS,
    AUDIT_LOG_READ_PERMISSION,
    listAuditLogsQuerySchema,
    publicAuditLog,
} from "@/lib/auditLogs/auditLogApi";
import {
    errorResponse,
    validationErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/users/userApi";

/*
 * Audit logs are written ONLY by trusted server-side code through
 * AuditLogService (inside the same transaction as the change they record).
 * There is deliberately no POST handler: clients must never be able to
 * create audit records, choose the actor/device, or fabricate events such
 * as "user.admin_granted". Next.js answers other methods with 405.
 */

// GET /api/audit-logs?page=1&limit=50&action=user.created&entity=User&entityId=...
export async function GET(
    request: NextRequest
) {
    try {
        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        await connectDB();

        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            userId: context.userId,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
            roleId: context.roleId,
            permission: AUDIT_LOG_READ_PERMISSION,
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const parsedQuery = listAuditLogsQuerySchema.safeParse(
            Object.fromEntries(request.nextUrl.searchParams)
        );

        if (!parsedQuery.success) {
            return validationErrorResponse(
                "Invalid query parameters",
                parsedQuery.error
            );
        }

        const { page, limit, action, entity, entityId } =
            parsedQuery.data;

        // Tenant isolation: tenantId always comes from the verified session.
        const filter: Record<string, unknown> = {
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(context.tenantId),
        };

        if (action) filter.action = action;
        if (entity) filter.entity = entity;
        if (entityId) filter.entityId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(entityId);

        const [auditLogs, total] = await Promise.all([
            AuditLog.find(filter)
                .select(AUDIT_LOG_PUBLIC_FIELDS)
                .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
                .skip((page - 1) * limit)
                .limit(limit)
                .lean(),
            AuditLog.countDocuments(filter),
        ]);

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            auditLogs: auditLogs.map(publicAuditLog),
            pagination: {
                page,
                limit,
                total,
                totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit),
            },
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Audit log fetch error:",
            error
        );

        return errorResponse("Failed to fetch audit logs", 500);
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\audit-logs\[id]\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { AuditLog } from "@/lib/models/AuditLog";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";
import {
    AUDIT_LOG_PUBLIC_FIELDS,
    AUDIT_LOG_READ_PERMISSION,
    publicAuditLog,
} from "@/lib/auditLogs/auditLogApi";
import { errorResponse } from "@/lib/users/userApi";

type RouteContext = {
    params: Promise<{
        id: string;
    }>;
};

export async function GET(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        const authContext =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!authContext) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        await connectDB();

        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            userId: authContext.userId,
            tenantId: authContext.tenantId,
            roleId: authContext.roleId,
            permission: AUDIT_LOG_READ_PERMISSION,
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const { id } = await context.params;

        // Validate audit log ID
        if (!/^[a-f\d]{24}$/i.test(id)) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid audit log ID", 400);
        }

        const auditLog = await AuditLog.findOne({
            _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),

            // Tenant isolation:
            // only allow the authenticated user's tenant
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                authContext.tenantId
            ),
        })
            .select(AUDIT_LOG_PUBLIC_FIELDS)
            .lean();

        if (!auditLog) {
            return errorResponse("Audit log not found", 404);
        }

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            auditLog: publicAuditLog(auditLog),
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Audit log fetch error:", error);

        return errorResponse("Failed to fetch audit log", 500);
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\auth\password.ts ==========
import argon2 from "argon2";

export async function hashPassword(
    password: string
): Promise<string> {
    return argon2.hash(password, {
        type: argon2.argon2id,
    });
}

export async function verifyPassword(
    password: string,
    passwordHash: string
): Promise<boolean> {
    return argon2.verify(passwordHash, password);
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\auth\session.ts ==========
import { SignJWT, jwtVerify, type JWTPayload } from "jose";

export interface SessionPayload extends JWTPayload {
    // Random server-side session ID. Its SHA-256 hash is stored in the
    // Session collection; the session is valid only while that record is
    // active (see lib/auth/sessionStore.ts).
    sid: string;
    userId: string;
    tenantId: string;
    deviceId: string;
    roleId?: string;
}

const SESSION_SECRET =
    process.env.SESSION_SECRET;

if (!SESSION_SECRET) {
    throw new Error(
        "SESSION_SECRET is not defined"
    );
}

const secretKey =
    new TextEncoder().encode(SESSION_SECRET);

export async function createSessionToken(
    payload: SessionPayload,
    expiresAt: Date
): Promise<string> {
    return new SignJWT(payload)
        .setProtectedHeader({
            alg: "HS256",
        })
        .setIssuedAt()
        .setExpirationTime(
            Math.floor(expiresAt.getTime() / 1000)
        )
        .sign(secretKey);
}

export async function verifySessionToken(
    token: string
): Promise<SessionPayload | null> {
    try {
        const { payload } =
            await jwtVerify(
                token,
                secretKey,
                { algorithms: ["HS256"] }
            );

        // Tokens issued before server-side sessions existed have no
        // "sid" and are rejected: those users must log in again once.
        if (
            typeof payload.sid !== "string" ||
            typeof payload.userId !== "string" ||
            typeof payload.tenantId !== "string" ||
            typeof payload.deviceId !== "string"
        ) {
            return null;
        }

        return {
            sid: payload.sid,
            userId: payload.userId,
            tenantId: payload.tenantId,
            deviceId: payload.deviceId,
            roleId:
                typeof payload.roleId === "string"
                    ? payload.roleId
                    : undefined,
        };
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\auth\change-password\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose, { Types } from "mongoose";
import { z } from "zod";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { User } from "@/lib/models/User";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hashPassword, verifyPassword } from "@/app/api/auth/password";
import {
    createUserSession,
    revokeAllUserSessions,
    SESSION_TTL_SECONDS,
} from "@/lib/auth/sessionStore";
import {
    RateLimitUnavailableError,
    checkLoginRateLimit,
    getClientIp,
    recordLoginFailure,
} from "@/lib/auth/loginRateLimit";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";
import {
    errorResponse,
    passwordSchema,
    readJsonBody,
    validationErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/users/userApi";

const changePasswordSchema = z
    .strictObject({
        currentPassword: z.string().min(1).max(128),
        newPassword: passwordSchema,
    })
    .refine((data) => data.currentPassword !== data.newPassword, {
        message: "The new password must be different from the current password",
        path: ["newPassword"],
    });

function sessionCookieOptions(maxAge: number) {
    return {
        httpOnly: true,
        secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
        sameSite: "lax" as const,
        path: "/",
        maxAge,
    };
}

/*
 * POST /api/auth/change-password
 *
 * The signed-in user changes their own password. On success EVERY session
 * of the user is revoked (including copies of the current token), and a
 * fresh session is issued for the current device only.
 *
 * Wrong current-password attempts count against the same rate limits as
 * failed logins, so a stolen session cannot be used to guess the password.
 */
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        // Restricted users (temporary password) must be able to reach this.
        const context = await getAuthenticatedContext(request, {
            allowPasswordChangeRequired: true,
        });

        if (!context) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        const json = await readJsonBody(request);

        if (!json.ok) {
            return errorResponse("Request body must be valid JSON", 400);
        }

        const parsed = changePasswordSchema.safeParse(json.body);

        if (!parsed.success) {
            return validationErrorResponse("Invalid password data", parsed.error);
        }

        const { currentPassword, newPassword } = parsed.data;

        await connectDB();

        const tenantId = new Types.ObjectId(context.tenantId);
        const userId = new Types.ObjectId(context.userId);

        const user = await User.findOne({ _id: userId, tenantId, status: "active" })
            .select("_id email passwordHash roleId")
            .lean();

        if (!user) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        const subject = { ip: getClientIp(request), email: user.email };
        const limit = await checkLoginRateLimit(subject);

        if (limit.limited) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Too many attempts. Please wait and try again." },
                { status: 429, headers: { "Retry-After": String(limit.retryAfterSeconds) } }
            );
        }

        if (!(await verifyPassword(currentPassword, user.passwordHash))) {
            await recordLoginFailure(subject);
            return errorResponse("Current password is incorrect", 400);
        }

        const passwordHash = await hashPassword(newPassword);
        const dbSession = await mongoose.startSession();

        try {
            await dbSession.withTransaction(async () => {
                // Only update if the password has not changed concurrently.
                const result = await User.updateOne(
                    { _id: userId, tenantId, status: "active", passwordHash: user.passwordHash },
                    // Choosing a new password lifts the temporary-password restriction.
                    { $set: { passwordHash, mustChangePassword: false } },
                    { session: dbSession }
                );

                if (result.modifiedCount !== 1) {
                    throw new Error("PASSWORD_CHANGED_CONCURRENTLY");
                }

                const revoked = await revokeAllUserSessions(
                    tenantId,
                    userId,
                    "password-changed",
                    dbSession
                );

                // Never store passwords or hashes in the audit log.
                await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                    {
                        userId,
                        tenantId,
                        deviceId: new Types.ObjectId(context.deviceId),
                    },
                    {
                        action: "user.password_changed",
                        entity: "User",
                        entityId: userId,
                        description: `User changed their own password; ${revoked} session(s) revoked`,
                        newValues: { revokedSessions: revoked },
                    },
                    dbSession
                );
            });
        } finally {
            await dbSession.endSession();
        }

        // All old sessions are revoked; give this device a new one.
        try {
            const { token } = await createUserSession({
                userId: context.userId,
                tenantId: context.tenantId,
                deviceId: context.deviceId,
                roleId: user.roleId?.toString(),
            });

            const response = NextResponse.json({
                success: true,
                message: "Password changed. You have been signed out on all other devices.",
            });
            response.cookies.set("session", token, sessionCookieOptions(SESSION_TTL_SECONDS));
            return response;
        } catch {
            const response = NextResponse.json({
                success: true,
                message: "Password changed. Please sign in again.",
                requireLogin: true,
            });
            response.cookies.set("session", "", { ...sessionCookieOptions(0), expires: new Date(0) });
            return response;
        }
    } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof RateLimitUnavailableError) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Password change is temporarily unavailable. Please try again shortly." },
                { status: 503, headers: { "Retry-After": "30" } }
            );
        }

        if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "PASSWORD_CHANGED_CONCURRENTLY") {
            return errorResponse("Your password was changed by another request. Please sign in again.", 409);
        }

        console.error(
            "Change password error:",
            error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown"
        );
        return errorResponse("Unable to change password", 500);
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\auth\login\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { z } from "zod";
import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { User } from "@/lib/models/User";
import { Tenant } from "@/lib/models/Tenant";
import { Device } from "@/lib/models/Device";
import { hashPassword, verifyPassword } from "@/app/api/auth/password";
import {
    createUserSession,
    SESSION_TTL_SECONDS,
} from "@/lib/auth/sessionStore";
import {
    RateLimitUnavailableError,
    checkLoginRateLimit,
    clearAccountLoginFailures,
    getClientIp,
    recordLoginFailure,
    type LoginAttemptSubject,
} from "@/lib/auth/loginRateLimit";

// Upper bound on accounts sharing one email across businesses,
// so a login request cannot trigger unbounded password hashing.
const MAX_LOGIN_CANDIDATES = 10;

// Matches the limits used when users are created (12-128 characters).
// The lower bound is 1 so older accounts with shorter passwords still work;
// the upper bound caps hashing work per request.
const loginSchema = z.object({
    email: z.string().trim().toLowerCase().min(1).max(254),
    password: z.string().min(1).max(128),
    deviceId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200),
});

const INVALID_CREDENTIALS = "Invalid email or password";

function jsonError(message: string, status: number, headers?: HeadersInit) {
    return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message },
        { status, headers }
    );
}

// Verified against when no account matches, so "unknown email" takes about
// as long as "wrong password" and timing does not reveal which emails exist.
let dummyHashPromise: Promise<string> | null = null;

function getDummyHash() {
    dummyHashPromise ??= hashPassword("timing-equalization-placeholder");
    return dummyHashPromise;
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    let body: unknown;

    try {
        body = await request.json();
    } catch {
        return jsonError("Request body must be valid JSON", 400);
    }

    const parsed = loginSchema.safeParse(body);

    if (!parsed.success) {
        return jsonError(
            "Email, password and deviceId are required (password at most 128 characters)",
            400
        );
    }

    const { email, password, deviceId } = parsed.data;

    const subject: LoginAttemptSubject = {
        ip: getClientIp(request),
        email,
    };

    const tooManyAttempts = (retryAfterSeconds: number) =>
        jsonError(
            "Too many login attempts. Please wait and try again.",
            429,
            { "Retry-After": String(retryAfterSeconds) }
        );

    // Record a failure, then answer with the given response. If the store
    // is down the login is refused rather than allowed without limits.
    const fail = async (response: NextResponse) => {
        await recordLoginFailure(subject);
        return response;
    };

    try {
        await connectDB();

        // 0. Rate limit BEFORE any password hashing.
        const limit = await checkLoginRateLimit(subject);

        if (limit.limited) {
            return tooManyAttempts(limit.retryAfterSeconds);
        }

        // 1. Find active users with this email.
        // Emails are unique per business, not globally, so the same
        // email may exist in several businesses.
        const candidates = await User.find({
            email,
            status: "active",
        })
            .limit(MAX_LOGIN_CANDIDATES)
            .lean();

        // 2. Keep only the accounts whose password matches.
        const passwordMatches: typeof candidates = [];

        if (!candidates.length) {
            await verifyPassword(password, await getDummyHash());
        }

        for (const candidate of candidates) {
            if (
                await verifyPassword(
                    password,
                    candidate.passwordHash
                )
            ) {
                passwordMatches.push(candidate);
            }
        }

        if (!passwordMatches.length) {
            return await fail(jsonError(INVALID_CREDENTIALS, 401));
        }

        // 3. The registered device identifies the business:
        // it must belong to one of the matching accounts.
        const devices = await Device.find({
            userId: {
                $in: passwordMatches.map((item) => item._id),
            },
            deviceId,
            status: "active",
        }).lean();

        if (devices.length > 1) {
            return await fail(
                jsonError(
                    "This device is registered to more than one business account. Contact support.",
                    409
                )
            );
        }

        const device = devices[0];

        const user = device
            ? passwordMatches.find((item) =>
                item._id.equals(device.userId) &&
                item.tenantId.equals(device.tenantId)
            )
            : undefined;

        // 4. Verify tenant
        const tenant = await Tenant.findOne({
            _id: (user ?? passwordMatches[0]).tenantId,
            status: "active",
        }).lean();

        if (!tenant) {
            return await fail(jsonError("Business account is inactive", 403));
        }

        // 5. Verify device belongs to this user
        if (!device || !user) {
            return await fail(
                jsonError("Device is not registered for this user", 403)
            );
        }

        // 6. Successful login: clear this account's failure counters.
        await clearAccountLoginFailures(subject);

        // 7. Create the server-side session and its signed token.
        const { token: sessionToken } =
            await createUserSession({
                userId: user._id.toString(),
                tenantId:
                    user.tenantId.toString(),
                deviceId: device._id.toString(),
                roleId:
                    user.roleId?.toString(),
            });

        // 8. Set secure HttpOnly cookie
        const response =
            NextResponse.json({
                success: true,
                message: "Login successful",
                user: {
                    id: user._id.toString(),
                    name: user.name,
                    email: user.email,
                    tenantId:
                        user.tenantId.toString(),
                    roleId:
                        user.roleId?.toString() ??
                        null,
                    deviceId:
                        device.deviceId,
                    mustChangePassword:
                        user.mustChangePassword === true,
                },
                tenant: {
                    id: tenant._id.toString(),
                    name: tenant.name,
                    businessType:
                        tenant.businessType ??
                        null,
                },
                device: {
                    id: device._id.toString(),
                    deviceId:
                        device.deviceId,
                    deviceName:
                        device.deviceName,
                    deviceType:
                        device.deviceType,
                },
            });

        response.cookies.set(
            "session",
            sessionToken,
            {
                httpOnly: true,
                secure:
                    process.env.NODE_ENV ===
                    "production",
                sameSite: "lax",
                path: "/",
                maxAge: SESSION_TTL_SECONDS,
            }
        );

        // Update device last seen
        await Device.updateOne(
            { _id: device._id },
            {
                $set: {
                    lastSeenAt: new Date(),
                },
            }
        );

        return response;
    } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof RateLimitUnavailableError) {
            // Fail closed: never allow logins without rate limiting.
            return jsonError(
                "Login is temporarily unavailable. Please try again shortly.",
                503,
                { "Retry-After": "30" }
            );
        }

        // Log only the error type and code (e.g. ECONNREFUSED): request
        // data such as the password is never logged.
        console.error(
            "Login error:",
            error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown",
            (error as { code?: unknown })?.code ?? ""
        );

        return jsonError(
            "An unexpected error occurred during login",
            500
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\auth\logout\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { verifySessionToken } from "@/app/api/auth/session";
import { revokeSession } from "@/lib/auth/sessionStore";

function clearSessionCookie(response: NextResponse) {
    response.cookies.set("session", "", {
        httpOnly: true,
        secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
        sameSite: "lax",
        path: "/",
        expires: new Date(0),
    });

    return response;
}

/*
 * Logout revokes the server-side session FIRST, so the token stops working
 * everywhere (including copies), then clears the browser cookie.
 * Only the session named by the caller's own validly signed token can be
 * revoked here.
 */
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    const token = request.cookies.get("session")?.value;
    const payload = token ? await verifySessionToken(token) : null;

    if (payload) {
        try {
            await connectDB();
            await revokeSession(payload, "logout");
        } catch (error) {
            console.error(
                "Logout revocation error:",
                error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown"
            );

            // The cookie is still cleared locally, but report honestly that
            // the server-side session may still be valid.
            return clearSessionCookie(
                NextResponse.json(
                    {
                        success: false,
                        message:
                            "Logged out on this device, but the session could not be revoked on the server. Please try again.",
                    },
                    { status: 503 }
                )
            );
        }
    }

    return clearSessionCookie(
        NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Logged out successfully",
        })
    );
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\auth\logout-all\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { Types } from "mongoose";

import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { revokeAllUserSessions } from "@/lib/auth/sessionStore";

// POST /api/auth/logout-all
// Revokes every session of the CALLER (all devices). Requires a valid
// session; it can never target another user.
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const context = await getAuthenticatedContext(request, {
            allowPasswordChangeRequired: true,
        });

        if (!context) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const revoked = await revokeAllUserSessions(
            new Types.ObjectId(context.tenantId),
            new Types.ObjectId(context.userId),
            "logout-all"
        );

        const response = NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Logged out of all sessions",
            revokedSessions: revoked,
        });

        response.cookies.set("session", "", {
            httpOnly: true,
            secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === "production",
            sameSite: "lax",
            path: "/",
            expires: new Date(0),
        });

        return response;
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Logout-all error:",
            error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown"
        );

        return NextResponse.json(
            { success: false, message: "Unable to log out of all sessions" },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\auth\me\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { User } from "@/lib/models/User";
import { Tenant } from "@/lib/models/Tenant";
import { Device } from "@/lib/models/Device";
import { getSession } from "@/lib/auth/getSession";
import { getGrantedPermissions } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";
import { Permission } from "@/lib/models/Permission";

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        // 1. Get authenticated session
        const session =
            await getSession(request);

        if (!session) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Not authenticated",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();

        // 2. Find active user
        const user = await User.findOne({
            _id: session.userId,
            tenantId: session.tenantId,
            status: "active",
        }).lean();

        if (!user) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "User not found or inactive",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        // 3. Find active tenant
        const tenant = await Tenant.findOne({
            _id: session.tenantId,
            status: "active",
        }).lean();

        if (!tenant) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Business account is inactive",
                },
                { status: 403 }
            );
        }

        // 4. Find active device
        const device = await Device.findOne({
            _id: session.deviceId,
            tenantId: session.tenantId,
            userId: session.userId,
            status: "active",
        }).lean();

        if (!device) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Device is no longer active",
                },
                { status: 403 }
            );
        }

        // 5. Update device activity
        await Device.updateOne(
            { _id: device._id },
            {
                $set: {
                    lastSeenAt: new Date(),
                },
            }
        );

        // 6. The user's CURRENT permission names (from the database role).
        //    For display and offline convenience checks in the browser only:
        //    every server route checks permissions itself. None while a
        //    temporary password must be changed.
        let permissions: string[] = [];

        if (user.mustChangePassword !== true && user.roleId) {
            const catalogue = await Permission.find({}).select("name").lean();
            const granted = await getGrantedPermissions({
                userId: user._id.toString(),
                tenantId: user.tenantId.toString(),
                roleId: user.roleId.toString(),
                permissions: catalogue.map((p) => p.name),
            });
            permissions = [...granted].sort();
        }

        // 7. Return authenticated context
        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            authenticated: true,
            // The server's clock. The browser bases the 72-hour offline
            // window on it and refuses to renew it when the device clock
            // differs by more than five minutes.
            serverTime: new Date().toISOString(),
            permissions,
            user: {
                id: user._id.toString(),
                name: user.name,
                email: user.email,
                tenantId:
                    user.tenantId.toString(),
                roleId:
                    user.roleId?.toString() ??
                    null,
                mustChangePassword:
                    user.mustChangePassword === true,
            },
            tenant: {
                id: tenant._id.toString(),
                name: tenant.name,
                businessType:
                    tenant.businessType ??
                    null,
            },
            device: {
                id: device._id.toString(),
                deviceId: device.deviceId,
                deviceName: device.deviceName,
                deviceType: device.deviceType,
            },
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Auth me error:",
            error
        );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message:
                    "An unexpected error occurred",
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\categories\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { Category } from "@/lib/models/Category";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
    if (!auth) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Authentication required" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    await connectDB();
    const tenantId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId);
    let categories = await Category.find({ tenantId }).sort({ name: 1 }).lean();

    // Seed default if empty
    if (categories.length === 0) {
      const defaultCat = await Category.create({
        tenantId,
        name: "General",
        description: "Default Category",
        status: "active",
      });
      categories = [defaultCat.toObject()];
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, categories });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("GET CATEGORIES ERROR:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to list categories" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
    if (!auth) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Authentication required" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const name = String(body.name || "").trim();
    if (!name) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Category name is required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await connectDB();
    const category = await Category.create({
      tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
      name,
      description: String(body.description || "").trim(),
      status: "active",
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, category }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("CREATE CATEGORY ERROR:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to create category" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\customers\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { Customer } from "@/lib/models/Customer";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();
        const searchParams = request.nextUrl.searchParams;
        const q = searchParams.get("q")?.trim();

        const filter: any = {
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
        };

        if (q) {
            filter.$or = [
                { name: { $regex: q, $options: "i" } },
                { phone: { $regex: q, $options: "i" } },
                { email: { $regex: q, $options: "i" } },
            ];
        }

        const customers = await Customer.find(filter)
            .sort({ name: 1 })
            .limit(200)
            .lean();

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            customers,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("GET CUSTOMERS ERROR:", error);
        return NextResponse.json(
            { success: false, message: "Failed to load customers" },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const body = await request.json();
        if (!body.name || typeof body.name !== "string" || !body.name.trim()) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Name is required" },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();
        const customer = await Customer.create({
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
            name: body.name.trim(),
            phone: body.phone?.trim() || undefined,
            email: body.email?.trim() || undefined,
            address: body.address?.trim() || undefined,
            status: body.status || "active",
        });

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,
                message: "Customer created successfully",
                customer,
            },
            { status: 201 }
        );
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error("CREATE CUSTOMER ERROR:", error);
        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message: error.code === 11000 ? "A customer with this phone number already exists." : "Failed to create customer",
            },
            { status: 400 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\customers\[id]\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { Customer } from "@/lib/models/Customer";
import { Sale } from "@/lib/models/Sale";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";

type RouteContext = {
    params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

export async function GET(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const { id } = await context.params;
        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Invalid customer ID" },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();
        const customer = await Customer.findOne({
            _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
        }).lean();

        if (!customer) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Customer not found" },
                { status: 404 }
            );
        }

        // Get customer bills/sales
        const bills = await Sale.find({
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
            customerId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),
        })
            .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
            .limit(50)
            .lean();

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            customer,
            bills,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("GET CUSTOMER ERROR:", error);
        return NextResponse.json(
            { success: false, message: "Failed to load customer" },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}

export async function PUT(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const { id } = await context.params;
        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Invalid customer ID" },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        const body = await request.json();
        await connectDB();

        const updated = await Customer.findOneAndUpdate(
            {
                _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),
                tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
            },
            {
                ...(body.name && { name: body.name.trim() }),
                ...(body.phone !== undefined && { phone: body.phone?.trim() || undefined }),
                ...(body.email !== undefined && { email: body.email?.trim() || undefined }),
                ...(body.address !== undefined && { address: body.address?.trim() || undefined }),
                ...(body.status && { status: body.status }),
            },
            { new: true }
        ).lean();

        if (!updated) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Customer not found" },
                { status: 404 }
            );
        }

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Customer updated successfully",
            customer: updated,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("UPDATE CUSTOMER ERROR:", error);
        return NextResponse.json(
            { success: false, message: "Failed to update customer" },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { Device } from "@/lib/models/Device";
import { User } from "@/lib/models/User";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";

const ALLOWED_DEVICE_TYPES = [
    "pos",
    "desktop",
    "laptop",
    "tablet",
    "mobile",
    "other",
] as const;

function errorResponse(
    message: string,
    status: number
) {
    return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message },
        { status }
    );
}

// GET /api/devices
export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return errorResponse("Not authenticated", 401);
        }

        const permitted = await hasPermission({
            userId: context.userId,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
            roleId: context.roleId,
            permission: "devices.read",
        });

        if (!permitted) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const devices = await Device.find({
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
        })
            .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
            .lean();

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            count: devices.length,
            devices,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Device list error:", error);
        return errorResponse("Failed to fetch devices", 500);
    }
}

// POST /api/devices
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    let session: mongoose.ClientSession | undefined;

    try {
        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return errorResponse("Not authenticated", 401);
        }

        const permitted = await hasPermission({
            userId: context.userId,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
            roleId: context.roleId,
            permission: "devices.create",
        });

        if (!permitted) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        let body: unknown;

        try {
            body = await request.json();
        } catch {
            return errorResponse("Invalid JSON body", 400);
        }

        if (
            !body ||
            typeof body !== "object" ||
            Array.isArray(body)
        ) {
            return errorResponse("Request body must be an object", 400);
        }

        const payload = body as Record<string, unknown>;
        const allowedFields = [
            "deviceName",
            "deviceType",
            "deviceId",
            "userId",
        ];

        if (
            Object.keys(payload).some(
                (key) => !allowedFields.includes(key)
            )
        ) {
            return errorResponse(
                "Request contains unsupported fields",
                400
            );
        }

        const { deviceName, deviceType, deviceId, userId } =
            payload;

        if (
            typeof deviceName !== "string" ||
            !deviceName.trim() ||
            deviceName.trim().length > 100
        ) {
            return errorResponse(
                "deviceName must contain 1–100 characters",
                400
            );
        }

        if (
            typeof deviceId !== "string" ||
            !deviceId.trim() ||
            deviceId.trim().length > 150
        ) {
            return errorResponse(
                "deviceId must contain 1–150 characters",
                400
            );
        }

        if (
            typeof deviceType !== "string" ||
            !ALLOWED_DEVICE_TYPES.includes(
                deviceType as (typeof ALLOWED_DEVICE_TYPES)[number]
            )
        ) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid deviceType", 400);
        }

        const tenantObjectId =
            new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(context.tenantId);

        const assignedUserId =
            userId === undefined
                ? context.userId
                : userId;

        if (
            typeof assignedUserId !== "string" ||
            !mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(assignedUserId)
        ) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid userId", 400);
        }

        // Critical tenant-isolation check:
        // the assigned user must be active and belong to this tenant.
        const assignedUser = await User.findOne({
            _id: assignedUserId,
            tenantId: tenantObjectId,
            status: "active",
        })
            .select("_id")
            .lean();

        if (!assignedUser) {
            return errorResponse(
                "Assigned user not found or inactive in this tenant",
                400
            );
        }

        session = await mongoose.startSession();

        let result:
            | {
                device: unknown;
                auditLog: unknown;
            }
            | undefined;

        await session.withTransaction(async () => {
            const device = new Device({
                tenantId: tenantObjectId,
                userId: assignedUser._id,
                deviceName: deviceName.trim(),
                deviceType,
                deviceId: deviceId.trim(),
                status: "active",
            });

            await device.save({ session });

            const auditLog =
                await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                    {
                        userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                            context.userId
                        ),
                        tenantId: tenantObjectId,
                    },
                    {
                        action: "CREATE",
                        entity: "Device",
                        entityId: device._id,
                        description:
                            `Device ${device.deviceName} created`,
                        newValues: {
                            deviceName: device.deviceName,
                            deviceType: device.deviceType,
                            deviceId: device.deviceId,
                            userId: device.userId.toString(),
                            status: device.status,
                        },
                    },
                    session
                );

            result = {
                device: device.toObject(),
                auditLog: auditLog.toObject(),
            };
        });

        if (!result) {
            throw new Error("Device creation transaction returned no result");
        }

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,
                message: "Device created successfully",
                ...result,
            },
            { status: 201 }
        );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
        console.error("Device creation error:", error);

        if (
            error instanceof mongoose.Error.ValidationError
        ) {
            return errorResponse(error.message, 400);
        }

        if (
            error instanceof mongoose.mongo.MongoServerError &&
            error.code === 11000
        ) {
            return errorResponse(
                "A device with this deviceId already exists in this tenant",
                409
            );
        }

        return errorResponse("Failed to create device", 500);
    } finally {
        await session?.endSession();
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\devices\[id]\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { Device } from "@/lib/models/Device";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";

const ALLOWED_DEVICE_TYPES = [
    "pos",
    "desktop",
    "laptop",
    "tablet",
    "mobile",
    "other",
] as const;

type RouteContext = {
    params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

function errorResponse(message: string, status: number) {
    return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message },
        { status }
    );
}

class DeviceNotFoundError extends Error { }

function isObjectBody(
    body: unknown
): body is Record<string, unknown> {
    return (
        typeof body === "object" &&
        body !== null &&
        !Array.isArray(body)
    );
}

// GET /api/devices/[id]
export async function GET(
    request: NextRequest,
    routeContext: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return errorResponse("Not authenticated", 401);
        }

        const permitted = await hasPermission({
            userId: context.userId,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
            roleId: context.roleId,
            permission: "devices.read",
        });

        if (!permitted) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const { id } = await routeContext.params;

        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid device ID", 400);
        }

        const device = await Device.findOne({
            _id: id,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
        }).lean();

        if (!device) {
            return errorResponse("Device not found", 404);
        }

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            device,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Device fetch error:", error);
        return errorResponse("Failed to fetch device", 500);
    }
}

// PUT /api/devices/[id]
export async function PUT(
    request: NextRequest,
    routeContext: RouteContext
) {
    let session: mongoose.ClientSession | undefined;

    try {
        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return errorResponse("Not authenticated", 401);
        }

        const permitted = await hasPermission({
            userId: context.userId,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
            roleId: context.roleId,
            permission: "devices.update",
        });

        if (!permitted) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const { id } = await routeContext.params;

        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid device ID", 400);
        }

        let body: unknown;

        try {
            body = await request.json();
        } catch {
            return errorResponse("Invalid JSON body", 400);
        }

        if (!isObjectBody(body)) {
            return errorResponse(
                "Request body must be an object",
                400
            );
        }

        const allowedFields = [
            "deviceName",
            "deviceType",
            "status",
        ];

        if (
            Object.keys(body).some(
                (key) => !allowedFields.includes(key)
            )
        ) {
            return errorResponse(
                "Request contains unsupported fields",
                400
            );
        }

        if (Object.keys(body).length === 0) {
            return errorResponse(
                "Provide at least one field to update",
                400
            );
        }

        const { deviceName, deviceType, status } = body;

        if (
            deviceName !== undefined &&
            (
                typeof deviceName !== "string" ||
                !deviceName.trim() ||
                deviceName.trim().length > 100
            )
        ) {
            return errorResponse(
                "deviceName must contain 1–100 characters",
                400
            );
        }

        if (
            deviceType !== undefined &&
            (
                typeof deviceType !== "string" ||
                !ALLOWED_DEVICE_TYPES.includes(
                    deviceType as (typeof ALLOWED_DEVICE_TYPES)[number]
                )
            )
        ) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid deviceType", 400);
        }

        if (
            status !== undefined &&
            status !== "active" &&
            status !== "inactive"
        ) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid device status", 400);
        }

        session = await mongoose.startSession();

        let result:
            | {
                device: unknown;
                auditLog: unknown;
            }
            | undefined;

        await session.withTransaction(async () => {
            // Always scope the lookup to the authenticated tenant.
            const device = await Device.findOne({
                _id: id,
                tenantId: context.tenantId,
            }).session(session!);

            if (!device) {
                throw new DeviceNotFoundError();
            }

            const oldValues = {
                deviceName: device.deviceName,
                deviceType: device.deviceType,
                status: device.status,
            };

            if (deviceName !== undefined) {
                device.deviceName = deviceName.trim();
            }

            if (deviceType !== undefined) {
                device.deviceType = deviceType as
                    (typeof ALLOWED_DEVICE_TYPES)[number];
            }

            if (status !== undefined) {
                device.status = status;
            }

            await device.save({ session });

            const newValues = {
                deviceName: device.deviceName,
                deviceType: device.deviceType,
                status: device.status,
            };

            let action = "UPDATE";

            if (
                oldValues.status === "active" &&
                newValues.status === "inactive"
            ) {
                action = "DEACTIVATE";
            } else if (
                oldValues.status === "inactive" &&
                newValues.status === "active"
            ) {
                action = "ACTIVATE";
            }

            const auditLog =
                await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                    {
                        userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                            context.userId
                        ),
                        tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                            context.tenantId
                        ),
                    },
                    {
                        action,
                        entity: "Device",
                        entityId: device._id,
                        description:
                            action === "DEACTIVATE"
                                ? `Device ${device.deviceName} deactivated`
                                : action === "ACTIVATE"
                                    ? `Device ${device.deviceName} activated`
                                    : `Device ${device.deviceName} updated`,
                        oldValues,
                        newValues,
                    },
                    session
                );

            result = {
                device: device.toObject(),
                auditLog: auditLog.toObject(),
            };
        });

        if (!result) {
            throw new Error("Device update transaction returned no result");
        }

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Device updated successfully",
            ...result,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof DeviceNotFoundError) {
            return errorResponse("Device not found", 404);
        }

        console.error("Device update error:", error);

        if (error instanceof mongoose.Error.ValidationError) {
            return errorResponse(error.message, 400);
        }

        return errorResponse("Failed to update device", 500);
    } finally {
        await session?.endSession();
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\ledger-entries\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { LedgerService } from "@/lib/services/LedgerService";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import {
    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS,
    requireBusinessPermission,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";
import { publicErrorMessage } from "@/lib/api/publicError";

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const authContext =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!authContext) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Not authenticated or invalid authentication context",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();

        // Manual ledger entries: permission from the user's current role.
        const denied = await requireBusinessPermission(
            authContext,
            BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.ledgerCreate
        );

        if (denied) {
            return denied;
        }

        const body = await request.json();

        if (
            !body.entries ||
            !Array.isArray(body.entries) ||
            body.entries.length === 0
        ) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "entries array is required",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        const user = {
            userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                authContext.userId
            ),
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                authContext.tenantId
            ),
        };

        const ledgerEntries =
            await LedgerService.createEntries(
                user,
                body.entries
            );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,
                message:
                    "Ledger entries created successfully",
                ledgerEntries,
            },
            { status: 201 }
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "LEDGER API ERROR:",
            error
        );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message:
                    publicErrorMessage(error, "Failed to create ledger entries"),
            },
            { status: 400 }
        );
    }
}
