import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { PaymentService } from "@/lib/services/PaymentService";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import {
    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS,
    requireBusinessPermission,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";
import { publicErrorMessage } from "@/lib/api/publicError";

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const context = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Not authenticated or invalid authentication context",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const denied = await requireBusinessPermission(
            context,
            BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.paymentsCreate
        );

        if (denied) {
            return denied;
        }

        const user = {
            userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                context.userId
            ),

            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                context.tenantId
            ),
        };

        const body = await request.json();

        const result =
            await PaymentService.createPayment(
                user,
                body,
                body.operationId
            );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,

                message:
                    result?.alreadyProcessed
                        ? "Payment was already processed"
                        : "Payment processed successfully",

                result,
            },
            {
                status: 200,
            }
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "PAYMENT API ERROR:",
            error
        );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,

                message:
                    publicErrorMessage(error, "Failed to process payment"),
            },
            {
                status: 400,
            }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\products\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { z } from "zod";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { Product } from "@/lib/models/Product";
import { Category } from "@/lib/models/Category";
import { Unit } from "@/lib/models/Unit";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";

const createProductSchema = z
    .object({
        name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200),
        SKU: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100),
        barcode: z.string().trim().max(100).optional(),
        categoryId: z.string().refine(
            (value) => mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(value),
            "Invalid category ID"
        ),
        unitId: z.string().refine(
            (value) => mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(value),
            "Invalid unit ID"
        ),
        purchasePrice: z.number().finite().min(0),
        sellingPrice: z.number().finite().min(0),
        status: z.enum(["active", "inactive"]).optional(),
    })
    .strict();

// GET: List products belonging to the authenticated tenant.
export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Authentication required",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const canReadProducts = await hasPermission({
            userId: auth.userId,
            tenantId: auth.tenantId,
            roleId: auth.roleId,
            permission: "products.read",
        });

        if (!canReadProducts) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "You do not have permission to read products",
                },
                { status: 403 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();

        const products = await Product.find({
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
        })
            .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
            .lean();

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            count: products.length,
            products,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("List products error:", error);

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message: "Failed to list products",
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}

// POST: Create a product and its audit log atomically.
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Authentication required",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            userId: auth.userId,
            tenantId: auth.tenantId,
            roleId: auth.roleId,
            permission: "products.create",
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "You do not have permission to create products",
                },
                { status: 403 }
            );
        }

        let body: unknown;

        try {
            body = await request.json();
        } catch {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Invalid JSON request body",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        const parsed = createProductSchema.safeParse(body);

        if (!parsed.success) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Invalid product data",
                    errors: parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
                        field: issue.path.join("."),
                        message: issue.message,
                    })),
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        const data = parsed.data;
        const tenantId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId);

        // Confirm the category and unit belong to this tenant.
        await connectDB();

        const [category, unit] = await Promise.all([
            Category.findOne({
                _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(data.categoryId),
                tenantId,
                status: "active",
            })
                .select("_id")
                .lean(),

            Unit.findOne({
                _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(data.unitId),
                tenantId,
                status: "active",
            })
                .select("_id")
                .lean(),
        ]);

        if (!category) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Active category not found for this business",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        if (!unit) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Active unit not found for this business",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // Normalize the SKU and check for existing duplicates.
        const sku = data.SKU.toUpperCase();

        const duplicateConditions: Record<string, unknown>[] = [
            { tenantId, SKU: sku },
        ];

        if (data.barcode) {
            duplicateConditions.push({
                tenantId,
                barcode: data.barcode,
            });
        }

        const duplicate = await Product.findOne({
            $or: duplicateConditions,
        })
            .select("_id SKU barcode")
            .lean();

        if (duplicate) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "A product with this SKU or barcode already exists",
                },
                { status: 409 }
            );
        }

        // Product creation and audit logging share one transaction.
        const session = await mongoose.startSession();

        try {
            const createdProduct = await session.withTransaction(async () => {
                const [product] = await Product.create(
                    [
                        {
                            tenantId,
                            name: data.name,
                            SKU: sku,
                            ...(data.barcode
                                ? { barcode: data.barcode }
                                : {}),
                            categoryId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                                data.categoryId
                            ),
                            unitId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(data.unitId),
                            purchasePrice: data.purchasePrice,
                            sellingPrice: data.sellingPrice,
                            stockQuantity: 0,
                            status: data.status ?? "active",
                        },
                    ],
                    { session }
                );

                await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                    {
                        userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.userId),
                        tenantId,
                    },
                    {
                        action: "CREATE",
                        entity: "Product",
                        entityId: product._id,
                        description: `Product ${product.name} created`,
                        newValues: {
                            name: product.name,
                            SKU: product.SKU,
                            barcode: product.barcode,
                            categoryId: product.categoryId,
                            unitId: product.unitId,
                            purchasePrice: product.purchasePrice,
                            sellingPrice: product.sellingPrice,
                            stockQuantity: product.stockQuantity,
                            status: product.status,
                        },
                    },
                    session
                );

                return product.toObject();
            });

            if (!createdProduct) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Product creation transaction did not complete"
                );
            }

            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: true,
                    message: "Product created successfully",
                    product: createdProduct,
                },
                { status: 201 }
            );
        } finally {
            await session.endSession();
        }
    } catch (error) {
        // MongoDB unique indexes also protect against concurrent requests.
        if (
            typeof error === "object" &&
            error !== null &&
            "code" in error &&
            error.code === 11000
        ) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "A product with this SKU or barcode already exists",
                },
                { status: 409 }
            );
        }

        console.error("Create product error:", error);

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message: "Failed to create product",
                error:
                    process.env.NODE_ENV === "development"
                        ? error instanceof Error
                            ? error.message
                            : String(error)
                        : undefined,
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\products\[id]\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { z } from "zod";

import { Product } from "@/lib/models/Product";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";

type RouteContext = {
    params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

const updateProductSchema = z.object({
    name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200).optional(),
    SKU: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100).optional(),
    barcode: z.string().trim().max(100).optional(),
    purchasePrice: z.number().finite().min(0).optional(),
    sellingPrice: z.number().finite().min(0).optional(),
    status: z.enum(["active", "inactive"]).optional(),
}).strict().refine(
    (data) => Object.keys(data).length > 0,
    "Provide at least one field to update"
);

// GET PRODUCT
export async function GET(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        // 1. Verify authentication and obtain the tenant context.
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Authentication required",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        // 2. Check product-read permission.
        const canReadProducts = await hasPermission({
            userId: auth.userId,
            tenantId: auth.tenantId,
            roleId: auth.roleId,
            permission: "products.read",
        });

        if (!canReadProducts) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "You do not have permission to read products",
                },
                { status: 403 }
            );
        }

        // 3. Validate the product ID.
        const { id } = await context.params;

        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Invalid product ID",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // 4. Find the product within the authenticated tenant only.
        const product = await Product.findOne({
            _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
        }).lean();

        if (!product) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Product not found",
                },
                { status: 404 }
            );
        }

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            product,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Get product error:", error);

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message: "Failed to get product",
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}

// UPDATE PRODUCT
export async function PUT(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        // 1. Verify authentication.
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Authentication required",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        // 2. Check product-update permission.
        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            userId: auth.userId,
            tenantId: auth.tenantId,
            roleId: auth.roleId,
            permission: "products.update",
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "You do not have permission to update products",
                },
                { status: 403 }
            );
        }

        // 3. Validate the product ID.
        const { id } = await context.params;

        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Invalid product ID",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // 4. Parse the request body safely.
        let body: unknown;

        try {
            body = await request.json();
        } catch {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Invalid JSON request body",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // 5. Validate allowed fields.
        const parsed = updateProductSchema.safeParse(body);

        if (!parsed.success) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Invalid product data",
                    errors: parsed.error.issues.map((issue) => ({
                        field: issue.path.join("."),
                        message: issue.message,
                    })),
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        const data = parsed.data;
        const tenantId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId);

        // 6. Find the product within this tenant only.
        const product = await Product.findOne({
            _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),
            tenantId,
        });

        if (!product) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Product not found",
                },
                { status: 404 }
            );
        }

        const oldValues = {
            name: product.name,
            SKU: product.SKU,
            barcode: product.barcode,
            purchasePrice: product.purchasePrice,
            sellingPrice: product.sellingPrice,
            stockQuantity: product.stockQuantity,
            status: product.status,
        };

        const newSKU = data.SKU?.toUpperCase();

        // 7. Check SKU and barcode conflicts within this tenant.
        const duplicateConditions: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];

        if (newSKU !== undefined && newSKU !== product.SKU) {
            duplicateConditions.push({ SKU: newSKU });
        }

        if (
            data.barcode !== undefined &&
            data.barcode !== product.barcode &&
            data.barcode !== ""
        ) {
            duplicateConditions.push({ barcode: data.barcode });
        }

        if (duplicateConditions.length > 0) {
            const duplicate = await Product.findOne({
                tenantId,
                _id: { $ne: product._id },
                $or: duplicateConditions,
            }).select("_id").lean();

            if (duplicate) {
                return NextResponse.json(
                    {
                        success: false,
                        message: "Another product already uses this SKU or barcode",
                    },
                    { status: 409 }
                );
            }
        }

        // 8. Apply only the validated changes.
        if (data.name !== undefined) {
            product.name = data.name;
        }

        if (newSKU !== undefined) {
            product.SKU = newSKU;
        }

        if (data.barcode !== undefined) {
            if (data.barcode === "") {
                product.barcode = undefined;
            } else {
                product.barcode = data.barcode;
            }
        }

        if (data.purchasePrice !== undefined) {
            product.purchasePrice = data.purchasePrice;
        }

        if (data.sellingPrice !== undefined) {
            product.sellingPrice = data.sellingPrice;
        }

        if (data.status !== undefined) {
            product.status = data.status;
        }

        await product.save();

        const newValues = {
            name: product.name,
            SKU: product.SKU,
            barcode: product.barcode,
            purchasePrice: product.purchasePrice,
            sellingPrice: product.sellingPrice,
            stockQuantity: product.stockQuantity,
            status: product.status,
        };

        // 9. Determine the audit action.
        let action = "UPDATE";

        if (
            oldValues.status === "active" &&
            newValues.status === "inactive"
        ) {
            action = "DEACTIVATE";
        } else if (
            oldValues.status === "inactive" &&
            newValues.status === "active"
        ) {
            action = "ACTIVATE";
        }

        // 10. Record the change.
        const auditLog = await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
            {
                userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.userId),
                tenantId,
            },
            {
                action,
                entity: "Product",
                entityId: product._id,
                description: `Product ${product.name} ${action.toLowerCase()}`,
                oldValues,
                newValues,
            }
        );

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Product updated successfully",
            product,
            auditLog,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (
            typeof error === "object" &&
            error !== null &&
            "code" in error &&
            error.code === 11000
        ) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Another product already uses this SKU or barcode",
                },
                { status: 409 }
            );
        }

        console.error("Update product error:", error);

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message: "Failed to update product",
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}

// DEACTIVATE PRODUCT (SOFT DELETE)
export async function DELETE(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        // 1. Verify authentication.
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Authentication required",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        // 2. Check the dedicated deletion permission.
        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            userId: auth.userId,
            tenantId: auth.tenantId,
            roleId: auth.roleId,
            permission: "products.delete",
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "You do not have permission to deactivate products",
                },
                { status: 403 }
            );
        }

        // 3. Validate the product ID.
        const { id } = await context.params;

        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Invalid product ID",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        const tenantId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId);

        // 4. Find the product within this tenant only.
        const product = await Product.findOne({
            _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),
            tenantId,
        });

        if (!product) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Product not found",
                },
                { status: 404 }
            );
        }

        // 5. Avoid duplicate audit entries for an inactive product.
        if (product.status === "inactive") {
            return NextResponse.json({
                success: true,
                message: "Product is already inactive",
                product,
            });
        }

        const oldValues = {
            name: product.name,
            SKU: product.SKU,
            status: product.status,
        };

        // 6. Soft delete: preserve the database record.
        product.status = "inactive";
        await product.save();

        // 7. Record the change in the audit log.
        const auditLog = await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
            {
                userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.userId),
                tenantId,
            },
            {
                action: "DEACTIVATE",
                entity: "Product",
                entityId: product._id,
                description: `Product ${product.name} deactivated`,
                oldValues,
                newValues: {
                    name: product.name,
                    SKU: product.SKU,
                    status: product.status,
                },
            }
        );

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Product deactivated successfully",
            product,
            auditLog,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Deactivate product error:", error);

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message: "Failed to deactivate product",
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\purchase-returns\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { PurchaseReturnService } from "@/lib/services/PurchaseReturnService";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import {
    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS,
    requireBusinessPermission,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";
import { publicErrorMessage } from "@/lib/api/publicError";

export async function POST(
    request: NextRequest
) {
    try {
        // 1. Get authenticated user/tenant/device context
        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Not authenticated or invalid authentication context",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        // 1b. Permission (the user's current role, checked on the server)
        const denied = await requireBusinessPermission(
            context,
            BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.purchaseReturnsCreate
        );

        if (denied) {
            return denied;
        }

        // 2. Convert trusted session IDs to MongoDB ObjectIds
        const user = {
            userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                context.userId
            ),
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                context.tenantId
            ),
        };

        // 3. Read request body
        const body = await request.json();

        // 4. Create purchase return using authenticated tenant
        const purchaseReturn =
            await PurchaseReturnService.createPurchaseReturn(
                user,
                body
            );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,
                message:
                    "Purchase return created successfully",
                purchaseReturn,
            },
            { status: 201 }
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "PURCHASE RETURN API ERROR:",
            error
        );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message:
                    publicErrorMessage(error, "Failed to create purchase return"),
            },
            { status: 400 }
        );
    }
}

========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\sales\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { SalesService } from "@/lib/services/SalesService";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import {
    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS,
    BusinessPermissionError,
    SALE_DATE_RULES,
    getGrantedBusinessPermissions,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";
import { publicErrorMessage } from "@/lib/api/publicError";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { Sale } from "@/lib/models/Sale";

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const context = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
        if (!context) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();
        const sales = await Sale.find({
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(context.tenantId),
        })
            .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
            .limit(100)
            .lean();

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            sales,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("GET SALES ERROR:", error);
        return NextResponse.json(
            { success: false, message: "Failed to load sales" },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}

export async function POST(
    request: NextRequest
) {
    try {
        // 1. Get authenticated tenant/device/user context
        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Not authenticated or invalid authentication context",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        // 1b. Permissions (the user's current role, checked on the server)
        const granted = await getGrantedBusinessPermissions(context, [
            BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesCreate,
            BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesPriceOverride,
            BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesBackdate,
        ]);

        if (!granted.has(BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesCreate)) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Permission denied" },
                { status: 403 }
            );
        }

        // 2. Convert trusted session IDs to MongoDB ObjectIds
        const user = {
            userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                context.userId
            ),
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                context.tenantId
            ),
            deviceId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                context.deviceId
            ),
        };

        // 3. Read request body
        const body = await request.json();

        // 4. Create sale using authenticated tenant. An online sale never
        //    carries an offline operation ID.
        const sale =
            await SalesService.createSale(
                user,
                body,
                undefined,
                {
                    allowPriceOverride: granted.has(BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesPriceOverride),
                    allowBackdate: granted.has(BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesBackdate),
                    referenceTime: new Date(),
                    maxAgeMs: SALE_DATE_RULES.onlineMaxAgeMs,
                }
            );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,
                message:
                    "Sale created successfully",
                sale,
            },
            { status: 201 }
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "SALE API ERROR:",
            error
        );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message:
                    publicErrorMessage(error, "Failed to create sale"),
            },
            { status: error instanceof BusinessPermissionError ? 403 : 400 }
        );
    }
}

========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\stock-adjustments\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { StockService } from "@/lib/services/StockService";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import {
    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS,
    requireBusinessPermission,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";
import { publicErrorMessage } from "@/lib/api/publicError";

export async function POST(
    request: NextRequest
) {
    try {
        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 1. Get authenticated user/tenant context
        // --------------------------------------------------

        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Not authenticated or invalid authentication context",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        // Permission (the user's current role, checked on the server)
        const denied = await requireBusinessPermission(
            context,
            BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.stockAdjust
        );

        if (denied) {
            return denied;
        }

        const body =
            await request.json();

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 2. Validate operationId
        // --------------------------------------------------

        if (
            !body.operationId ||
            typeof body.operationId !== "string" ||
            !body.operationId.trim()
        ) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "operationId is required",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        const operationId =
            body.operationId.trim();

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 3. Validate product ID
        // --------------------------------------------------

        if (
            !body.productId ||
            !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                body.productId
            )
        ) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Valid productId is required",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 4. Validate quantity
        // --------------------------------------------------

        if (
            typeof body.quantity !== "number" ||
            !Number.isFinite(body.quantity) ||
            body.quantity === 0
        ) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Quantity must be a valid non-zero number",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 5. Validate warehouse
        // --------------------------------------------------

        if (
            !body.warehouse?.name?.trim()
        ) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Warehouse name is required",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 6. Validate reason
        // --------------------------------------------------

        if (
            !body.reason?.trim()
        ) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Reason is required for stock adjustment",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 7. Use authenticated user context
        // --------------------------------------------------

        const user = {
            userId:
                new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                    context.userId
                ),

            tenantId:
                new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                    context.tenantId
                ),
        };

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 8. Process stock adjustment
        // --------------------------------------------------

        const result =
            await StockService.changeStock(
                user,
                {
                    productId:
                        body.productId,

                    quantity:
                        body.quantity,

                    type:
                        "adjustment",

                    warehouse: {
                        name:
                            body.warehouse.name,

                        location:
                            body.warehouse.location,
                    },

                    reason:
                        body.reason,
                },

                undefined,

                operationId
            );

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // 9. Response
        // --------------------------------------------------

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,

                message:
                    result.alreadyProcessed
                        ? "Stock adjustment was already processed"
                        : "Stock adjustment completed successfully",

                result,
            },
            { status: 200 }
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Stock adjustment error:",
            error
        );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,

                message:
                    publicErrorMessage(error, "Stock adjustment failed"),
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\suppliers\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { Supplier } from "@/lib/models/Supplier";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();
        const searchParams = request.nextUrl.searchParams;
        const q = searchParams.get("q")?.trim();

        const filter: any = {
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
        };

        if (q) {
            filter.$or = [
                { name: { $regex: q, $options: "i" } },
                { "contact.phone": { $regex: q, $options: "i" } },
                { "tax.gstin": { $regex: q, $options: "i" } },
                { supplierCode: { $regex: q, $options: "i" } },
            ];
        }

        const suppliers = await Supplier.find(filter)
            .sort({ name: 1 })
            .limit(200)
            .lean();

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            suppliers,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("GET SUPPLIERS ERROR:", error);
        return NextResponse.json(
            { success: false, message: "Failed to load suppliers" },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const body = await request.json();
        if (!body.name || typeof body.name !== "string" || !body.name.trim()) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Supplier name is required" },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();

        // Generate supplierCode if not provided
        const count = await Supplier.countDocuments({
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
        });
        const supplierCode = body.supplierCode?.trim() || `SUP-${String(count + 1).padStart(4, "0")}`;

        const supplier = await Supplier.create({
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
            supplierCode,
            name: body.name.trim(),
            contact: {
                contactPerson: body.contactPerson?.trim() || undefined,
                phone: body.phone?.trim() || undefined,
                email: body.email?.trim() || undefined,
            },
            address: {
                line1: body.address?.trim() || undefined,
                state: body.state?.trim() || undefined,
            },
            tax: {
                gstin: body.gstin?.trim() || undefined,
                taxRegistrationType: body.gstin ? "registered" : "unregistered",
            },
            paymentTerms: {
                type: body.paymentType || "cash",
                creditDays: Number(body.creditDays) || 0,
                dueDateCalculation: "invoiceDate",
            },
            creditLimit: Number(body.creditLimit) || 0,
            currency: "INR",
        });

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,
                message: "Supplier created successfully",
                supplier,
            },
            { status: 201 }
        );
    } catch (error: any) {
        console.error("CREATE SUPPLIER ERROR:", error);
        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message: error.code === 11000 ? "A supplier with this code already exists." : "Failed to create supplier",
            },
            { status: 400 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\suppliers\[id]\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { Supplier } from "@/lib/models/Supplier";
import { Purchase } from "@/lib/models/Purchase";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";

type RouteContext = {
    params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

export async function GET(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const { id } = await context.params;
        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Invalid supplier ID" },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();
        const supplier = await Supplier.findOne({
            _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
        }).lean();

        if (!supplier) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Supplier not found" },
                { status: 404 }
            );
        }

        const purchases = await Purchase.find({
            tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
            supplierId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),
        })
            .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
            .limit(50)
            .lean();

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            supplier,
            purchases,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("GET SUPPLIER ERROR:", error);
        return NextResponse.json(
            { success: false, message: "Failed to load supplier" },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}

export async function PUT(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
        if (!auth) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const { id } = await context.params;
        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Invalid supplier ID" },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        const body = await request.json();
        await connectDB();

        const updateData: any = {};
        if (body.name) updateData.name = body.name.trim();
        if (body.phone !== undefined) updateData["contact.phone"] = body.phone?.trim() || undefined;
        if (body.email !== undefined) updateData["contact.email"] = body.email?.trim() || undefined;
        if (body.contactPerson !== undefined) updateData["contact.contactPerson"] = body.contactPerson?.trim() || undefined;
        if (body.gstin !== undefined) {
            updateData["tax.gstin"] = body.gstin?.trim() || undefined;
            updateData["tax.taxRegistrationType"] = body.gstin ? "registered" : "unregistered";
        }
        if (body.address !== undefined) updateData["address.line1"] = body.address?.trim() || undefined;
        if (body.state !== undefined) updateData["address.state"] = body.state?.trim() || undefined;

        const updated = await Supplier.findOneAndUpdate(
            {
                _id: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(id),
                tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
            },
            { $set: updateData },
            { new: true }
        ).lean();

        if (!updated) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, message: "Supplier not found" },
                { status: 404 }
            );
        }

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Supplier updated successfully",
            supplier: updated,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("UPDATE SUPPLIER ERROR:", error);
        return NextResponse.json(
            { success: false, message: "Failed to update supplier" },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\sync-operations\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { z } from "zod";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { SyncOperation } from "@/lib/models/SyncOperation";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { SyncProcessError } from "@/lib/services/SyncErrors";
import {
    getGrantedBusinessPermissions,
    getSyncEntityPermission,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";

const objectIdSchema = z.string().regex(/^[a-f\d]{24}$/i, "Invalid ID");

// Payloads are validated in detail when processed; here only shape and size.
const MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES = 100_000;

const uploadSchema = z.strictObject({
    // Generated on the device with crypto.randomUUID().
    operationId: z.uuid("operationId must be a UUID"),
    // Processing always creates records, so only CREATE is accepted.
    operationType: z.literal("CREATE"),
    // Only entity types the server can actually process.
    entityType: z.enum(["Sale", "Purchase", "SalesReturn", "PurchaseReturn", "Payment", "StockAdjustment"]),
    entityId: objectIdSchema.optional(),
    payload: z.record(z.string(), z.unknown()),
    // The account that queued the operation on the device. It is only
    // compared with the session: identity is always taken from the session.
    owner: z.strictObject({
        tenantId: objectIdSchema,
        userId: objectIdSchema,
        deviceId: objectIdSchema,
    }),
});

function errorJson(error: SyncProcessError) {
    return NextResponse.json(error.toJSON(), { status: error.httpStatus });
}

// POST /api/sync-operations — upload one queued operation.
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const authContext = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!authContext) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, code: "UNAUTHENTICATED", retryable: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        let body: unknown;
        try {
            body = await request.json();
        } catch {
            return errorJson(new SyncProcessError(400, "INVALID_REQUEST", "Request body must be valid JSON", false));
        }

        const parsed = uploadSchema.safeParse(body);

        if (!parsed.success) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
                    retryable: false,
                    message: "Invalid sync operation",
                    errors: z.flattenError(parsed.error),
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        const input = parsed.data;

        if (JSON.stringify(input.payload).length > MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES) {
            return errorJson(new SyncProcessError(400, "INVALID_REQUEST", "Payload is too large", false));
        }

        // Never upload one account's queued work under another session.
        if (
            input.owner.tenantId.toLowerCase() !== authContext.tenantId ||
            input.owner.userId.toLowerCase() !== authContext.userId ||
            input.owner.deviceId.toLowerCase() !== authContext.deviceId
        ) {
            return errorJson(
                new SyncProcessError(
                    403,
                    "OWNER_MISMATCH",
                    "This operation was queued by another account or device and was not uploaded",
                    false
                )
            );
        }

        await connectDB();

        // Operation types without an explicit permission mapping are never
        // accepted (deny by default), whatever the user's role.
        const requiredPermission = getSyncEntityPermission(input.entityType);

        if (!requiredPermission) {
            return errorJson(
                new SyncProcessError(
                    403,
                    "PERMISSION_DENIED",
                    "This type of operation cannot be synchronized",
                    false
                )
            );
        }

        // The user must be allowed to perform this kind of operation. It is
        // checked again (with the role at that time) when it is processed.
        const granted = await getGrantedBusinessPermissions(authContext, [requiredPermission]);

        if (!granted.has(requiredPermission)) {
            return errorJson(
                new SyncProcessError(
                    403,
                    "PERMISSION_DENIED",
                    "You do not have permission to record this operation",
                    false
                )
            );
        }

        // Identity always comes from the authenticated session.
        const tenantId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(authContext.tenantId);
        const userId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(authContext.userId);
        const deviceId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(authContext.deviceId);

        try {
            const syncOperation = await SyncOperation.create({
                tenantId,
                userId,
                deviceId,
                operationId: input.operationId,
                operationType: input.operationType,
                entityType: input.entityType,
                entityId: input.entityId ? new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(input.entityId) : undefined,
                payload: input.payload,
                status: "PENDING",
                attempts: 0,
            });

            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: true,
                    alreadyExists: false,
                    operationId: syncOperation.operationId,
                    status: syncOperation.status,
                },
                { status: 201 }
            );
        } catch (error) {
            if (!(error instanceof mongoose.mongo.MongoServerError && error.code === 11000)) {
                throw error;
            }

            // Re-upload after a lost response is normal: accept it only if
            // the existing operation belongs to this same user and device.
            const existing = await SyncOperation.findOne({ tenantId, operationId: input.operationId })
                .select("deviceId userId status")
                .lean();

            if (
                existing &&
                existing.deviceId.equals(deviceId) &&
                (!existing.userId || existing.userId.equals(userId))
            ) {
                return NextResponse.json({
                    success: true,
                    alreadyExists: true,
                    operationId: input.operationId,
                    status: existing.status,
                });
            }

            return errorJson(
                new SyncProcessError(
                    409,
                    "OPERATION_ID_CONFLICT",
                    "This operation ID is already used by another device or user",
                    false
                )
            );
        }
    } catch (error: unknown) {
        console.error(
            "Create sync operation error:",
            error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown"
        );

        return errorJson(
            new SyncProcessError(503, "RETRYABLE_FAILURE", "Temporary problem while uploading; retry later", true)
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\sync-operations\process\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { z } from "zod";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { SyncService } from "@/lib/services/SyncService";
import { SyncProcessError, classifySyncError } from "@/lib/services/SyncErrors";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";

const processSchema = z.strictObject({
    // Legacy operations may have non-UUID IDs, so any bounded string.
    operationId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(200),
});

function errorJson(error: SyncProcessError) {
    return NextResponse.json(error.toJSON(), {
        status: error.httpStatus,
        headers: error.retryAfterSeconds !== undefined
            ? { "Retry-After": String(error.retryAfterSeconds) }
            : undefined,
    });
}

/*
 * POST /api/sync-operations/process
 *
 * 200 synced (or already synced)
 * 400 invalid request            (retryable: false)
 * 401 not signed in
 * 403 OWNER_MISMATCH             (belongs to another user/device)
 * 403 PERMISSION_DENIED          (role does not allow it now; NEEDS_ATTENTION)
 * 404 NOT_FOUND                  (upload it first)
 * 409 IN_PROGRESS                (retryable, see Retry-After)
 * 422 PERMANENT_FAILURE / NEEDS_ATTENTION (never retried automatically)
 * 503 RETRYABLE_FAILURE          (temporary; retry with backoff)
 */
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const authContext = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!authContext) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                { success: false, code: "UNAUTHENTICATED", retryable: false, message: "Authentication required" },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        let body: unknown;
        try {
            body = await request.json();
        } catch {
            return errorJson(new SyncProcessError(400, "INVALID_REQUEST", "Request body must be valid JSON", false));
        }

        const parsed = processSchema.safeParse(body);

        if (!parsed.success) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    code: "INVALID_REQUEST",
                    retryable: false,
                    message: "Invalid request",
                    errors: z.flattenError(parsed.error),
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        await connectDB();

        // Identity always comes from the authenticated session.
        const result = await SyncService.processOperation(
            {
                tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(authContext.tenantId),
                userId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(authContext.userId),
                deviceId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(authContext.deviceId),
                roleId: authContext.roleId,
            },
            parsed.data.operationId
        );

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            alreadySynced: result.alreadySynced,
            operationId: result.operation.operationId,
            status: result.operation.status,
        });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
        if (error instanceof SyncProcessError) {
            return errorJson(error);
        }

        console.error(
            "Process sync operation error:",
            error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown"
        );

        const retryable = classifySyncError(error) === "RETRYABLE";
        return errorJson(
            retryable
                ? new SyncProcessError(503, "RETRYABLE_FAILURE", "Temporary problem while syncing; retry later", true)
                : new SyncProcessError(500, "RETRYABLE_FAILURE", "Unexpected error while syncing", true)
        );
    }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";

import { Tax } from "@/lib/models/Tax";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import {
    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS,
    requireBusinessPermission,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";

export async function GET(
    request: NextRequest
) {
    try {
        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Not authenticated or invalid authentication context",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const denied = await requireBusinessPermission(
            context,
            BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.taxesRead
        );

        if (denied) {
            return denied;
        }

        // Get taxes belonging only to the authenticated tenant
        const taxes = await Tax.find({
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
        })
            .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
            .lean();

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,
                message: "Taxes fetched successfully",
                taxes,
            },
            { status: 200 }
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Get taxes error:",
            error
        );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message: "Failed to fetch taxes",
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\taxes\[id]\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { Tax } from "@/lib/models/Tax";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import {
    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS,
    requireBusinessPermission,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";

export async function DELETE(
    request: NextRequest,
    { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
    try {
        const context =
            await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message:
                        "Not authenticated or invalid authentication context",
                },
                { status: 401 }
            );
        }

        const denied = await requireBusinessPermission(
            context,
            BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.taxesDelete
        );

        if (denied) {
            return denied;
        }

        const { id } = await params;

        // Validate tax ID
        if (!mongoose.Types.ObjectId.isValid(id)) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Invalid tax ID",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // Find tax belonging only to the authenticated tenant
        const tax = await Tax.findOne({
            _id: id,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
        });

        if (!tax) {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Tax not found",
                },
                { status: 404 }
            );
        }

        // Already inactive
        if (tax.status === "inactive") {
            return NextResponse.json(
                {
                    success: false,
                    message: "Tax is already inactive",
                },
                { status: 400 }
            );
        }

        // Soft delete
        tax.status = "inactive";

        await tax.save();

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,
                message: "Tax deactivated successfully",
                tax,
            },
            { status: 200 }
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.error(
            "Delete tax error:",
            error
        );

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: false,
                message: "Failed to deactivate tax",
            },
            { status: 500 }
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\units\route.ts ==========
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { Unit } from "@/lib/models/Unit";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
    if (!auth) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Authentication required" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    await connectDB();
    const tenantId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId);
    let units = await Unit.find({ tenantId }).sort({ name: 1 }).lean();

    // Seed default if empty
    if (units.length === 0) {
      const defaultUnit = await Unit.create({
        tenantId,
        name: "Pieces",
        shortName: "pcs",
        status: "active",
      });
      units = [defaultUnit.toObject()];
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, units });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("GET UNITS ERROR:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to list units" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);
    if (!auth) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Authentication required" }, { status: 401 });
    }

    const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({}));
    const name = String(body.name || "").trim();
    const shortName = String(body.shortName || body.code || "pcs").trim();
    if (!name) {
      return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Unit name is required" }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await connectDB();
    const unit = await Unit.create({
      tenantId: new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId),
      name,
      shortName,
      status: "active",
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, unit }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("CREATE UNIT ERROR:", error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: "Failed to create unit" }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\users\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose, { Types } from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { User } from "@/lib/models/User";
import { Role } from "@/lib/models/Role";
import {
    getAuthenticatedContext,
    type AuthenticatedContext,
} from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";
import {
    canAssignRole,
    canManageUserWithRole,
    ensureTenantAdminGuard,
    isPrivilegedRole,
    loadUserManagementPolicy,
    lockTenantAdminGuard,
} from "@/lib/auth/userManagementPolicy";
import { hashPassword } from "@/app/api/auth/password";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";
import {
    UserManagementError,
    auditDeniedAttempt,
    createUserSchema,
    errorResponse,
    escapeRegex,
    isDuplicateKeyError,
    listUsersQuerySchema,
    publicUser,
    readJsonBody,
    validationErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/users/userApi";

const DUPLICATE_EMAIL_MESSAGE =
    "A user with this email already exists in this business";

// GET /api/users?page=1&limit=20&search=jo&status=active
export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const context = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        await connectDB();

        const permissionInput = {
            userId: context.userId,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
            roleId: context.roleId,
        };

        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            ...permissionInput,
            permission: "users.read",
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const parsedQuery = listUsersQuerySchema.safeParse(
            Object.fromEntries(request.nextUrl.searchParams)
        );

        if (!parsedQuery.success) {
            return validationErrorResponse(
                "Invalid query parameters",
                parsedQuery.error
            );
        }

        const { page, limit, search, status } = parsedQuery.data;
        const tenantId = new Types.ObjectId(context.tenantId);

        // tenantId always comes from the verified session.
        const filter: Record<string, unknown> = { tenantId };

        if (status) {
            filter.status = status;
        }

        if (search) {
            const pattern = new RegExp(escapeRegex(search), "i");
            filter.$or = [{ name: pattern }, { email: pattern }];
        }

        const [users, total, roles, policy, canCreate, canUpdate, canChangeStatus] =
            await Promise.all([
                User.find(filter)
                    .select("_id name email roleId status createdAt updatedAt")
                    .sort({ createdAt: -1 })
                    .skip((page - 1) * limit)
                    .limit(limit)
                    .lean(),
                User.countDocuments(filter),
                Role.find({ tenantId }).select("_id name permissions").lean(),
                loadUserManagementPolicy(context),
                hasPermission({ ...permissionInput, permission: "users.create" }),
                hasPermission({ ...permissionInput, permission: "users.update" }),
                hasPermission({ ...permissionInput, permission: "users.delete" }),
            ]);

        const rolesById = new Map(
            roles.map((role) => [role._id.toString(), role])
        );

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            users: users.map((user) => {
                const role = user.roleId
                    ? rolesById.get(user.roleId.toString())
                    : undefined;

                return publicUser(user, {
                    roleName: role?.name ?? null,
                    ownerUserId: policy.ownerUserId,
                    canEdit:
                        canUpdate &&
                        (user._id.toString() === context.userId ||
                            canManageUserWithRole(policy, role)),
                });
            }),
            pagination: {
                page,
                limit,
                total,
                totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit),
            },
            // UI hints only. Every action is re-checked on the server.
            capabilities: {
                currentUserId: context.userId,
                isOwner: policy.isOwner,
                canCreate,
                canUpdate,
                canChangeStatus,
                ownerDesignated: policy.ownerUserId !== null,
            },
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("GET /api/users error:", error);
        return errorResponse("Unable to retrieve users", 500);
    }
}

// POST /api/users
export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
    let auth: AuthenticatedContext | null = null;

    try {
        auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!auth) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        const context = auth;

        await connectDB();

        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            userId: context.userId,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
            roleId: context.roleId,
            permission: "users.create",
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const json = await readJsonBody(request);

        if (!json.ok) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid JSON request body", 400);
        }

        const parsed = createUserSchema.safeParse(json.body);

        if (!parsed.success) {
            return validationErrorResponse("Invalid user data", parsed.error);
        }

        const { name, email, password, roleId } = parsed.data;
        const tenantId = new Types.ObjectId(context.tenantId);

        // Fast duplicate check before the (slow) password hash.
        // The unique index and the in-transaction check remain authoritative.
        const existingUser = await User.exists({ tenantId, email });

        if (existingUser) {
            return errorResponse(DUPLICATE_EMAIL_MESSAGE, 409);
        }

        const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);

        await ensureTenantAdminGuard(tenantId);

        const dbSession = await mongoose.startSession();
        let createdUser: ReturnType<typeof publicUser> | null = null;

        try {
            await dbSession.withTransaction(async () => {
                createdUser = null;

                await lockTenantAdminGuard(tenantId, dbSession);

                // Role must belong to the caller's tenant and be active.
                const role = await Role.findOne({
                    _id: new Types.ObjectId(roleId),
                    tenantId,
                    status: "active",
                })
                    .select("_id name permissions")
                    .session(dbSession)
                    .lean();

                if (!role) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        400,
                        "The selected role is invalid or inactive for this business",
                        "INVALID_ROLE"
                    );
                }

                const policy = await loadUserManagementPolicy(
                    context,
                    dbSession
                );
                const grantsAdministrator = isPrivilegedRole(policy, role);

                if (!canAssignRole(policy, role)) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        403,
                        grantsAdministrator
                            ? "Only the business owner can create users with an administrator-level role"
                            : "You cannot assign a role with permissions you do not have",
                        "PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION_DENIED",
                        {
                            details: {
                                operation: "create",
                                email,
                                attemptedRoleId: String(role._id),
                                attemptedRoleName: role.name,
                            },
                        }
                    );
                }

                const duplicate = await User.exists({ tenantId, email }).session(
                    dbSession
                );

                if (duplicate) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        409,
                        DUPLICATE_EMAIL_MESSAGE,
                        "DUPLICATE_EMAIL"
                    );
                }

                const [user] = await User.create(
                    [
                        {
                            tenantId,
                            name,
                            email,
                            passwordHash,
                            roleId: role._id,
                            status: "active",
                        },
                    ],
                    { session: dbSession }
                );

                const actor = {
                    userId: new Types.ObjectId(context.userId),
                    tenantId,
                    deviceId: new Types.ObjectId(context.deviceId),
                };

                // Never include the password or its hash in audit data.
                await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                    actor,
                    {
                        action: "user.created",
                        entity: "User",
                        entityId: user._id,
                        description: `Created user ${email}`,
                        newValues: {
                            name,
                            email,
                            roleId: String(role._id),
                            roleName: role.name,
                            status: "active",
                        },
                    },
                    dbSession
                );

                if (grantsAdministrator) {
                    await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                        actor,
                        {
                            action: "user.admin_granted",
                            entity: "User",
                            entityId: user._id,
                            description: `Granted administrator-level role ${role.name} to ${email}`,
                            newValues: {
                                roleId: String(role._id),
                                roleName: role.name,
                            },
                        },
                        dbSession
                    );
                }

                createdUser = publicUser(user, {
                    roleName: role.name,
                    ownerUserId: policy.ownerUserId,
                });
            });
        } finally {
            await dbSession.endSession();
        }

        if (!createdUser) {
            throw new Error("User creation transaction did not complete");
        }

        return NextResponse.json(
            {
                success: true,
                message: "User created successfully",
                user: createdUser,
            },
            { status: 201 }
        );
    } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof UserManagementError) {
            if (auth) {
                await auditDeniedAttempt(auth, error);
            }
            return errorResponse(error.message, error.status);
        }

        if (isDuplicateKeyError(error)) {
            return errorResponse(DUPLICATE_EMAIL_MESSAGE, 409);
        }

        console.error("POST /api/users error:", error);
        return errorResponse("Unable to create user", 500);
    }
}
