import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { Types } from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { Role } from "@/lib/models/Role";
import { getAuthenticatedContext } from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";
import {
    canAssignRole,
    isPrivilegedRole,
    loadUserManagementPolicy,
} from "@/lib/auth/userManagementPolicy";
import { errorResponse } from "@/lib/users/userApi";

// GET /api/users/roles
// Active roles of the caller's tenant, with whether the caller may assign
// each one. "assignable" is a UI hint; POST/PATCH re-check it on the server.
export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
    try {
        const context = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!context) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        await connectDB();

        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            userId: context.userId,
            tenantId: context.tenantId,
            roleId: context.roleId,
            permission: "users.read",
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const [roles, policy] = await Promise.all([
            Role.find({
                tenantId: new Types.ObjectId(context.tenantId),
                status: "active",
            })
                .select("_id name description permissions")
                .sort({ name: 1 })
                .lean(),
            loadUserManagementPolicy(context),
        ]);

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            roles: roles.map((role) => ({
                id: role._id.toString(),
                name: role.name,
                description: role.description ?? null,
                isAdministrator: isPrivilegedRole(policy, role),
                assignable: canAssignRole(policy, role),
            })),
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("GET /api/users/roles error:", error);
        return errorResponse("Unable to retrieve roles", 500);
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\users\[id]\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose, { Types } from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { User } from "@/lib/models/User";
import { Role } from "@/lib/models/Role";
import { getAdministratorRoleIds } from "@/lib/auth/isAdministratorRole";
import {
    getAuthenticatedContext,
    type AuthenticatedContext,
} from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";
import {
    canAssignRole,
    canManageUserWithRole,
    ensureTenantAdminGuard,
    isPrivilegedRole,
    loadUserManagementPolicy,
    lockTenantAdminGuard,
} from "@/lib/auth/userManagementPolicy";
import { revokeAllUserSessions } from "@/lib/auth/sessionStore";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";
import {
    UserManagementError,
    auditDeniedAttempt,
    errorResponse,
    isDuplicateKeyError,
    publicUser,
    readJsonBody,
    updateUserSchema,
    validationErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/users/userApi";

type RouteContext = {
    params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

const DUPLICATE_EMAIL_MESSAGE =
    "A user with this email already exists in this business";

// GET /api/users/[id]
export async function GET(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    try {
        await connectDB();

        const auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!auth) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            userId: auth.userId,
            tenantId: auth.tenantId,
            roleId: auth.roleId,
            permission: "users.read",
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const { id } = await context.params;

        if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id) || !/^[a-f\d]{24}$/i.test(id)) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid user ID", 400);
        }

        const tenantId = new Types.ObjectId(auth.tenantId);

        // Tenant-scoped: another tenant's user is simply "not found".
        const user = await User.findOne({
            _id: new Types.ObjectId(id),
            tenantId,
        })
            .select("_id name email roleId status createdAt updatedAt")
            .lean();

        if (!user) {
            return errorResponse("User not found", 404);
        }

        const [role, policy] = await Promise.all([
            user.roleId
                ? Role.findOne({ _id: user.roleId, tenantId })
                    .select("_id name")
                    .lean()
                : Promise.resolve(null),
            loadUserManagementPolicy(auth),
        ]);

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            user: publicUser(user, {
                roleName: role?.name ?? null,
                ownerUserId: policy.ownerUserId,
            }),
        });
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("GET /api/users/[id] error:", error);
        return errorResponse("Unable to retrieve user", 500);
    }
}

// PATCH /api/users/[id]
export async function PATCH(
    request: NextRequest,
    context: RouteContext
) {
    let auth: AuthenticatedContext | null = null;

    try {
        await connectDB();

        auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!auth) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        const caller = auth;
        const permissionInput = {
            userId: caller.userId,
            tenantId: caller.tenantId,
            roleId: caller.roleId,
        };

        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            ...permissionInput,
            permission: "users.update",
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const { id } = await context.params;

        if (!Types.ObjectId.isValid(id) || !/^[a-f\d]{24}$/i.test(id)) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid user ID", 400);
        }

        const json = await readJsonBody(request);

        if (!json.ok) {
            return errorResponse("Request body must be valid JSON", 400);
        }

        const parsed = updateUserSchema.safeParse(json.body);

        if (!parsed.success) {
            return validationErrorResponse("Invalid input", parsed.error);
        }

        const input = parsed.data;

        // Activating or deactivating an account is the "delete" operation
        // of this module (users are never hard-deleted).
        if (input.status !== undefined) {
            const canChangeStatus = await hasPermission({
                ...permissionInput,
                permission: "users.delete",
            });

            if (!canChangeStatus) {
                return errorResponse(
                    "Permission denied: activating or deactivating users requires the users.delete permission",
                    403
                );
            }
        }

        const tenantId = new Types.ObjectId(caller.tenantId);
        const targetId = new Types.ObjectId(id);

        await ensureTenantAdminGuard(tenantId);

        const mongoSession = await mongoose.startSession();
        let responseUser: ReturnType<typeof publicUser> | null = null;

        try {
            await mongoSession.withTransaction(async () => {
                responseUser = null;

                // Serialize privilege changes for this tenant.
                await lockTenantAdminGuard(tenantId, mongoSession);

                // Read the target inside the transaction, tenant-scoped.
                const user = await User.findOne({
                    _id: targetId,
                    tenantId,
                }).session(mongoSession);

                if (!user) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        404,
                        "User not found",
                        "TARGET_USER_NOT_FOUND"
                    );
                }

                const policy = await loadUserManagementPolicy(
                    caller,
                    mongoSession
                );

                const isSelf = user._id.toString() === caller.userId;
                const isOwnerTarget =
                    policy.ownerUserId === user._id.toString();
                const currentRoleId = user.roleId?.toString() ?? null;

                const roleChanging =
                    input.roleId !== undefined &&
                    (input.roleId?.toLowerCase() ?? null) !== currentRoleId;
                const statusChanging =
                    input.status !== undefined &&
                    input.status !== user.status;
                const emailChanging =
                    input.email !== undefined && input.email !== user.email;

                if (isSelf && statusChanging && input.status === "inactive") {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        400,
                        "You cannot deactivate your own account",
                        "CANNOT_DEACTIVATE_SELF"
                    );
                }

                if (isSelf && (roleChanging || statusChanging)) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        403,
                        "You cannot change your own role or status",
                        "PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION_DENIED",
                        {
                            entityId: user._id,
                            details: {
                                operation: "update-self",
                                attemptedRoleId: input.roleId,
                                attemptedStatus: input.status,
                            },
                        }
                    );
                }

                // Owner protection is separate from administrator protection:
                // nobody can demote or deactivate the business owner here.
                if (isOwnerTarget && (roleChanging || statusChanging)) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        409,
                        "The business owner's role and status are protected and cannot be changed",
                        "OWNER_PROTECTED"
                    );
                }

                const [currentRole, nextRole] = await Promise.all([
                    user.roleId
                        ? Role.findOne({ _id: user.roleId, tenantId })
                            .select("_id name permissions")
                            .session(mongoSession)
                            .lean()
                        : Promise.resolve(null),
                    roleChanging && input.roleId
                        ? Role.findOne({
                            _id: new Types.ObjectId(input.roleId),
                            tenantId,
                            status: "active",
                        })
                            .select("_id name permissions")
                            .session(mongoSession)
                            .lean()
                        : Promise.resolve(null),
                ]);

                // Non-owners cannot modify administrators or users who hold
                // more permissions than they do.
                if (!isSelf && !canManageUserWithRole(policy, currentRole)) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        403,
                        "Only the business owner can change users who hold an administrator-level role",
                        "PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION_DENIED",
                        {
                            entityId: user._id,
                            details: {
                                operation: "update",
                                targetRoleId: currentRoleId,
                                attemptedRoleId: input.roleId,
                                attemptedStatus: input.status,
                            },
                        }
                    );
                }

                if (roleChanging && input.roleId) {
                    // Role must belong to the caller's tenant and be active.
                    if (!nextRole) {
                        throw new UserManagementError(
                            400,
                            "The selected role is invalid or inactive for this business",
                            "INVALID_ROLE"
                        );
                    }

                    if (!canAssignRole(policy, nextRole)) {
                        throw new UserManagementError(
                            403,
                            isPrivilegedRole(policy, nextRole)
                                ? "Only the business owner can grant an administrator-level role"
                                : "You cannot assign a role with permissions you do not have",
                            "PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION_DENIED",
                            {
                                entityId: user._id,
                                details: {
                                    operation: "update",
                                    attemptedRoleId: String(nextRole._id),
                                    attemptedRoleName: nextRole.name,
                                },
                            }
                        );
                    }
                }

                if (emailChanging) {
                    const duplicate = await User.exists({
                        tenantId,
                        email: input.email,
                        _id: { $ne: user._id },
                    }).session(mongoSession);

                    if (duplicate) {
                        throw new UserManagementError(
                            409,
                            DUPLICATE_EMAIL_MESSAGE,
                            "DUPLICATE_EMAIL"
                        );
                    }
                }

                const nextStatus = input.status ?? user.status;
                const nextRoleId: Types.ObjectId | undefined = roleChanging
                    ? nextRole?._id
                    : user.roleId;

                // Last-active-administrator (lockout) protection.
                if (roleChanging || statusChanging) {
                    const administratorRoleIds = await getAdministratorRoleIds(
                        tenantId,
                        mongoSession
                    );

                    const isAdministratorRole = (
                        roleId: Types.ObjectId | undefined | null
                    ) =>
                        roleId != null &&
                        administratorRoleIds.some((adminRoleId) =>
                            adminRoleId.equals(roleId)
                        );

                    const targetWouldStopBeingAdministrator =
                        user.status === "active" &&
                        isAdministratorRole(user.roleId) &&
                        (nextStatus !== "active" ||
                            !isAdministratorRole(nextRoleId));

                    if (targetWouldStopBeingAdministrator) {
                        const remainingAdministrators =
                            await User.countDocuments({
                                tenantId,
                                status: "active",
                                roleId: { $in: administratorRoleIds },
                                _id: { $ne: user._id },
                            }).session(mongoSession);

                        if (remainingAdministrators === 0) {
                            throw new UserManagementError(
                                409,
                                "You cannot deactivate or change the role of the last active administrator.",
                                "LAST_ACTIVE_ADMINISTRATOR"
                            );
                        }
                    }
                }

                const oldValues = {
                    name: user.name,
                    email: user.email,
                    roleId: currentRoleId,
                    roleName: currentRole?.name ?? null,
                    status: user.status,
                };

                if (input.name !== undefined) {
                    user.name = input.name;
                }

                if (emailChanging && input.email) {
                    user.email = input.email;
                }

                if (roleChanging) {
                    user.roleId = nextRoleId;
                }

                if (statusChanging && input.status) {
                    user.status = input.status;
                }

                await user.save({ session: mongoSession });

                // Deactivation ends every session of that user, in the same
                // transaction. Reactivating them later does not revive old
                // (possibly copied) tokens: they must log in again.
                if (statusChanging && user.status === "inactive") {
                    await revokeAllUserSessions(
                        tenantId,
                        user._id,
                        "user-deactivated",
                        mongoSession
                    );
                }

                const newValues = {
                    name: user.name,
                    email: user.email,
                    roleId: user.roleId?.toString() ?? null,
                    roleName: roleChanging
                        ? nextRole?.name ?? null
                        : currentRole?.name ?? null,
                    status: user.status,
                };

                const actor = {
                    userId: new Types.ObjectId(caller.userId),
                    tenantId,
                    deviceId: new Types.ObjectId(caller.deviceId),
                };

                const audit = (
                    action: string,
                    description: string,
                    values?: {
                        oldValues?: Record<string, unknown>;
                        newValues?: Record<string, unknown>;
                    }
                ) =>
                    AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                        actor,
                        {
                            action,
                            entity: "User",
                            entityId: user._id,
                            description,
                            ...values,
                        },
                        mongoSession
                    );

                // Audit entries are written in the same transaction, so a
                // failed audit write rolls back the user change too.
                await audit("user.updated", `Updated user ${user.email}`, {
                    oldValues,
                    newValues,
                });

                if (roleChanging) {
                    await audit(
                        "user.role_changed",
                        `Changed role of ${user.email} from ${oldValues.roleName ?? "none"} to ${newValues.roleName ?? "none"}`,
                        {
                            oldValues: {
                                roleId: oldValues.roleId,
                                roleName: oldValues.roleName,
                            },
                            newValues: {
                                roleId: newValues.roleId,
                                roleName: newValues.roleName,
                            },
                        }
                    );
                }

                if (statusChanging) {
                    await audit(
                        user.status === "active"
                            ? "user.activated"
                            : "user.deactivated",
                        `${user.status === "active" ? "Activated" : "Deactivated"} user ${user.email}`,
                        {
                            oldValues: { status: oldValues.status },
                            newValues: { status: user.status },
                        }
                    );
                }

                const wasAdministrator =
                    oldValues.status === "active" &&
                    isPrivilegedRole(policy, currentRole);
                const isAdministratorNow =
                    user.status === "active" &&
                    isPrivilegedRole(
                        policy,
                        roleChanging ? nextRole : currentRole
                    );

                if (wasAdministrator !== isAdministratorNow) {
                    await audit(
                        isAdministratorNow
                            ? "user.admin_granted"
                            : "user.admin_revoked",
                        `${isAdministratorNow ? "Granted" : "Revoked"} administrator-level access for ${user.email}`,
                        {
                            oldValues: { roleId: oldValues.roleId, status: oldValues.status },
                            newValues: { roleId: newValues.roleId, status: newValues.status },
                        }
                    );
                }

                responseUser = publicUser(user, {
                    roleName: newValues.roleName,
                    ownerUserId: policy.ownerUserId,
                });
            });
        } finally {
            await mongoSession.endSession();
        }

        if (!responseUser) {
            return errorResponse("Unable to update user", 500);
        }

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            message: "User updated successfully",
            user: responseUser,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof UserManagementError) {
            if (auth) {
                await auditDeniedAttempt(auth, error);
            }

            return errorResponse(error.message, error.status);
        }

        if (isDuplicateKeyError(error)) {
            return errorResponse(DUPLICATE_EMAIL_MESSAGE, 409);
        }

        console.error("PATCH /api/users/[id] error:", error);
        return errorResponse("Unable to update user", 500);
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\app\api\users\[id]\password\route.ts ==========

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import mongoose, { Types } from "mongoose";

import { connectDB } from "@/lib/db/mongodb";
import { User } from "@/lib/models/User";
import { Role } from "@/lib/models/Role";
import {
    getAuthenticatedContext,
    type AuthenticatedContext,
} from "@/lib/auth/getAuthenticatedContext";
import { hasPermission } from "@/lib/auth/hasPermission";
import {
    canManageUserWithRole,
    ensureTenantAdminGuard,
    loadUserManagementPolicy,
    lockTenantAdminGuard,
} from "@/lib/auth/userManagementPolicy";
import { revokeAllUserSessions } from "@/lib/auth/sessionStore";
import { hashPassword } from "@/app/api/auth/password";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";
import {
    UserManagementError,
    auditDeniedAttempt,
    errorResponse,
    readJsonBody,
    resetPasswordSchema,
    validationErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/users/userApi";

type RouteContext = {
    params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
};

/*
 * POST /api/users/[id]/password
 *
 * An authorized administrator sets a new (temporary) password for another
 * user of the same business. Same rules as editing that user:
 *   - requires users.update
 *   - tenant-scoped (other businesses' users are "not found")
 *   - only the owner may reset administrators' passwords
 *   - the owner's password can only be changed by the owner (change-password)
 *   - users change their own password via /api/auth/change-password
 * Every session of the target user is revoked. The password is never
 * returned or logged.
 */
export async function POST(request: NextRequest, context: RouteContext) {
    let auth: AuthenticatedContext | null = null;

    try {
        await connectDB();

        auth = await getAuthenticatedContext(request);

        if (!auth) {
            return errorResponse("Authentication required", 401);
        }

        const caller = auth;

        const allowed = await hasPermission({
            userId: caller.userId,
            tenantId: caller.tenantId,
            roleId: caller.roleId,
            permission: "users.update",
        });

        if (!allowed) {
            return errorResponse("Permission denied", 403);
        }

        const { id } = await context.params;

        if (!/^[a-f\d]{24}$/i.test(id)) {
            return errorResponse("Invalid user ID", 400);
        }

        if (id.toLowerCase() === caller.userId) {
            return errorResponse(
                "Use 'Change password' to change your own password",
                400
            );
        }

        const json = await readJsonBody(request);

        if (!json.ok) {
            return errorResponse("Request body must be valid JSON", 400);
        }

        const parsed = resetPasswordSchema.safeParse(json.body);

        if (!parsed.success) {
            return validationErrorResponse("Invalid password data", parsed.error);
        }

        const tenantId = new Types.ObjectId(caller.tenantId);
        const targetId = new Types.ObjectId(id);
        const passwordHash = await hashPassword(parsed.data.newPassword);

        await ensureTenantAdminGuard(tenantId);

        const dbSession = await mongoose.startSession();
        let revokedSessions = 0;

        try {
            await dbSession.withTransaction(async () => {
                // Serialize with other privilege-sensitive user changes.
                await lockTenantAdminGuard(tenantId, dbSession);

                const user = await User.findOne({ _id: targetId, tenantId })
                    .select("_id email roleId")
                    .session(dbSession)
                    .lean();

                if (!user) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(404, "User not found", "TARGET_USER_NOT_FOUND");
                }

                const policy = await loadUserManagementPolicy(caller, dbSession);

                if (policy.ownerUserId === user._id.toString()) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        409,
                        "The business owner's password can only be changed by the owner",
                        "OWNER_PROTECTED"
                    );
                }

                const currentRole = user.roleId
                    ? await Role.findOne({ _id: user.roleId, tenantId })
                        .select("_id permissions")
                        .session(dbSession)
                        .lean()
                    : null;

                if (!canManageUserWithRole(policy, currentRole)) {
                    throw new UserManagementError(
                        403,
                        "Only the business owner can reset the password of a user with an administrator-level role",
                        "PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION_DENIED",
                        {
                            entityId: user._id,
                            details: { operation: "password-reset", targetRoleId: user.roleId?.toString() ?? null },
                        }
                    );
                }

                await User.updateOne(
                    { _id: user._id, tenantId },
                    // The user must choose their own password at next login.
                    { $set: { passwordHash, mustChangePassword: true } },
                    { session: dbSession }
                );

                revokedSessions = await revokeAllUserSessions(
                    tenantId,
                    user._id,
                    "password-reset-by-admin",
                    dbSession
                );

                await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                    {
                        userId: new Types.ObjectId(caller.userId),
                        tenantId,
                        deviceId: new Types.ObjectId(caller.deviceId),
                    },
                    {
                        action: "user.password_reset",
                        entity: "User",
                        entityId: user._id,
                        description: `Reset password of ${user.email}; ${revokedSessions} session(s) revoked`,
                        newValues: { revokedSessions },
                    },
                    dbSession
                );
            });
        } finally {
            await dbSession.endSession();
        }

        return NextResponse.json({
            success: true,
            message: "Password reset. The user has been signed out everywhere and must use the new password.",
            revokedSessions,
        });
    } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof UserManagementError) {
            if (auth) {
                await auditDeniedAttempt(auth, error);
            }

            return errorResponse(error.message, error.status);
        }

        console.error(
            "POST /api/users/[id]/password error:",
            error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown"
        );
        return errorResponse("Unable to reset password", 500);
    }
}

import mongoose, { Schema, Document, Model } from "mongoose";

export interface IAuditLog extends Document {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    deviceId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    action: string;

    entity: string;
    entityId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    description?: string;

    oldValues?: Record<string, unknown>;
    newValues?: Record<string, unknown>;

    createdAt: Date;
}

const AuditLogSchema = new Schema<IAuditLog>(
    {
        tenantId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Tenant",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        userId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "User",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        deviceId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Device",
            index: true,
        },

        action: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
        },

        entity: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
        },

        entityId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            index: true,
        },

        description: {
            type: String,
            trim: true,
        },

        oldValues: {
            type: Schema.Types.Mixed,
        },

        newValues: {
            type: Schema.Types.Mixed,
        },
    },
    {
        timestamps: {
            createdAt: true,
            updatedAt: false,
        },
    }
);

AuditLogSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    createdAt: -1,
});

export const AuditLog: Model<IAuditLog> =
    mongoose.models.AuditLog ||
    mongoose.model<IAuditLog>("AuditLog", AuditLogSchema);

import mongoose, { Schema, Document, Model } from "mongoose";

export interface IDevice extends Document {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    deviceName: string;

    deviceType:
    | "pos"
    | "desktop"
    | "laptop"
    | "tablet"
    | "mobile"
    | "other";

    deviceId: string;

    status: "active" | "inactive";

    lastSeenAt?: Date;

    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
}

const DeviceSchema = new Schema<IDevice>(
    {
        tenantId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Tenant",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        userId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "User",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        deviceName: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
        },

        deviceType: {
            type: String,
            enum: [
                "pos",
                "desktop",
                "laptop",
                "tablet",
                "mobile",
                "other",
            ],
            required: true,
        },

        deviceId: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
        },

        status: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["active", "inactive"],
            default: "active",
        },

        lastSeenAt: {
            type: Date,
        },

    {
        timestamps: true,
    }
);

DeviceSchema.index(
    { tenantId: 1, deviceId: 1 },
    { unique: true }
);

========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\models\LoginRateLimit.ts ==========
import mongoose, { Schema, Document, Model } from "mongoose";

/*
 * Counter for failed login attempts, shared by every app instance.
 * "key" is an HMAC of the IP and/or normalized email, so raw IPs and
 * email addresses are never stored here.
 */
export interface ILoginRateLimit extends Document {
    key: string;
    failures: number;
    windowStart: Date;
    lockedUntil?: Date;
    lockoutLevel: number;
    lastLockedAt?: Date;
    expiresAt: Date;
}

const LoginRateLimitSchema = new Schema<ILoginRateLimit>(
    {
        key: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
        },

        failures: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            required: true,
        },

        windowStart: {
            type: Date,
            required: true,
        },

        lockedUntil: {
            type: Date,
        },

        // Number of consecutive lockouts, used for progressive lockouts.
        lockoutLevel: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            required: true,
        },

        lastLockedAt: {
            type: Date,
        },

        expiresAt: {
            type: Date,
            required: true,
        },
    },
    {
        timestamps: true,
    }
);

LoginRateLimitSchema.index({ key: 1 }, { unique: true });

// Automatic cleanup of idle counters.
LoginRateLimitSchema.index({ expiresAt: 1 }, { expireAfterSeconds: 0 });

export const LoginRateLimit: Model<ILoginRateLimit> =
    mongoose.models.LoginRateLimit ||
    mongoose.model<ILoginRateLimit>("LoginRateLimit", LoginRateLimitSchema);


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\models\Payment.ts ==========
import mongoose, {
    Schema,
    Document,
    Model,
} from "mongoose";

export interface IPayment extends Document {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    /*
     * Idempotency key for offline/sync operations.
     *
     * One operationId can create only one payment
     * within a tenant.
     */
    operationId?: string;

    paymentNo: string;

    type:
    | "receipt"
    | "payment"
    | "sales_refund"
    | "purchase_refund";

    amount: number;

    paymentMethod:
    | "cash"
    | "upi"
    | "card"
    | "bank"
    | "credit"
    | "other";

    paymentDate: Date;

    customerId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    supplierId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    saleId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    purchaseId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    salesReturnId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    purchaseReturnId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    referenceNo?: string;
    notes?: string;

    status:
    | "pending"
    | "completed"
    | "failed"
    | "cancelled";

    createdBy?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
}

const PaymentSchema = new Schema<IPayment>(
    {
        tenantId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Tenant",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        /*
         * Idempotency key.
         *
         * Used mainly for offline/sync operations.
         */
        operationId: {
            type: String,
            trim: true,
        },

        paymentNo: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
        },

        type: {
            type: String,
            enum: [
                "receipt",
                "payment",
                "sales_refund",
                "purchase_refund",
            ],
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        amount: {
            type: Number,
            required: true,
            min: 0.01,
        },

        paymentMethod: {
            type: String,
            enum: [
                "cash",
                "upi",
                "card",
                "bank",
                "credit",
                "other",
            ],
            required: true,
        },

        paymentDate: {
            type: Date,
            required: true,
            default: Date.now,
        },

        customerId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Customer",
            index: true,
        },

        supplierId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Supplier",
            index: true,
        },

        saleId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Sale",
            index: true,
        },

        purchaseId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Purchase",
            index: true,
        },

        salesReturnId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "SalesReturn",
            index: true,
        },

        purchaseReturnId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "PurchaseReturn",
            index: true,
        },

        referenceNo: {
            type: String,
            trim: true,
        },

        notes: {
            type: String,
            trim: true,
        },

        status: {
            type: String,
            enum: [
                "pending",
                "completed",
                "failed",
                "cancelled",
            ],
            default: "completed",
            index: true,
        },

        createdBy: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "User",
        },
    },
    {
        timestamps: true,
    }
);

/*
 * Payment number must be unique
 * within each tenant.
 */
PaymentSchema.index(
    { tenantId: 1, paymentNo: 1 },
    { unique: true }
);

/*
 * Idempotency protection.
 *
 * If operationId exists:
 *
 * tenant A + OP-001 → allowed
 * tenant A + OP-001 → duplicate
 *
 * tenant B + OP-001 → allowed
 *
 * This allows the same operationId to exist
 * in different tenants.
 */
PaymentSchema.index(
    { tenantId: 1, operationId: 1 },
    {
        unique: true,
        partialFilterExpression: {
            operationId: {
                $exists: true,
            },
        },
    }
);

/*
 * Useful for payment reports
 * such as daily/monthly collections.
 */
PaymentSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    paymentDate: -1,
});

/*
 * Useful for filtering payments
 * by method.
 */
PaymentSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    paymentMethod: 1,
});

/*
 * Useful for finding customer
 * payment history.
 */
PaymentSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    customerId: 1,
    paymentDate: -1,
});

/*
 * Useful for finding supplier
 * payment history.
 */
PaymentSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    supplierId: 1,
    paymentDate: -1,
});

export const Payment: Model<IPayment> =
    mongoose.models.Payment ||
    mongoose.model<IPayment>(
        "Payment",
        PaymentSchema
    );

import mongoose, { Schema, Document, Model } from "mongoose";

/* =========================
   Purchase Item
========================= */

export interface IPurchaseItem {
    productId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    productSnapshot: {
        name: string;
        SKU: string;
        unit: string;
    };

    quantity: number;
    unitPrice: number;

    discountRate: number;
    discountAmount: number;

    tax: {
        rate: number;
        amount: number;
    };

    total: number;
}

/* =========================
   Purchase
========================= */

export interface IPurchase extends Document {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    // Used for offline-sync idempotency
    operationId?: string;

    purchaseNo: string;

    supplierId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    supplierSnapshot: {
        name: string;
        supplierCode: string;
        phone?: string;
        gstin?: string;
    };

    purchaseDate: Date;

    supplierInvoiceNo?: string;
    supplierInvoiceDate?: Date;

    dueDate?: Date;

    warehouse: {
        name: string;
        location?: string;
    };

    items: IPurchaseItem[];

    subtotal: number;
    discountTotal: number;
    taxTotal: number;
    shippingCost: number;
    grandTotal: number;

    paidAmount: number;
    dueAmount: number;

    paymentStatus: "unpaid" | "partial" | "paid";

    status: "draft" | "received" | "cancelled";

    notes?: string;

    createdBy?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
}

/* =========================
   Purchase Item Schema
========================= */

const PurchaseItemSchema = new Schema<IPurchaseItem>(
    {
        productId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Product",
            required: true,
        },

        productSnapshot: {
            name: {
                type: String,
                required: true,
                trim: true,
            },

            SKU: {
                type: String,
                required: true,
                trim: true,
                uppercase: true,
            },

            unit: {
                type: String,
                required: true,
                trim: true,
            },
        },

        quantity: {
            type: Number,
            required: true,
            min: 0.001,
        },

        unitPrice: {
            type: Number,
            required: true,
            min: 0,
        },

        // Discount percentage
        discountRate: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            min: 0,
            max: 100,
        },

        // Actual discount amount in currency
        discountAmount: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            min: 0,
        },

        tax: {
            rate: {
                type: Number,
                default: 0,
                min: 0,
            },

            amount: {
                type: Number,
                default: 0,
                min: 0,
            },
        },

        total: {
            type: Number,
            required: true,
            min: 0,
        },
    },
    {
        _id: false,
    }
);

/* =========================
   Purchase Schema
========================= */

const PurchaseSchema = new Schema<IPurchase>(
    {
        tenantId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Tenant",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        // Used to identify the exact offline operation
        // and prevent duplicate processing during retries.
        operationId: {
            type: String,
            trim: true,
        },

        purchaseNo: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
            uppercase: true,
        },

        supplierId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Supplier",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        supplierSnapshot: {
            name: {
                type: String,
                required: true,
                trim: true,
            },

            supplierCode: {
                type: String,
                required: true,
                trim: true,
                uppercase: true,
            },

            phone: {
                type: String,
                trim: true,
            },

            gstin: {
                type: String,
                trim: true,
                uppercase: true,
            },
        },

        purchaseDate: {
            type: Date,
            required: true,
            default: Date.now,
        },

        supplierInvoiceNo: {
            type: String,
            trim: true,
        },

        supplierInvoiceDate: {
            type: Date,
        },

        dueDate: {
            type: Date,
        },

        warehouse: {
            name: {
                type: String,
                required: true,
                trim: true,
            },

            location: {
                type: String,
                trim: true,
            },
        },

        items: {
            type: [PurchaseItemSchema],
            required: true,

            validate: {
                validator: function (items: IPurchaseItem[]) {
                    return items.length > 0;
                },
                message: "Purchase must contain at least one item",
            },
        },

        subtotal: {
            type: Number,
            required: true,
            min: 0,
        },

        discountTotal: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            min: 0,
        },

        taxTotal: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            min: 0,
        },

        shippingCost: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            min: 0,
        },

        grandTotal: {
            type: Number,
            required: true,
            min: 0,
        },

        paidAmount: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            min: 0,
        },

        dueAmount: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            min: 0,
        },

        paymentStatus: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["unpaid", "partial", "paid"],
            default: "unpaid",
        },

        status: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["draft", "received", "cancelled"],
            default: "draft",
        },

        notes: {
            type: String,
            trim: true,
        },

        createdBy: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "User",
        },
    },
    {
        timestamps: true,
    }
);

/* =========================
   Indexes
========================= */

// Purchase number unique for each tenant
PurchaseSchema.index(
    {
        tenantId: 1,
        purchaseNo: 1,
    },
    {
        unique: true,
    }
);

// Each offline operation can be processed only once
// inside a tenant.
//
// Partial unique index allows normal online purchases
// that do not have an operationId.
PurchaseSchema.index(
    {
        tenantId: 1,
        operationId: 1,
    },
    {
        unique: true,
        partialFilterExpression: {
            operationId: {
                $exists: true,
            },
        },
    }
);

// Find purchases by supplier
PurchaseSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    supplierId: 1,
});

// Find purchases by date
PurchaseSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    purchaseDate: -1,
});

// Find unpaid / partial purchases
PurchaseSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    paymentStatus: 1,
});

/* =========================
   Model
========================= */

export const Purchase: Model<IPurchase> =
    mongoose.models.Purchase ||
    mongoose.model<IPurchase>("Purchase", PurchaseSchema);

import mongoose, {
    Schema,
    Document,
    Model,
} from "mongoose";

export interface IPurchaseReturn extends Document {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    // Idempotency / Offline Sync
    operationId?: string;

    purchaseReturnNo: string;

    purchaseId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    supplierId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    supplierSnapshot: {
        name: string;
        supplierCode: string;
        phone?: string;
        gstin?: string;
    };

    returnDate: Date;

    warehouse: {
        name: string;
        location?: string;
    };

    items: {
        productId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

        productSnapshot: {
            name: string;
            SKU: string;
            unit: string;
        };

        quantity: number;
        unitPrice: number;

        discountRate: number;
        discountAmount: number;

        tax: {
            rate: number;
            amount: number;
        };

        total: number;
    }[];

    subtotal: number;
    discountTotal: number;
    taxTotal: number;
    shippingCost: number;
    grandTotal: number;

    refundAmount: number;

    refundStatus:
    | "pending"
    | "refunded"
    | "not_applicable";

    status:
    | "draft"
    | "completed"
    | "cancelled";

    reason?: string;
    notes?: string;

    createdBy?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
}

const PurchaseReturnSchema =
    new Schema<IPurchaseReturn>(
        {

            tenantId: {
                type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
                ref: "Tenant",
                required: true,
                index: true,
            },

            operationId: {
                type: String,
                trim: true,
            },

            purchaseReturnNo: {
                type: String,
                required: true,
                trim: true,

            purchaseId: {
                type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
                ref: "Purchase",
                required: true,
                index: true,
            },

            // Supplier
            supplierId: {
                type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
                ref: "Supplier",
                required: true,
                index: true,
            },

            // Supplier snapshot
            supplierSnapshot: {

                    type: String,
                    required: true,
                    trim: true,
                },

                supplierCode: {
                    type: String,
                    required: true,
                    trim: true,
                },

                phone: {
                    type: String,
                    trim: true,
                },

                gstin: {
                    type: String,
                    trim: true,
                },
            },

            // Return date
            returnDate: {
                type: Date,
                required: true,
                default: Date.now,
            },

            // Warehouse
            warehouse: {
                name: {
                    type: String,
                    required: true,
                    trim: true,
                },

                location: {
                    type: String,
                    trim: true,
                },
            },

            // Returned items
            items: [
                {
                    productId: {
                        type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
                        ref: "Product",
                        required: true,
                    },

                    productSnapshot: {
                        name: {
                            type: String,
                            required: true,
                            trim: true,
                        },

                        SKU: {
                            type: String,
                            required: true,
                            trim: true,
                        },

                        unit: {
                            type: String,
                            required: true,
                            trim: true,
                        },
                    },

                    quantity: {
                        type: Number,
                        required: true,
                        min: 0.000001,

                        required: true,
                        min: 0,
                    },

                    discountRate: {
                        type: Number,
                        required: true,
                        default: 0,
                        min: 0,
                        max: 100,
                    },

                        type: Number,
                        required: true,
                        default: 0,
                        min: 0,
                    },

                    tax: {
                        rate: {
                            type: Number,
                            required: true,
                            default: 0,
                            min: 0,
                        },

                        amount: {
                            type: Number,
                            required: true,
                            default: 0,
                            min: 0,
                        },
                    },

                    total: {
                        type: Number,
                        required: true,
                        min: 0,
                    },
                },
            ],

            // Totals
            subtotal: {
                type: Number,
                required: true,
                min: 0,
            },

            discountTotal: {
                type: Number,
                required: true,
                default: 0,
                min: 0,
            },

            taxTotal: {
                type: Number,
                required: true,
                default: 0,
                min: 0,
            },

            shippingCost: {
                type: Number,
                required: true,
                default: 0,
                min: 0,
            },

            grandTotal: {
                type: Number,
                required: true,
                min: 0,
            },

            // Refund
            refundAmount: {
                type: Number,
                required: true,
                default: 0,
                min: 0,
            },

            refundStatus: {
                type: String,
                enum: [
                    "pending",
                    "refunded",
                    "not_applicable",
                ],
                default: "not_applicable",
            },

            // Return status
            status: {
                type: String,
                enum: [
                    "draft",
                    "completed",
                    "cancelled",
                ],
                default: "completed",
            },

            // Additional information
            reason: {
                type: String,
                trim: true,
            },

            notes: {
                type: String,
                trim: true,
            },

            // User who created the return
            createdBy: {
                type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
                ref: "User",
            },
        },
        {
            timestamps: true,
        }
    );


// Unique purchase return number per tenant
PurchaseReturnSchema.index(
    {
        tenantId: 1,
        purchaseReturnNo: 1,
    },
    {
        unique: true,
    }
);


// Unique sync operation per tenant
// This prevents the same offline operation
// from creating multiple PurchaseReturn documents.
PurchaseReturnSchema.index(
    {
        tenantId: 1,
        operationId: 1,
    },
    {
        unique: true,
        partialFilterExpression: {
            operationId: {
                $exists: true,
            },
        },
    }
);


// Find returns for a purchase
PurchaseReturnSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    purchaseId: 1,
});


// Find returns for a supplier
PurchaseReturnSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    supplierId: 1,
});


// Sort returns by date
PurchaseReturnSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    returnDate: -1,
});


// Filter by status
PurchaseReturnSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    status: 1,
});

    mongoose.models.PurchaseReturn ||
    mongoose.model<IPurchaseReturn>(
        "PurchaseReturn",
        PurchaseReturnSchema

    // Used for offline-sync idempotency
    operationId?: string;

        tenantId: 1,
        operationId: 1,

        unique: true,

                $exists: true,

    tenantId: 1,
    paymentStatus: 1,
});

SaleSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    status: 1,
});

   Model
========================= */

========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\models\Session.ts ==========
import mongoose, { Schema, Document, Model } from "mongoose";

/*
 * Server-side login session.
 *
 * The signed session cookie (JWT) carries a random session ID ("sid").
 * Only the SHA-256 hash of that ID is stored here, so a database leak does
 * not reveal usable session identifiers. Every authenticated request must
 * find an active (not revoked, not expired) session with that hash.
 */
export interface ISession extends Document {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    // Device document _id (not the client-side device string).
    deviceId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    tokenHash: string;

    expiresAt: Date;
    revokedAt?: Date;
    revokedReason?: string;

    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
}

const SessionSchema = new Schema<ISession>(
    {
        tenantId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Tenant",
            required: true,
        },

        userId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "User",
            required: true,
        },

        deviceId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Device",
            required: true,
        },

        tokenHash: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
        },

        expiresAt: {
            type: Date,
            required: true,
        },

        revokedAt: {
            type: Date,
        },

        revokedReason: {
            type: String,
            trim: true,
        },
    },
    {
        timestamps: true,
    }
);

// Lookup on every authenticated request.
SessionSchema.index({ tokenHash: 1 }, { unique: true });

// "Revoke all sessions of this user" (deactivation, logout everywhere).
SessionSchema.index({ tenantId: 1, userId: 1, revokedAt: 1 });

// MongoDB deletes sessions automatically once they expire (TTL cleanup).
// Expiry is ALSO checked in every query, because the TTL monitor only
// runs about once a minute.
SessionSchema.index({ expiresAt: 1 }, { expireAfterSeconds: 0 });

export const Session: Model<ISession> =
    mongoose.models.Session ||
    mongoose.model<ISession>("Session", SessionSchema);


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\models\StockMovement.ts ==========
import mongoose, {
    Schema,
    Document,
    Model,
} from "mongoose";

/* =========================
   Stock Movement
========================= */

export interface IStockMovement extends Document {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    productId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    /**
     * Idempotency key used mainly
     * for offline stock adjustments.
     *
     * Same operationId must not
     * create another stock movement
     * for the same tenant.
     */
    operationId?: string;

    type:
    | "purchase"
    | "sale"
    | "purchase_return"
    | "sales_return"
    | "adjustment";

    quantity: number;

    referenceId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    previousStock: number;
    newStock: number;

    warehouse: {
        name: string;
        location?: string;
    };

    reason?: string;

    createdBy?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
}

/* =========================
   Stock Movement Schema
========================= */

const StockMovementSchema =
    new Schema<IStockMovement>(
        {
            tenantId: {
                type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
                ref: "Tenant",
                required: true,
                index: true,
            },

            productId: {
                type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
                ref: "Product",
                required: true,
                index: true,
            },

            operationId: {
                type: String,
                trim: true,
            },

            type: {
                type: String,
                enum: [
                    "purchase",
                    "sale",
                    "purchase_return",
                    "sales_return",
                    "adjustment",
                ],
                required: true,
                index: true,
            },

            quantity: {
                type: Number,
                required: true,
            },

            referenceId: {
                type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            },

            previousStock: {
                type: Number,
                required: true,
                min: 0,
            },

            newStock: {
                type: Number,
                required: true,
                min: 0,
            },

            warehouse: {
                name: {
                    type: String,
                    required: true,
                    trim: true,
                },

                location: {
                    type: String,
                    trim: true,
                },
            },

            reason: {
                type: String,
                trim: true,
            },

            createdBy: {
                type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
                ref: "User",
            },
        },
        {
            timestamps: true,
        }
    );

/* =========================
   Indexes
========================= */

StockMovementSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    productId: 1,
    createdAt: -1,
});

StockMovementSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    type: 1,
    createdAt: -1,
});

StockMovementSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    createdBy: 1,
});

/*
 * Idempotency protection.
 *
 * The same operationId can only be
 * used once within the same tenant.
 *
 * Existing movements without an
 * operationId are still allowed.
 */
StockMovementSchema.index(
    {
        tenantId: 1,
        operationId: 1,
    },
    {
        unique: true,
        partialFilterExpression: {
            operationId: {
                $exists: true,
            },
        },
    }
);

/* =========================
   Model Handling
========================= */

/*
 * During development Next.js can keep
 * previously compiled Mongoose models
 * in memory during hot reloads.
 *
 * If the existing model does not contain
 * operationId, remove that stale model
 * and compile the current schema.
 */

let StockMovementModel: Model<IStockMovement>;

const existingModel =
    mongoose.models.StockMovement as
    | Model<IStockMovement>
    | undefined;

if (
    existingModel &&
    existingModel.schema.path("operationId")
) {
    StockMovementModel = existingModel;
} else {
    if (existingModel) {
        delete mongoose.models.StockMovement;
    }

    StockMovementModel =
        mongoose.model<IStockMovement>(

import mongoose, { Schema, Document, Model } from "mongoose";

export interface ISyncOperation extends Document {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    deviceId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    operationId: string;

    operationType:
    | "CREATE"
    | "UPDATE"
    | "DELETE";

    entityType:
    | "Sale"
    | "Purchase"
    | "SalesReturn"
    | "PurchaseReturn"
    | "Payment"
    | "StockAdjustment"
    | "Customer"
    | "Supplier"
    | "Product";

    entityId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    payload: Record<string, unknown>;

    // User whose session uploaded the operation. Missing on legacy records.
    userId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    status:
    | "PENDING"
    | "PROCESSING"
    | "SYNCED"
    | "FAILED"
    | "NEEDS_ATTENTION"
    | "CANCELLED";

    attempts: number;

    // Processing lease: only the holder of leaseToken may finish the
    // operation; an expired lease can be reclaimed after a crash.
    leaseToken?: string;
    leaseExpiresAt?: Date;

    // Why the last attempt failed: RETRYABLE (temporary), PERMANENT
    // (business/data error) or RETRIES_EXHAUSTED.
    failureKind?: "RETRYABLE" | "PERMANENT" | "RETRIES_EXHAUSTED";

    lastAttemptAt?: Date;

    syncedAt?: Date;

    errorMessage?: string;

    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
}

const SyncOperationSchema = new Schema<ISyncOperation>(
    {
        tenantId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Tenant",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        deviceId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Device",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        operationId: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
        },

        operationType: {
            type: String,
            enum: [
                "CREATE",
                "UPDATE",
                "DELETE",
            ],
            required: true,
        },

        entityType: {
            type: String,
            enum: [
                "Sale",
                "Purchase",
                "SalesReturn",
                "PurchaseReturn",
                "Payment",
                "StockAdjustment",
                "Customer",
                "Supplier",
                "Product",
            ],
            required: true,
        },

        entityId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
        },

        payload: {
            type: Schema.Types.Mixed,
            required: true,
        },

        status: {
            type: String,
            enum: [
                "PENDING",
                "PROCESSING",
                "SYNCED",
                "FAILED",
                "NEEDS_ATTENTION",
                "CANCELLED",
            ],
            default: "PENDING",
            required: true,
        },

        userId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "User",
        },

        attempts: {
            type: Number,
            default: 0,
            min: 0,
        },

        leaseToken: {
            type: String,
        },

        leaseExpiresAt: {
            type: Date,
        },

        failureKind: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["RETRYABLE", "PERMANENT", "RETRIES_EXHAUSTED"],
        },

        lastAttemptAt: {
            type: Date,
        },

        syncedAt: {
            type: Date,
        },

        errorMessage: {
            type: String,
            trim: true,
        },
    },
    {
        timestamps: true,
    }
);

// operationId must be unique inside a tenant.
SyncOperationSchema.index(
    { tenantId: 1, operationId: 1 },
    { unique: true }
);

// Helps find pending/failed operations for a device.
SyncOperationSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    deviceId: 1,
    status: 1,
});

// Helps retrieve synchronization history.
SyncOperationSchema.index({
    tenantId: 1,
    createdAt: -1,
});

export const SyncOperation: Model<ISyncOperation> =
    mongoose.models.SyncOperation ||
    mongoose.model<ISyncOperation>(
        "SyncOperation",
        SyncOperationSchema
    );

            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Tenant",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        name: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
        },

        code: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
            uppercase: true,
        },

        type: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["GST", "IGST", "CGST", "SGST", "CESS", "OTHER"],
            required: true,
        },

            type: Number,
            required: true,
            min: 0,
            max: 100,
        },

            type: String,
            trim: true,

        status: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["active", "inactive"],
            default: "active",

            ref: "User",
            required: true,

    { unique: true }
);

import mongoose, { Schema, Document, Model } from "mongoose";

export interface IUser extends Document {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    name: string;
    email: string;
    passwordHash: string;

    roleId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;

    status: "active" | "inactive";

    // Set when an administrator resets the password. Until the user picks
    // a new password, only the password-change endpoints accept the session.
    // Missing/false means unrestricted. Never settable through user APIs.
    mustChangePassword?: boolean;

    createdAt: Date;
    updatedAt: Date;
}

const UserSchema = new Schema<IUser>(
    {
        tenantId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Tenant",
            required: true,
            index: true,
        },

        name: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
        },

        email: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
            trim: true,
            lowercase: true,
        },

        passwordHash: {
            type: String,
            required: true,
        },

        roleId: {
            type: Schema.Types.ObjectId,
            ref: "Role",
        },

        status: {
            type: String,
            enum: ["active", "inactive"],
            default: "active",
        },

        mustChangePassword: {
            type: Boolean,
            default: false,
        },
    },
    {
        timestamps: true,
    }
);

// Same email can exist in different businesses,
// but should be unique within the same business.
UserSchema.index(
    { tenantId: 1, email: 1 },
    { unique: true }
);

export const User: Model<IUser> =
    mongoose.models.User ||
    mongoose.model<IUser>("User", UserSchema);


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\services\AuditLogService.ts ==========

import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { AuditLog } from "@/lib/models/AuditLog";

export interface AuditLogUserContext {
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    deviceId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
}

export interface CreateAuditLogInput {
    action: string;
    entity: string;
    entityId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    description?: string;
    oldValues?: Record<string, unknown>;
    newValues?: Record<string, unknown>;
}

export class AuditLogService {
    /**
     * Create an audit log.
     *
     * When a MongoDB session is provided, the audit log participates
     * in the same transaction as the related business operation.
     */
    static async createAuditLog(
        user: AuditLogUserContext,
        input: CreateAuditLogInput,
        session?: mongoose.ClientSession
    ) {
        if (!user.userId) {
            throw new Error("User ID is required");
        }

        if (!user.tenantId) {
            throw new Error("Tenant ID is required");
        }

        if (!input.action?.trim()) {
            throw new Error("Audit action is required");
        }

        if (!input.entity?.trim()) {
            throw new Error("Audit entity is required");
        }

        const auditLogData = {
            tenantId: user.tenantId,
            userId: user.userId,
            deviceId: user.deviceId,
            action: input.action.trim(),
            entity: input.entity.trim(),
            entityId: input.entityId,
            description: input.description?.trim(),
            oldValues: input.oldValues,
            newValues: input.newValues,
        };

        // TEMPORARY LOCAL TEST ONLY.
        // Set this environment variable to "true" to simulate audit failure.
        if (
            process.env.TEST_AUDIT_LOG_FAILURE === "true" &&
            process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production"
        ) {
            throw new Error("TEST_ONLY_AUDIT_FAILURE");
        }

        const [auditLog] = await AuditLog.create(
            [auditLogData],
            {
                session,
                ordered: true,
            }
        );

        return auditLog;
    }
}



========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\services\LedgerService.ts ==========
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { LedgerEntry } from "@/lib/models/LedgerEntry";

export interface LedgerUserContext {
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
}

export type LedgerAccount =
    | "cash"
    | "bank"
    | "accounts_receivable"
    | "accounts_payable"
    | "sales_revenue"
    | "inventory"
    | "sales_return"
    | "purchase_return";

export type LedgerTransactionType =
    | "sale"
    | "purchase"
    | "receipt"
    | "payment"
    | "sales_refund"
    | "purchase_refund";

export interface LedgerEntryInput {
    transactionId: string;

    transactionType: LedgerTransactionType;

    account: LedgerAccount;

    entryType: "debit" | "credit";

    amount: number;

    customerId?: string;

    supplierId?: string;

    referenceId?: string;

    description?: string;

    entryDate?: Date;
}

export class LedgerService {
    static async createEntries(
        user: LedgerUserContext,
        entries: LedgerEntryInput[],
        session?: mongoose.ClientSession
    ) {
        // 1. Validate user
        if (!user.userId) {
            throw new Error(
                "Authenticated user is required"
            );
        }

        // 2. Validate tenant
        if (!user.tenantId) {
            throw new Error(
                "Tenant context is required"
            );
        }

        // 3. Validate entries
        if (
            !Array.isArray(entries) ||
            entries.length < 2
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "At least two ledger entries are required"
            );
        }

        // 4. Get transaction ID
        const transactionId =
            entries[0].transactionId;

        if (
            !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                transactionId
            )
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid transaction ID"
            );
        }

        // 5. Get transaction type
        const transactionType =
            entries[0].transactionType;

        // 6. Validate every entry
        for (const entry of entries) {
            if (
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    entry.transactionId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid transaction ID"
                );
            }

            if (
                entry.transactionId !==
                transactionId
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "All ledger entries must belong to the same transaction"
                );
            }

            if (
                entry.transactionType !==
                transactionType
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "All ledger entries must have the same transaction type"
                );
            }

            if (
                typeof entry.amount !== "number" ||
                !Number.isFinite(entry.amount) ||
                entry.amount <= 0
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Ledger amount must be greater than zero"
                );
            }

            if (
                entry.entryType !== "debit" &&
                entry.entryType !== "credit"
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid ledger entry type"
                );
            }

            if (
                entry.customerId &&
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    entry.customerId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid customer ID"
                );
            }

            if (
                entry.supplierId &&
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    entry.supplierId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid supplier ID"
                );
            }

            if (
                entry.referenceId &&
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    entry.referenceId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid reference ID"
                );
            }
        }

        // 7. Calculate Debit and Credit totals
        let totalDebit = 0;
        let totalCredit = 0;

        for (const entry of entries) {
            if (entry.entryType === "debit") {
                totalDebit += entry.amount;
            }

            if (entry.entryType === "credit") {
                totalCredit += entry.amount;
            }
        }

        // 8. Make sure the ledger is balanced
        if (
            Math.abs(
                totalDebit - totalCredit
            ) > 0.000001
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                `Ledger is not balanced. Debit: ${totalDebit}, Credit: ${totalCredit}`
            );
        }

        // 9. Prepare MongoDB documents
        const documents = entries.map(
            (entry) => ({
                tenantId: user.tenantId,

                transactionId:
                    new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                        entry.transactionId
                    ),

                transactionType:
                    entry.transactionType,

                account: entry.account,

                entryType:
                    entry.entryType,

                amount: entry.amount,

                customerId:
                    entry.customerId
                        ? new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                            entry.customerId
                        )
                        : undefined,

                supplierId:
                    entry.supplierId
                        ? new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                            entry.supplierId
                        )
                        : undefined,

                referenceId:
                    entry.referenceId
                        ? new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                            entry.referenceId
                        )
                        : undefined,

                description:
                    entry.description,

                entryDate:
                    entry.entryDate ??
                    new Date(),

                createdBy:
                    user.userId,
            })
        );

        // 10. Save using existing transaction session
        if (session) {
            return await LedgerEntry.create(documents, {
                session,
                ordered: true,
            });
        }

        // 11. Save normally if no transaction exists
        return await LedgerEntry.create(
            documents
        );
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\services\PaymentService.ts ==========

import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { Payment } from "@/lib/models/Payment";
import { Sale } from "@/lib/models/Sale";
import { Purchase } from "@/lib/models/Purchase";
import { SalesReturn } from "@/lib/models/SalesReturn";
import { PurchaseReturn } from "@/lib/models/PurchaseReturn";

import { LedgerService } from "@/lib/services/LedgerService";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";

export interface PaymentUserContext {
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
}

export interface CreatePaymentInput {
    paymentNo: string;
    type:
    | "receipt"
    | "payment"
    | "sales_refund"
    | "purchase_refund";
    amount: number;
    paymentMethod:
    | "cash"
    | "upi"
    | "card"
    | "bank"
    | "credit"
    | "other";
    paymentDate?: Date | string;
    customerId?: string;
    supplierId?: string;
    saleId?: string;
    purchaseId?: string;
    salesReturnId?: string;
    purchaseReturnId?: string;
    referenceNo?: string;
