                            !Number.isFinite(
                                item.unitPrice
                            ) ||
                            item.unitPrice < 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Invalid unit price for product ${item.productId}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Find product
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const product =
                            await Product.findOne({
                                _id: item.productId,
                                tenantId: user.tenantId,
                                status: "active",
                            }).session(session);

                        if (!product) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Product not found or inactive: ${item.productId}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Find unit
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const unit =
                            await Unit.findOne({
                                _id: product.unitId,
                                tenantId: user.tenantId,
                                status: "active",
                            }).session(session);

                        if (!unit) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Unit not found for product ${product.name}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Discount
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const discountRate =
                            item.discountRate ?? 0;

                        if (
                            typeof discountRate !== "number" ||
                            !Number.isFinite(
                                discountRate
                            ) ||
                            discountRate < 0 ||
                            discountRate > 100
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Invalid discount rate for ${product.name}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Gross amount
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const grossAmount =
                            item.quantity *
                            item.unitPrice;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Discount amount
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const discountAmount =
                            (grossAmount *
                                discountRate) /
                            100;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Taxable amount
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const taxableAmount =
                            grossAmount -
                            discountAmount;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Tax
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const taxRate =
                            item.taxRate ?? 0;

                        if (
                            typeof taxRate !== "number" ||
                            !Number.isFinite(
                                taxRate
                            ) ||
                            taxRate < 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Invalid tax rate for ${product.name}`
                            );
                        }

                        const taxAmount =
                            (taxableAmount *
                                taxRate) /
                            100;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Line total
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const lineTotal =
                            taxableAmount +
                            taxAmount;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Update totals
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        subtotal += grossAmount;

                        discountTotal +=
                            discountAmount;

                        taxTotal += taxAmount;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Purchase item
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        purchaseItems.push({
                            productId: product._id,

                            productSnapshot: {
                                name: product.name,
                                SKU: product.SKU,
                                unit: unit.shortName,
                            },

                            quantity:
                                item.quantity,

                            unitPrice:
                                item.unitPrice,

                            discountRate,

                            discountAmount,

                            tax: {
                                rate: taxRate,
                                amount: taxAmount,
                            },

                            total: lineTotal,
                        });
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 11. Shipping
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const shippingCost =
                        input.shippingCost ?? 0;

                    if (
                        typeof shippingCost !== "number" ||
                        !Number.isFinite(
                            shippingCost
                        ) ||
                        shippingCost < 0
                    ) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Invalid shipping cost"
                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 12. Calculate grand total
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const grandTotal =
                        subtotal -
                        discountTotal +
                        taxTotal +
                        shippingCost;

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 13. Paid amount
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const paidAmount =
                        input.paidAmount ?? 0;

                    if (
                        typeof paidAmount !== "number" ||
                        !Number.isFinite(
                            paidAmount
                        ) ||
                        paidAmount < 0
                    ) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Invalid paid amount"
                        );
                    }

                    if (
                        paidAmount > grandTotal
                    ) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Paid amount cannot exceed grandTotal"
                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 14. Due amount
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const dueAmount =
                        grandTotal -
                        paidAmount;

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 15. Payment status
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    let paymentStatus:
                        | "unpaid"
                        | "partial"
                        | "paid";

                    if (
                        paidAmount === 0
                    ) {
                        paymentStatus =
                            "unpaid";
                    } else if (
                        paidAmount < grandTotal
                    ) {
                        paymentStatus =
                            "partial";
                    } else {
                        paymentStatus =
                            "paid";
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 16. Create purchase
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const purchase =
                        new Purchase({

                            tenantId:
                                user.tenantId,

                            // Offline operation identifier
                            operationId:
                                normalizedOperationId,

                            purchaseNo:
                                input.purchaseNo.trim(),

                            supplierId:
                                supplier._id,

                            supplierSnapshot: {
                                name:
                                    supplier.name,

                                supplierCode:
                                    supplier.supplierCode,

                                phone:
                                    supplier.contact?.phone,

                                gstin:
                                    supplier.tax?.gstin,
                            },

                            purchaseDate:
                                input.purchaseDate ??
                                new Date(),

                            supplierInvoiceNo:
                                input.supplierInvoiceNo,

                            supplierInvoiceDate:
                                input.supplierInvoiceDate,

                            dueDate:
                                input.dueDate,

                            warehouse: {
                                name:
                                    input.warehouse.name.trim(),

                                location:
                                    input.warehouse.location?.trim(),
                            },

                            items:
                                purchaseItems,

                            subtotal,

                            discountTotal,

                            taxTotal,

                            shippingCost,

                            grandTotal,

                            paidAmount,

                            dueAmount,

                            paymentStatus,

                            status:
                                "received",

                            notes:
                                input.notes,

                            createdBy:
                                user.userId,
                        });

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 17. Save purchase
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    await purchase.save({
                        session,
                    });

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 18. Increase stock
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    for (
                        const item of purchaseItems
                    ) {

                        await StockService.changeStock(
                            user,

                            {
                                productId:
                                    item.productId.toString(),

                                // Purchase increases stock
                                quantity:
                                    item.quantity,

                                type:
                                    "purchase",

                                warehouse: {
                                    name:
                                        input.warehouse.name.trim(),

                                    location:
                                        input.warehouse.location?.trim(),
                                },

                                referenceId:
                                    purchase._id.toString(),

                                reason:
                                    `Purchase ${purchase.purchaseNo}`,
                            },

                            session
                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 19. Create audit log
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                        {
                            userId:
                                user.userId,

                            tenantId:
                                user.tenantId,
                        },

                        {
                            action:
                                "CREATE",

                            entity:
                                "Purchase",

                            entityId:
                                purchase._id,

                            description:
                                `Purchase ${purchase.purchaseNo} created`,

                            newValues: {
                                purchaseNo:
                                    purchase.purchaseNo,

                                supplierId:
                                    purchase.supplierId,

                                grandTotal:
                                    purchase.grandTotal,

                                paidAmount:
                                    purchase.paidAmount,

                                dueAmount:
                                    purchase.dueAmount,

                                paymentStatus:
                                    purchase.paymentStatus,

                                status:
                                    purchase.status,
                            },
                        },

                        session
                    );

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 20. Store created purchase
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    createdPurchase =
                        purchase;
                }
            );

            return createdPurchase;

        } catch (error: unknown) {

            /*
             * Concurrent idempotency protection.
             *
             * If two identical operations arrive at the same time,
             * the unique tenantId + operationId index prevents
             * duplicate purchases.
             *
             * The transaction that loses the race is rolled back.
             * We then return the already-created purchase.
             */

            const mongoError =
                error as mongoose.mongo.MongoServerError;

            if (
                normalizedOperationId &&
                mongoError?.code === 11000
            ) {

                const existingOperationPurchase =
                    await Purchase.findOne({
                        tenantId:
                            user.tenantId,

                        operationId:
                            normalizedOperationId,
                    });

                if (
                    existingOperationPurchase
                ) {
                    return existingOperationPurchase;
                }
            }

            throw error;

        } finally {

            await session.endSession();
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // GET ONE PURCHASE
    // ============================================================

    static async getPurchase(
        user: PurchaseUserContext,
        purchaseId: string
    ) {

        if (
            !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                purchaseId
            )
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid purchase ID"
            );
        }

        const purchase =
            await Purchase.findOne({
                _id: purchaseId,
                tenantId: user.tenantId,
            });

        if (!purchase) {
            throw new Error(
                "Purchase not found"
            );
        }

        return purchase;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // GET PURCHASES
    // ============================================================

    static async getPurchases(
        user: PurchaseUserContext,
        options?: {
            page?: number;
            limit?: number;

            supplierId?: string;

            paymentStatus?:
            | "unpaid"
            | "partial"
            | "paid";

            status?:
            | "draft"
            | "received"
            | "cancelled";
        }
    ) {

        const page =
            Math.max(
                options?.page ?? 1,
                1
            );

        const limit =
            Math.min(
                Math.max(
                    options?.limit ?? 20,
                    1
                ),
                100
            );

        const filter: Record<
            string,
            unknown
        > = {
            tenantId:
                user.tenantId,
        };

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Supplier filter
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            options?.supplierId
        ) {

            if (
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    options.supplierId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid supplier ID"
                );
            }

            filter.supplierId =
                new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                    options.supplierId
                );
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Payment status
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            options?.paymentStatus
        ) {

            filter.paymentStatus =
                options.paymentStatus;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Purchase status
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            options?.status
        ) {

            filter.status =
                options.status;
        }

        const skip =
            (page - 1) *
            limit;

        const [
            purchases,
            total,
        ] = await Promise.all([

            Purchase.find(
                filter
            )
                .sort({
                    purchaseDate: -1,
                    _id: -1,
                })
                .skip(skip)
                .limit(limit)
                .lean(),

            Purchase.countDocuments(
                filter
            ),
        ]);

        return {

            purchases,

            pagination: {

                page,

                limit,

                total,

                totalPages:
                    Math.ceil(
                        total / limit
                    ),
            },
        };
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\services\SalesReturnService.ts ==========
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import {
    Sale,
} from "@/lib/models/Sale";

import {
    SalesReturn,
    ISalesReturn,
} from "@/lib/models/SalesReturn";

import {
    Product,
} from "@/lib/models/Product";

import {
    Unit,
} from "@/lib/models/Unit";

import {
    StockService,
} from "@/lib/services/StockService";

import {
    AuditLogService,
} from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";

/* =========================================================
   TYPES
========================================================= */

export interface SalesReturnUserContext {
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
}

export interface SalesReturnItemInput {
    productId: string;
    quantity: number;
}

export interface CreateSalesReturnInput {
    salesReturnNo: string;

    saleId: string;

    customerId?: string;

    returnDate?: string | Date;

    warehouse: {
        name: string;
        location?: string;
    };

    items: SalesReturnItemInput[];

    refundAmount?: number;

    refundStatus?:
    | "pending"
    | "refunded"
    | "not_applicable";

    reason?: string;

    notes?: string;
}

/* =========================================================
   SERVICE
========================================================= */

export class SalesReturnService {

    /* =====================================================
       CREATE SALES RETURN
    ===================================================== */

    static async createSalesReturn(
        user: SalesReturnUserContext,
        input: CreateSalesReturnInput,
        operationId?: string
    ) {

        /* =================================================
           1. VALIDATE USER
        ================================================= */

        if (!user.userId) {
            throw new Error(
                "Authenticated user is required"
            );
        }

        if (!user.tenantId) {
            throw new Error(
                "Tenant context is required"
            );
        }

        /* =================================================
           2. NORMALIZE OPERATION ID
        ================================================= */

        const normalizedOperationId =
            operationId?.trim() || undefined;

        /* =================================================
           3. FIRST IDEMPOTENCY CHECK
        ================================================= */

        if (normalizedOperationId) {

            const existingByOperation =
                await SalesReturn.findOne({
                    tenantId:
                        user.tenantId,

                    operationId:
                        normalizedOperationId,
                });

            if (existingByOperation) {
                return existingByOperation;
            }
        }

        /* =================================================
           4. VALIDATE SALES RETURN NUMBER
        ================================================= */

        if (!input.salesReturnNo?.trim()) {
            throw new Error(
                "Sales return number is required"
            );
        }

        /* =================================================
           5. VALIDATE SALE ID
        ================================================= */

        if (
            !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                input.saleId
            )
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid sale ID"
            );
        }

        /* =================================================
           6. VALIDATE WAREHOUSE
        ================================================= */

        if (
            !input.warehouse?.name?.trim()
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Warehouse name is required"
            );
        }

        /* =================================================
           7. VALIDATE ITEMS
        ================================================= */

        if (
            !Array.isArray(input.items) ||
            input.items.length === 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "At least one return item is required"
            );
        }

        /* =================================================
           8. CHECK DUPLICATE RETURN NUMBER
        ================================================= */

        const existingReturn =
            await SalesReturn.findOne({
                tenantId:
                    user.tenantId,

                salesReturnNo:
                    input.salesReturnNo.trim(),
            });

        if (existingReturn) {
            throw new Error(
                "Sales return number already exists"
            );
        }

        /* =================================================
           9. FIND ORIGINAL SALE
        ================================================= */

        const sale =
            await Sale.findOne({
                _id:
                    input.saleId,

                tenantId:
                    user.tenantId,

                status:
                    "completed",
            });

        if (!sale) {
            throw new Error(
                "Original sale not found or not completed"
            );
        }

        /* =================================================
           10. VALIDATE CUSTOMER
        ================================================= */

        if (input.customerId) {

            if (
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    input.customerId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid customer ID"
                );
            }

            if (
                !sale.customerId ||
                sale.customerId.toString() !==
                input.customerId
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Customer does not match the original sale"
                );
            }
        }

        /* =================================================
           11. FIND PREVIOUS RETURNS
        ================================================= */

        const previousReturns =
            await SalesReturn.find({
                tenantId:
                    user.tenantId,

                saleId:
                    sale._id,

                status:
                    "completed",
            }).lean();

        /* =================================================
           12. CALCULATE RETURNED QUANTITY
        ================================================= */

        const alreadyReturnedMap =
            new Map<string, number>();

        for (
            const previousReturn
            of previousReturns
        ) {

            for (
                const item
                of previousReturn.items
            ) {

                const productId =
                    item.productId.toString();

                const current =
                    alreadyReturnedMap.get(
                        productId
                    ) ?? 0;

                alreadyReturnedMap.set(
                    productId,
                    current +
                    item.quantity
                );
            }
        }

        /* =================================================
           13. VALIDATE CURRENT ITEMS
        ================================================= */

        const currentProductIds =
            new Set<string>();

        for (
            const item
            of input.items
        ) {

            if (
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    item.productId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Invalid product ID: ${item.productId}`
                );
            }

            if (
                typeof item.quantity !==
                "number" ||
                !Number.isFinite(
                    item.quantity
                ) ||
                item.quantity <= 0
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Return quantity must be greater than zero"
                );
            }

            if (
                currentProductIds.has(
                    item.productId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Product ${item.productId} appears more than once`
                );
            }

            currentProductIds.add(
                item.productId
            );
        }

        /* =================================================
           14. PREPARE RETURN ITEMS
        ================================================= */

        const returnItems: ISalesReturn["items"] =
            [];

        for (
            const inputItem
            of input.items
        ) {

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Find product in original sale
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const saleItem =
                sale.items.find(
                    (item) =>
                        item.productId.toString() ===
                        inputItem.productId
                );

            if (!saleItem) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Product ${inputItem.productId} was not part of the original sale`
                );
            }

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Previously returned quantity
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const previouslyReturned =
                alreadyReturnedMap.get(
                    inputItem.productId
                ) ?? 0;

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Available quantity
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const availableToReturn =
                saleItem.quantity -
                previouslyReturned;

            if (
                inputItem.quantity >
                availableToReturn
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Cannot return ${inputItem.quantity} units. Only ${availableToReturn} units are available to return for ${saleItem.productSnapshot.name}`
                );
            }

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Find current product
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const product =
                await Product.findOne({
                    _id:
                        inputItem.productId,

                    tenantId:
                        user.tenantId,

                    status:
                        "active",
                });

            if (!product) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Product ${inputItem.productId} not found or inactive`
                );
            }

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Find unit
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const unit =
                await Unit.findOne({
                    _id:
                        product.unitId,

                    tenantId:
                        user.tenantId,

                    status:
                        "active",
                });

            if (!unit) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unit not found for product ${product.name}`
                );
            }

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Original sale price
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const unitPrice =
                saleItem.unitPrice;

            const discountRate =
                saleItem.discountRate;

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Gross amount
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const grossAmount =
                inputItem.quantity *
                unitPrice;

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Discount
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const discountAmount =
                (
                    grossAmount *
                    discountRate
                ) / 100;

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Amount after discount
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const amountAfterDiscount =
                grossAmount -
                discountAmount;

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Tax
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const taxRate =
                saleItem.tax?.rate ??
                0;

            const taxAmount =
                (
                    amountAfterDiscount *
                    taxRate
                ) / 100;

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Total
            --------------------------------------------- */

            const total =
                amountAfterDiscount +
                taxAmount;

            /* ---------------------------------------------
               Add item
            --------------------------------------------- */

            returnItems.push({
                productId:
                    product._id,

                productSnapshot: {
                    name:
                        product.name,

                    SKU:
                        product.SKU,

                    unit:
                        unit.shortName,
                },

                quantity:
                    inputItem.quantity,

                unitPrice,

                discountRate,

                discountAmount,

                tax: {
                    rate:
                        taxRate,

                    amount:
                        taxAmount,
                },

                total,
            });
        }

        /* =================================================
           15. CALCULATE TOTALS
        ================================================= */

        const subtotal =
            returnItems.reduce(
                (
                    sum,
                    item
                ) =>
                    sum +
                    item.quantity *
                    item.unitPrice,
                0
            );

        const discountTotal =
            returnItems.reduce(
                (
                    sum,
                    item
                ) =>
                    sum +
                    item.discountAmount,
                0
            );

        const taxTotal =
            returnItems.reduce(
                (
                    sum,
                    item
                ) =>
                    sum +
                    item.tax.amount,
                0
            );

        const shippingCost = 0;

        /* =================================================
           16. GRAND TOTAL
        ================================================= */

        const grandTotal =
            subtotal -
            discountTotal +
            taxTotal +
            shippingCost;

        /* =================================================
           17. REFUND AMOUNT
        ================================================= */

        const refundAmount =
            input.refundAmount ??
            grandTotal;

        if (
            typeof refundAmount !==
            "number" ||
            !Number.isFinite(
                refundAmount
            ) ||
            refundAmount < 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid refund amount"
            );
        }

        if (
            refundAmount >
            grandTotal
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Refund amount cannot exceed return total"
            );
        }

        /* =================================================
           18. REFUND STATUS
        ================================================= */

        const refundStatus =
            input.refundStatus ??
            "not_applicable";

        /* =================================================
           19. START SESSION
        ================================================= */

        const session =
            await mongoose.startSession();

        try {

            let createdReturn:
                | mongoose.HydratedDocument<ISalesReturn>
                | undefined;

            /* =================================================
               20. TRANSACTION
            ================================================= */

            await session.withTransaction(
                async () => {

                    /* -----------------------------------------
                       SECOND IDEMPOTENCY CHECK
                    ----------------------------------------- */

                    if (
                        normalizedOperationId
                    ) {

                        const existingByOperation =
                            await SalesReturn.findOne({
                                tenantId:
                                    user.tenantId,

                                operationId:
                                    normalizedOperationId,
                            }).session(
                                session
                            );

                        if (
                            existingByOperation
                        ) {

                            createdReturn =
                                existingByOperation;

                            return;
                        }
                    }

                    /* -----------------------------------------
                       CREATE SALES RETURN
                    ----------------------------------------- */

                    const result =
                        await SalesReturn.create(
                            [
                                {
                                    tenantId:
                                        user.tenantId,

                                    /*
                                     * THIS IS THE FIELD THAT
                                     * PREVIOUSLY CAUSED YOUR
                                     * TYPESCRIPT ERROR.
                                     */
                                    operationId:
                                        normalizedOperationId,

                                    salesReturnNo:
                                        input
                                            .salesReturnNo
                                            .trim(),

                                    saleId:
                                        sale._id,

                                    customerId:
                                        sale.customerId ??
                                        undefined,

                                    customerSnapshot:
                                        sale.customerSnapshot
                                            ? {
                                                name:
                                                    sale
                                                        .customerSnapshot
                                                        .name,

                                                phone:
                                                    sale
                                                        .customerSnapshot
                                                        .phone,

                                                email:
                                                    sale
                                                        .customerSnapshot
                                                        .email,
                                            }
                                            : undefined,

                                    returnDate:
                                        input.returnDate
                                            ? new Date(
                                                input.returnDate
                                            )
                                            : new Date(),

                                    warehouse: {
                                        name:
                                            input
                                                .warehouse
                                                .name
                                                .trim(),

                                        location:
                                            input
                                                .warehouse
                                                .location
                                                ?.trim(),
                                    },

                                    items:
                                        returnItems,

                                    subtotal,

                                    discountTotal,

                                    taxTotal,

                                    shippingCost,

                                    grandTotal,

                                    refundAmount,

                                    refundStatus,

                                    status:
                                        "completed",

                                    reason:
                                        input
                                            .reason
                                            ?.trim(),

                                    notes:
                                        input
                                            .notes
                                            ?.trim(),

                                    createdBy:
                                        user.userId,
                                },
                            ],
                            {
                                session,
                            }
                        );

                    /* -----------------------------------------
                       GET CREATED DOCUMENT
                    ----------------------------------------- */

                    const newReturn =
                        result[0];

                    createdReturn =
                        newReturn;

                    /* -----------------------------------------
                       INCREASE STOCK
                    ----------------------------------------- */

                    for (
                        const item
                        of input.items
                    ) {

                        await StockService.changeStock(
                            user,
                            {
                                productId:
                                    item.productId,

                                quantity:
                                    item.quantity,

                                type:
                                    "sales_return",

                                warehouse: {
                                    name:
                                        input
                                            .warehouse
                                            .name
                                            .trim(),

                                    location:
                                        input
                                            .warehouse
                                            .location
                                            ?.trim(),
                                },

                                referenceId:
                                    newReturn
                                        ._id
                                        .toString(),

                                reason:
                                    `Sales Return ${newReturn.salesReturnNo}`,
                            },
                            session
                        );
                    }

                    /* -----------------------------------------
                       AUDIT LOG
                    ----------------------------------------- */

                    await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                        {
                            userId:
                                user.userId,

                            tenantId:
                                user.tenantId,
                        },
                        {
                            action:
                                "CREATE",

                            entity:
                                "SalesReturn",

                            entityId:
                                newReturn._id,

                            description:
                                `Sales Return ${newReturn.salesReturnNo} created`,

                            newValues: {
                                salesReturnNo:
                                    newReturn
                                        .salesReturnNo,

                                operationId:
                                    newReturn
                                        .operationId,

                                saleId:
                                    newReturn
                                        .saleId,

                                customerId:
                                    newReturn
                                        .customerId,

                                grandTotal:
                                    newReturn
                                        .grandTotal,

                                refundAmount:
                                    newReturn
                                        .refundAmount,

                                refundStatus:
                                    newReturn
                                        .refundStatus,

                                status:
                                    newReturn
                                        .status,

                                reason:
                                    newReturn
                                        .reason,
                            },
                        },
                        session
                    );
                }
            );

            /* =================================================
               21. SAFETY CHECK
            ================================================= */

            if (!createdReturn) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Sales return creation failed"
                );
            }

            return createdReturn;

        } catch (error: unknown) {

            /* =================================================
               22. DUPLICATE OPERATION ID
            ================================================= */

            const mongoError =
                error as {
                    code?: number;
                };

            if (
                normalizedOperationId &&
                mongoError.code === 11000
            ) {

                const existingByOperation =
                    await SalesReturn.findOne({
                        tenantId:
                            user.tenantId,

                        operationId:
                            normalizedOperationId,
                    });

                if (
                    existingByOperation
                ) {
                    return existingByOperation;
                }
            }

            throw error;

        } finally {

            await session.endSession();
        }
    }

    /* =====================================================
       GET ONE SALES RETURN
    ===================================================== */

    static async getSalesReturn(
        user: SalesReturnUserContext,
        salesReturnId: string
    ) {

        if (
            !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                salesReturnId
            )
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid sales return ID"
            );
        }

        const salesReturn =
            await SalesReturn.findOne({
                _id:
                    salesReturnId,

                tenantId:
                    user.tenantId,
            }).lean();

        if (!salesReturn) {
            throw new Error(
                "Sales return not found"
            );
        }

        return salesReturn;
    }

    /* =====================================================
       GET SALES RETURNS
    ===================================================== */

    static async getSalesReturns(
        user: SalesReturnUserContext,
        options?: {
            saleId?: string;

            customerId?: string;

            status?:
            | "draft"
            | "completed"
            | "cancelled";

            page?: number;

            limit?: number;
        }
    ) {

        const page =
            Math.max(
                options?.page ?? 1,
                1
            );

        const limit =
            Math.min(
                Math.max(
                    options?.limit ?? 20,
                    1
                ),
                100
            );

        const filter: Record<
            string,
            unknown
        > = {
            tenantId:
                user.tenantId,
        };

        /* ---------------------------------------------
           Sale filter
        --------------------------------------------- */

        if (options?.saleId) {

            if (
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    options.saleId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid sale ID"
                );
            }

            filter.saleId =
                new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                    options.saleId
                );
        }

        /* ---------------------------------------------
           Customer filter
        --------------------------------------------- */

        if (options?.customerId) {

            if (
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    options.customerId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid customer ID"
                );
            }

            filter.customerId =
                new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                    options.customerId
                );
        }

        /* ---------------------------------------------
           Status filter
        --------------------------------------------- */

        if (options?.status) {
            filter.status =
                options.status;
        }

        /* ---------------------------------------------
           Pagination
        --------------------------------------------- */

        const skip =
            (page - 1) *
            limit;

        /* ---------------------------------------------
           Query
        --------------------------------------------- */

        const [
            salesReturns,
            total,
        ] = await Promise.all([

            SalesReturn.find(filter)
                .sort({
                    returnDate: -1,
                    _id: -1,
                })
                .skip(skip)
                .limit(limit)
                .lean(),

            SalesReturn.countDocuments(
                filter
            ),
        ]);

        return {
            salesReturns,

            pagination: {
                page,

                limit,

                total,

                totalPages:
                    Math.ceil(
                        total / limit
                    ),
            },
        };
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\services\SalesService.ts ==========
import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { Sale } from "@/lib/models/Sale";
import { Product } from "@/lib/models/Product";
import { Customer } from "@/lib/models/Customer";
import { Unit } from "@/lib/models/Unit";
import { StockService } from "@/lib/services/StockService";
import { LedgerService } from "@/lib/services/LedgerService";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";
import {
    BusinessPermissionError,
    SALE_DATE_RULES,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";

export interface SalesUserContext {
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    // Device of the authenticated session (recorded in the audit log).
    deviceId?: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
}

/**
 * What the caller is allowed to do beyond a plain sale. Computed from the
 * user's CURRENT permissions by the caller (route or sync processing).
 * When omitted, the strictest policy applies.
 */
export interface SalePolicy {
    // sales.priceOverride: a price other than the selling price, or a discount.
    allowPriceOverride: boolean;
    // sales.backdate: a sale date older than referenceTime - maxAgeMs.
    allowBackdate: boolean;
    // Online: now. Offline sync: when the server first received the operation.
    referenceTime: Date;
    maxAgeMs: number;
}

function strictSalePolicy(): SalePolicy {
    return {
        allowPriceOverride: false,
        allowBackdate: false,
        referenceTime: new Date(),
        maxAgeMs: SALE_DATE_RULES.onlineMaxAgeMs,
    };
}

/** Sale date after the date rules; throws when the date is not allowed. */
function resolveSaleDate(
    value: unknown,
    policy: SalePolicy,
    now: Date
): { saleDate: Date; backdated: boolean } {
    if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") {
        return { saleDate: now, backdated: false };
    }

    const saleDate =
        value instanceof Date || typeof value === "string"
            ? new Date(value)
            : new Date(Number.NaN);

    if (!Number.isFinite(saleDate.getTime())) {
        throw new Error("Invalid sale date");
    }

    // Never in the future (beyond a small clock difference), whatever the
    // user's permissions.
    if (saleDate.getTime() > now.getTime() + SALE_DATE_RULES.maxFutureMs) {
        throw new Error("Sale date cannot be in the future");
    }

    const oldestAllowed = policy.referenceTime.getTime() - policy.maxAgeMs;
    const backdated = saleDate.getTime() < oldestAllowed;

    if (backdated && !policy.allowBackdate) {
        throw new BusinessPermissionError(
            "This sale date is too far in the past; it requires the sales.backdate permission"
        );
    }

    return { saleDate, backdated };
}

/** True for a duplicate-key error on the tenant's invoice-number index. */
function isDuplicateInvoiceError(error: unknown): boolean {
    const mongoError = error as { code?: unknown; keyPattern?: Record<string, unknown> } | null;
    return mongoError?.code === 11000 && Boolean(mongoError.keyPattern?.invoiceNo);
}

export interface SaleItemInput {
    productId: string;
    quantity: number;
    unitPrice?: number;
    discountRate?: number;
    taxRate?: number;
}

export interface CreateSaleInput {
    invoiceNo: string;

    customerId?: string;

    saleDate?: Date;

    warehouse: {
        name: string;
        location?: string;
    };

    items: SaleItemInput[];

    paidAmount?: number;

    paymentMethod?:
    | "cash"
    | "bank"
    | "upi"
    | "card"
    | "credit";

    notes?: string;
}

export class SalesService {
    // ============================================================
    // CREATE SALE
    // ============================================================

    static async createSale(
        user: SalesUserContext,
        input: CreateSaleInput,
        operationId?: string,
        policy: SalePolicy = strictSalePolicy()
    ) {
        const session =
            await mongoose.startSession();

        // Invoice numbers are stored upper-case and trimmed (schema), and
        // are unique per tenant (database index tenantId + invoiceNo).
        const invoiceNo =
            typeof input.invoiceNo === "string"
                ? input.invoiceNo.trim().toUpperCase()
                : "";

        /*
         * operationId is supplied by SyncService when the sale
         * comes from an offline synchronization operation.
         *
         * Online sales normally do not have an operationId.
         */
        const normalizedOperationId =
            operationId?.trim() || undefined;

        try {
            let createdSale = null;

            await session.withTransaction(
                async () => {
                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 1. Validate user and tenant
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    if (!user.userId) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Authenticated user is required"
                        );
                    }

                    if (!user.tenantId) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Tenant context is required"
                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 2. Validate invoice number
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    if (!invoiceNo) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Invoice number is required"
                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 3. Validate warehouse
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    if (!input.warehouse?.name?.trim()) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Warehouse name is required"
                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 4. Validate sale items
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    if (
                        !Array.isArray(input.items) ||
                        input.items.length === 0
                    ) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "At least one sale item is required"
                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 5. IDEMPOTENCY CHECK
                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    //
                    // If this exact offline operation has already
                    // created a sale, return that existing sale.
                    //
                    // This is what makes retrying the same operation safe.
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    if (normalizedOperationId) {
                        const existingOperationSale =
                            await Sale.findOne({
                                tenantId: user.tenantId,
                                operationId:
                                    normalizedOperationId,
                            }).session(session);

                        if (existingOperationSale) {
                            createdSale =
                                existingOperationSale;

                            return;
                        }
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 5b. Sale date rules (future dates, backdating)
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const { saleDate, backdated } =
                        resolveSaleDate(
                            input.saleDate,
                            policy,
                            new Date()
                        );

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 6. Check duplicate invoice
                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    //
                    // The unique index (tenantId + invoiceNo) also rejects
                    // a concurrent duplicate; see the catch block below.

                    const existingInvoice =
                        await Sale.findOne({
                            tenantId: user.tenantId,
                            invoiceNo,
                        }).session(session);

                    if (existingInvoice) {
                        throw new Error(
                            `Invoice ${invoiceNo} already exists`
                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 7. Find customer
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    let customer = null;

                    if (input.customerId) {
                        if (
                            !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                                input.customerId
                            )
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Invalid customer ID"
                            );
                        }

                        customer =
                            await Customer.findOne({
                                _id: input.customerId,
                                tenantId:
                                    user.tenantId,
                                status: "active",
                            }).session(session);

                        if (!customer) {
                            throw new Error(
                                "Customer not found or inactive"
                            );
                        }
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 8. Prepare sale items
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const saleItems = [];

                    let subtotal = 0;
                    let discountTotal = 0;
                    let taxTotal = 0;
                    let priceOverridden = false;

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 9. Process each product
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    for (const item of input.items) {
                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Product ID validation
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        if (
                            !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                                item.productId
                            )
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Invalid product ID: ${item.productId}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Quantity validation
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        if (
                            typeof item.quantity !== "number" ||
                            !Number.isFinite(
                                item.quantity
                            ) ||
                            item.quantity <= 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Invalid quantity for product ${item.productId}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Find product
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const product =
                            await Product.findOne({
                                _id: item.productId,
                                tenantId:
                                    user.tenantId,
                                status: "active",
                            }).session(session);

                        if (!product) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Product not found or inactive: ${item.productId}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Find unit
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const unit =
                            await Unit.findOne({
                                _id: product.unitId,
                                tenantId:
                                    user.tenantId,
                                status: "active",
                            }).session(session);

                        if (!unit) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Unit not found for product ${product.name}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Unit price
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const unitPrice =
                            item.unitPrice ??
                            product.sellingPrice;

                        if (
                            typeof unitPrice !== "number" ||
                            !Number.isFinite(
                                unitPrice
                            ) ||
                            unitPrice < 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Invalid price for ${product.name}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Discount
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const discountRate =
                            item.discountRate ?? 0;

                        if (
                            typeof discountRate !==
                            "number" ||
                            !Number.isFinite(
                                discountRate
                            ) ||
                            discountRate < 0 ||
                            discountRate > 100
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Invalid discount rate for ${product.name}`
                            );
                        }

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Price override / discount permission
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const lineOverridesPrice =
                            Math.abs(unitPrice - product.sellingPrice) > 1e-9 ||
                            discountRate > 0;

                        if (
                            lineOverridesPrice &&
                            !policy.allowPriceOverride
                        ) {
                            throw new BusinessPermissionError(
                                `Changing the price of ${product.name} or giving a discount requires the sales.priceOverride permission`
                            );
                        }

                        priceOverridden ||= lineOverridesPrice;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Calculate gross amount
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const grossAmount =
                            item.quantity *
                            unitPrice;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Calculate discount
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const discountAmount =
                            (grossAmount *
                                discountRate) /
                            100;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Calculate taxable amount
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const taxableAmount =
                            grossAmount -
                            discountAmount;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Tax
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const taxRate =
                            item.taxRate ?? 0;

                        if (
                            typeof taxRate !== "number" ||
                            !Number.isFinite(
                                taxRate
                            ) ||
                            taxRate < 0
                        ) {
                            throw new Error(
                                `Invalid tax rate for ${product.name}`
                            );
                        }

                        const taxAmount =
                            (taxableAmount *
                                taxRate) /
                            100;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Line total
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        const lineTotal =
                            taxableAmount +
                            taxAmount;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Add totals
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        subtotal +=
                            grossAmount;

                        discountTotal +=
                            discountAmount;

                        taxTotal +=
                            taxAmount;

                        // ------------------------------------------------
                        // Create sale item
                        // ------------------------------------------------

                        saleItems.push({
                            productId:
                                product._id,

                            productSnapshot: {
                                name:
                                    product.name,

                                SKU:
                                    product.SKU,

                                unit:
                                    unit.shortName,
                            },

                            quantity:
                                item.quantity,

                            unitPrice,

                            discountRate,

                            discountAmount,

                            tax: {
                                rate:
                                    taxRate,

                                amount:
                                    taxAmount,
                            },

                            total:
                                lineTotal,
                        });
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 10. Additional charges
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const shippingCost = 0;

                    const otherCharges = 0;

                    const roundOff = 0;

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 11. Calculate grand total
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const grandTotal =
                        subtotal -
                        discountTotal +
                        taxTotal +
                        shippingCost +
                        otherCharges +
                        roundOff;

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 12. Validate paid amount
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const paidAmount =
                        input.paidAmount ?? 0;

                    if (
                        typeof paidAmount !==
                        "number" ||
                        !Number.isFinite(
                            paidAmount
                        ) ||
                        paidAmount < 0
                    ) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Invalid paid amount"
                        );
                    }

                    if (
                        paidAmount >
                        grandTotal
                    ) {
                        throw new Error(
                            "Paid amount cannot exceed grandTotal"
                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 13. Calculate due amount
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const dueAmount =
                        grandTotal -
                        paidAmount;

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 14. Payment status
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    let paymentStatus:
                        | "unpaid"
                        | "partial"
                        | "paid";

                    if (
                        paidAmount === 0
                    ) {
                        paymentStatus =
                            "unpaid";
                    } else if (
                        paidAmount <
                        grandTotal
                    ) {
                        paymentStatus =
                            "partial";
                    } else {
                        paymentStatus =
                            "paid";
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 15. Create sale document
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    const sale = new Sale({
                        tenantId:
                            user.tenantId,

                        /*
                         * IMPORTANT:
                         * Store the sync operation ID in the actual
                         * business transaction.
                         */
                        operationId:
                            normalizedOperationId,

                        invoiceNo,

                        customerId:
                            customer?._id,

                        customerSnapshot:
                            customer
                                ? {
                                    name:
                                        customer.name,

                                    phone:
                                        customer.phone,

                                    email:
                                        customer.email,
                                }
                                : undefined,

                        saleDate,

                        warehouse: {
                            name:
                                input.warehouse.name.trim(),

                            location:
                                input.warehouse.location?.trim(),
                        },

                        items:
                            saleItems,

                        subtotal,

                        discountTotal,

                        taxTotal,

                        shippingCost,

                        otherCharges,

                        roundOff,

                        grandTotal,

                        paidAmount,

                        dueAmount,

                        paymentStatus,

                        paymentMethod:
                            input.paymentMethod,

                        status:
                            "completed",

                        notes:
                            input.notes,

                        createdBy:
                            user.userId,
                    });

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 16. Save sale
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    await sale.save({
                        session,
                    });

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 17. Update stock
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    for (
                        const item of saleItems
                    ) {
                        await StockService.changeStock(
                            user,
                            {
                                productId:
                                    item.productId.toString(),

                                // Sale decreases stock
                                quantity:
                                    -item.quantity,

                                type:
                                    "sale",

                                warehouse: {
                                    name:
                                        input.warehouse.name.trim(),

                                    location:
                                        input.warehouse.location?.trim(),
                                },

                                referenceId:
                                    sale._id.toString(),

                                reason:
                                    `Sale ${sale.invoiceNo}`,
                            },
                            session
