                        );
                    }

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 18. Create ledger entries
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    await LedgerService.createEntries(
                        user,
                        [
                            {
                                transactionId:
                                    sale._id.toString(),

                                transactionType:
                                    "sale",

                                account:
                                    "accounts_receivable",

                                entryType:
                                    "debit",

                                amount:
                                    sale.grandTotal,

                                customerId:
                                    sale.customerId?.toString(),

                                description:
                                    `Sale receivable - ${sale.invoiceNo}`,

                                entryDate:
                                    sale.saleDate,
                            },

                            {
                                transactionId:
                                    sale._id.toString(),

                                transactionType:
                                    "sale",

                                account:
                                    "sales_revenue",

                                entryType:
                                    "credit",

                                amount:
                                    sale.grandTotal,

                                description:
                                    `Sales revenue - ${sale.invoiceNo}`,

                                entryDate:
                                    sale.saleDate,
                            },
                        ],
                        session
                    );

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 18b. Audit log (same transaction: if it fails, the
                    //      sale, stock and ledger changes roll back)
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                        {
                            userId: user.userId,
                            tenantId: user.tenantId,
                            deviceId: user.deviceId,
                        },
                        {
                            action: "CREATE",
                            entity: "Sale",
                            entityId: sale._id,
                            description:
                                `Sale ${sale.invoiceNo} created`,
                            newValues: {
                                invoiceNo: sale.invoiceNo,
                                saleDate: sale.saleDate,
                                grandTotal: sale.grandTotal,
                                paidAmount: sale.paidAmount,
                                paymentStatus: sale.paymentStatus,
                                itemCount: saleItems.length,
                                operationId: sale.operationId,
                                priceOverridden,
                                backdated,
                            },
                        },
                        session
                    );

                    // ----------------------------------------------------
                    // 19. Store created sale
                    // ----------------------------------------------------

                    createdSale =
                        sale;
                }
            );

            return createdSale;
        } catch (error: unknown) {
            /*
             * ----------------------------------------------------------
             * CONCURRENT IDEMPOTENCY PROTECTION
             * ----------------------------------------------------------
             *
             * Two sync requests could arrive at almost exactly the
             * same time.
             *
             * Both could initially see:
             *
             *     operationId = not found
             *
             * One transaction will successfully create the Sale.
             *
             * The second transaction will hit the unique
             * tenantId + operationId index.
             *
             * After its transaction is aborted, find the already
             * committed sale and return it.
             *
             * This prevents duplicate stock/ledger processing.
             * ----------------------------------------------------------
             */

            const mongoError =
                error as
                | mongoose.mongo.MongoServerError
                | undefined;

            if (
                normalizedOperationId &&
                mongoError?.code === 11000
            ) {
                const existingOperationSale =
                    await Sale.findOne({
                        tenantId:
                            user.tenantId,

                        operationId:
                            normalizedOperationId,
                    });

                if (
                    existingOperationSale
                ) {
                    return existingOperationSale;
                }
            }

            // A concurrent sale took this invoice number first.
            if (isDuplicateInvoiceError(error)) {
                throw new Error(
                    `Invoice ${invoiceNo} already exists`
                );
            }

            throw error;
        } finally {
            await session.endSession();
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // GET ONE SALE
    // ============================================================

    static async getSale(
        user: SalesUserContext,
        saleId: string
    ) {
        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Validate sale ID
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                saleId
            )
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Invalid sale ID"
            );
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Find sale
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        const sale =
            await Sale.findOne({
                _id: saleId,
                tenantId:
                    user.tenantId,
            });

        if (!sale) {
            throw new Error(
                "Sale not found"
            );
        }

        return sale;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // GET SALES
    // ============================================================

    static async getSales(
        user: SalesUserContext,
        options?: {
            page?: number;

            limit?: number;

            customerId?: string;

            paymentStatus?:
            | "unpaid"
            | "partial"
            | "paid";

            status?:
            | "draft"
            | "completed"
            | "cancelled";
        }
    ) {
        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Pagination
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        const page = Math.max(
            options?.page ?? 1,
            1
        );

        const limit = Math.min(
            Math.max(
                options?.limit ?? 20,
                1
            ),
            100
        );

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Base filter
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        const filter: Record<
            string,
            unknown
        > = {
            tenantId:
                user.tenantId,
        };

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Customer filter
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        if (options?.customerId) {
            if (
                !mongoose.isValidObjectId(
                    options.customerId
                )
            ) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Invalid customer ID"
                );
            }

            filter.customerId =
                new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                    options.customerId
                );
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Payment status filter
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            options?.paymentStatus
        ) {
            filter.paymentStatus =
                options.paymentStatus;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Sale status filter
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        if (options?.status) {
            filter.status =
                options.status;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Calculate pagination
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        const skip =
            (page - 1) * limit;

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Fetch sales + total
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        const [
            sales,
            total,
        ] = await Promise.all([
            Sale.find(filter)
                .sort({
                    saleDate: -1,
                    _id: -1,
                })
                .skip(skip)
                .limit(limit)
                .lean(),

            Sale.countDocuments(
                filter
            ),
        ]);

        // ----------------------------------------------------------
        // Return result
        // ----------------------------------------------------------

        return {
            sales,

            pagination: {
                page,

                limit,

                total,

                totalPages:
                    Math.ceil(
                        total / limit
                    ),
            },
        };
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\services\StockService.ts ==========

import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { Product } from "@/lib/models/Product";
import { StockMovement } from "@/lib/models/StockMovement";
import { AuditLogService } from "@/lib/services/AuditLogService";

export interface StockUserContext {
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
}

export interface StockChangeInput {
    productId: string;

    /**
     * Positive quantity = stock increase.
     * Negative quantity = stock decrease.
     */
    quantity: number;

    type:
    | "purchase"
    | "sale"
    | "purchase_return"
    | "sales_return"
    | "adjustment";

    warehouse: {
        name: string;
        location?: string;
    };

    referenceId?: string;
    reason?: string;
}

export class StockService {
    /**
     * Changes stock.
     *
     * If a caller supplies a session, its existing transaction
     * is reused. Otherwise, this method creates and owns a
     * MongoDB transaction.
     */
    static async changeStock(
        user: StockUserContext,
        input: StockChangeInput,
        session?: mongoose.ClientSession,
        operationId?: string
    ) {
        const normalizedOperationId =
            operationId?.trim() || undefined;

        // Reuse the transaction owned by SalesService,
        // PurchaseService, or another calling service.
        if (session) {
            return this.changeStockInSession(
                user,
                input,
                session,
                normalizedOperationId
            );
        }

        // Standalone calls get their own transaction.
        const ownSession = await mongoose.startSession();

        try {
            let result:
                | Awaited<
                    ReturnType<
                        typeof StockService.changeStockInSession
                    >
                >
                | undefined;

            await ownSession.withTransaction(async () => {
                result = await this.changeStockInSession(
                    user,
                    input,
                    ownSession,
                    normalizedOperationId
                );
            });

            if (!result) {
                throw new Error(
                    "Stock transaction completed without a result"
                );
            }

            return result;
        } catch (error: unknown) {
            // A concurrent request may have completed the
            // same adjustment first. Look up the movement
            // only after the transaction has failed.
            const mongoError = error as { code?: number };

            if (
                mongoError?.code === 11000 &&
                input.type === "adjustment" &&
                normalizedOperationId
            ) {
                const existingMovement =
                    await StockMovement.findOne({
                        tenantId: user.tenantId,
                        operationId: normalizedOperationId,
                        type: "adjustment",
                    });

                if (existingMovement) {
                    const product = await Product.findOne({
                        _id: existingMovement.productId,
                        tenantId: user.tenantId,
                    });

                    return {
                        product,
                        movement: existingMovement,
                        previousStock:
                            existingMovement.previousStock,
                        newStock: existingMovement.newStock,
                        quantityChanged:
                            existingMovement.quantity,
                        auditLog: undefined,
                        alreadyProcessed: true,
                    };
                }
            }

            throw error;
        } finally {
            await ownSession.endSession();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Performs the stock update, movement creation, and
     * audit logging inside the supplied transaction.
     */
    private static async changeStockInSession(
        user: StockUserContext,
        input: StockChangeInput,
        session: mongoose.ClientSession,
        operationId?: string
    ) {
        // 1. Validate authenticated context.
        if (!user.userId) {
            throw new Error("Authenticated user is required");
        }

        if (!user.tenantId) {
            throw new Error("Tenant context is required");
        }

        // 2. Validate product ID.
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(input.productId)) {
            throw new Error("Invalid product ID");
        }

        // 3. Validate quantity.
        if (
            typeof input.quantity !== "number" ||
            !Number.isFinite(input.quantity) ||
            input.quantity === 0
        ) {
            throw new Error(
                "Stock quantity must be a valid non-zero number"
            );
        }

        // 4. Validate movement type.
        const validTypes = [
            "purchase",
            "sale",
            "purchase_return",
            "sales_return",
            "adjustment",
        ] as const;

        if (
            !validTypes.includes(
                input.type as (typeof validTypes)[number]
            )
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid stock movement type");
        }

        // 5. Validate warehouse.
        if (!input.warehouse?.name?.trim()) {
            throw new Error("Warehouse name is required");
        }

        // 6. Validate reference ID.
        if (
            input.referenceId &&
            !mongoose.isValidObjectId(input.referenceId)
        ) {
            throw new Error("Invalid reference ID");
        }

        // 7. Idempotency check for adjustments.
        // A retry of the same operation must not change stock again.
        if (input.type === "adjustment" && operationId) {
            const existingMovement =
                await StockMovement.findOne({
                    tenantId: user.tenantId,
                    operationId,
                    type: "adjustment",
                }).session(session);

            if (existingMovement) {
                const existingProduct = await Product.findOne({
                    _id: existingMovement.productId,
                    tenantId: user.tenantId,
                }).session(session);

                return {
                    product: existingProduct,
                    movement: existingMovement,
                    previousStock:
                        existingMovement.previousStock,
                    newStock: existingMovement.newStock,
                    quantityChanged:
                        existingMovement.quantity,
                    auditLog: undefined,
                    alreadyProcessed: true,
                };
            }
        }

        // 8. Find the active product in this tenant only.
        const product = await Product.findOne({
            _id: input.productId,
            tenantId: user.tenantId,
            status: "active",
        }).session(session);

        if (!product) {
            throw new Error("Product not found or inactive");
        }

        // 9. Calculate stock change.
        const previousStock = product.stockQuantity;
        const newStock = previousStock + input.quantity;

        if (newStock < 0) {
            throw new Error(
                `Insufficient stock for product ${product.name}`
            );
        }

        // 10. Atomically update stock.
        // The stockQuantity condition prevents concurrent
        // decrements from taking stock below zero.
        const updatedProduct = await Product.findOneAndUpdate(
            {
                _id: product._id,
                tenantId: user.tenantId,
                status: "active",
                ...(input.quantity < 0
                    ? {
                        stockQuantity: {
                            $gte: Math.abs(input.quantity),
                        },
                    }
                    : {}),
            },
            {
                $inc: {
                    stockQuantity: input.quantity,
                },
            },
            {
                new: true,
                session,
            }
        );

        if (!updatedProduct) {
            throw new Error(
                `Insufficient stock or product unavailable: ${product.name}`
            );
        }

        // 11. Create stock movement in the same transaction.
        const movementData = {
            tenantId: user.tenantId,
            productId: product._id,
            ...(input.type === "adjustment" && operationId
                ? { operationId }
                : {}),
            type: input.type,
            quantity: input.quantity,
            referenceId: input.referenceId
                ? new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(input.referenceId)
                : undefined,
            previousStock,
            newStock,
            warehouse: {
                name: input.warehouse.name.trim(),
                location: input.warehouse.location?.trim(),
            },
            reason: input.reason?.trim(),
            createdBy: user.userId,
        };

        // Do not catch duplicate-key errors here: the transaction
        // owner must be allowed to abort the transaction.
        const [movement] = await StockMovement.create(
            [movementData],
            { session }
        );

        // 12. Record audit history for manual adjustments.
        // An audit failure must roll back the stock update and
        // movement because all three writes share this session.
        let auditLog: unknown;

        if (input.type === "adjustment") {
            auditLog = await AuditLogService.createAuditLog(
                {
                    userId: user.userId,
                    tenantId: user.tenantId,
                },
                {
                    action: "STOCK_ADJUSTMENT",
                    entity: "Product",
                    entityId: product._id,
                    description:
                        input.reason?.trim() ||
                        `Stock adjusted for product ${product.name}`,
                    oldValues: {
                        stockQuantity: previousStock,
                    },
                    newValues: {
                        stockQuantity: newStock,
                        operationId,
                    },
                },
                session
            );
        }

        // 13. Return the result.
        return {
            product: updatedProduct,
            movement,
            previousStock,
            newStock,
            quantityChanged: input.quantity,
            auditLog,
            alreadyProcessed: false,
        };
    }

    /**
     * Get current stock for a product.
     */
    static async getStock(
        user: StockUserContext,
        productId: string
    ) {
        if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(productId)) {
            throw new Error("Invalid product ID");
        }

        const product = await Product.findOne({
            _id: productId,
            tenantId: user.tenantId,
            status: "active",
        }).select("_id name SKU stockQuantity unitId");

        if (!product) {
            throw new Error("Product not found or inactive");
        }

        return product;
    }

    /**
     * Get stock movement history.
     */
    static async getMovements(
        user: StockUserContext,
        options?: {
            productId?: string;
            type?:
            | "purchase"
            | "sale"
            | "purchase_return"
            | "sales_return"
            | "adjustment";
            page?: number;
            limit?: number;
        }
    ) {
        const page = Math.max(
            Math.floor(options?.page ?? 1),
            1
        );

        const limit = Math.min(
            Math.max(
                Math.floor(options?.limit ?? 20),
                1
            ),
            100
        );

        const filter: Record<string, unknown> = {
            tenantId: user.tenantId,
        };

        if (options?.productId) {
            if (!mongoose.isValidObjectId(options.productId)) {
                throw new Error("Invalid product ID");
            }

            filter.productId = new mongoose.Types.ObjectId(
                options.productId
            );
        }

        if (options?.type) {
            filter.type = options.type;
        }

        const skip = (page - 1) * limit;

        const [movements, total] = await Promise.all([
            StockMovement.find(filter)
                .sort({ createdAt: -1, _id: -1 })
                .skip(skip)
                .limit(limit)
                .lean(),

            StockMovement.countDocuments(filter),
        ]);

        return {
            movements,
            pagination: {
                page,
                limit,
                total,
                totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit),
            },
        };
    }
}


========== FILE: G:\Nexvra\billing-software\billing\lib\services\SyncErrors.ts ==========

import mongoose from "mongoose";

import { GENERIC_ERROR_MESSAGE, isInternalError, publicErrorMessage } from "@/lib/api/publicError";

/*
 * Failure handling for offline-sync processing.
 *
 * RETRYABLE  - temporary infrastructure problems (network, database
 *              unavailable, write conflicts). Safe to retry because every
 *              synced business operation is idempotent per operationId.
 * PERMANENT  - business or data errors (insufficient stock, invalid data,
 *              duplicate invoice number, unsupported entity). Retrying can
 *              never succeed, so the operation needs a person to act.
 */

/** Server-side attempt limit; beyond it the operation needs attention. */
export const MAX_SYNC_ATTEMPTS = 8;

/** How long a claim on an operation is valid before it can be reclaimed. */
export const SYNC_LEASE_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000;

export type SyncFailureKind = "RETRYABLE" | "PERMANENT";

export type SyncErrorCode =
    | "INVALID_REQUEST"
    | "OWNER_MISMATCH"
    | "PERMISSION_DENIED"
    | "NOT_FOUND"
    | "OPERATION_ID_CONFLICT"
    | "IN_PROGRESS"
    | "NEEDS_ATTENTION"
    | "PERMANENT_FAILURE"
    | "RETRYABLE_FAILURE";

/** A sync failure with the HTTP status and guidance the client needs. */
export class SyncProcessError extends Error {
    constructor(
        public readonly httpStatus: number,
        public readonly code: SyncErrorCode,
        message: string,
        public readonly retryable: boolean,
        public readonly retryAfterSeconds?: number
    ) {
        super(message);
        this.name = "SyncProcessError";
    }

    toJSON() {
        return {
            success: false,
            code: this.code,
            retryable: this.retryable,
            message: this.message,
            ...(this.retryAfterSeconds !== undefined
                ? { retryAfterSeconds: this.retryAfterSeconds }
                : {}),
        };
    }
}

// MongoDB server error codes that indicate a temporary condition.
const RETRYABLE_SERVER_CODES = new Set([
    6, // HostUnreachable
    7, // HostNotFound
    50, // MaxTimeMSExpired
    89, // NetworkTimeout
    91, // ShutdownInProgress
    112, // WriteConflict
    189, // PrimarySteppedDown
    251, // NoSuchTransaction
    262, // ExceededTimeLimit
    9001, // SocketException
    10107, // NotWritablePrimary
    11600, // InterruptedAtShutdown
    11602, // InterruptedDueToReplStateChange
    13435, // NotPrimaryNoSecondaryOk
    13436, // NotPrimaryOrSecondary
]);

const RETRYABLE_LABELS = [
    "TransientTransactionError",
    "RetryableWriteError",
    "UnknownTransactionCommitResult",
];

// Driver/Mongoose errors caused by the operation's own data: retrying the
// same payload can never succeed, so they stay PERMANENT.
const PERMANENT_DATA_ERROR_NAMES = new Set([
    "CastError",
    "StrictModeError",
    "ObjectParameterError",
    "DivergentArrayError",
]);

const PERMANENT_SERVER_CODES = new Set([
    2, // BadValue
    9, // FailedToParse
    14, // TypeMismatch
    121, // DocumentValidationFailure
    11000, // DuplicateKey
]);

// Built-in JavaScript errors point at a programming bug; their text means
// nothing to a shop and is never stored or shown.
const PROGRAMMING_ERROR_NAMES = new Set([
    "TypeError",
    "ReferenceError",
    "RangeError",
    "SyntaxError",
    "EvalError",
    "URIError",
]);

/** Shown instead of any database, driver or programming error text. */
export const SYNC_INTERNAL_ERROR_MESSAGE =
    "An internal problem occurred while processing this operation";

/** Decide whether an error from processing is temporary or permanent. */
export function classifySyncError(error: unknown): SyncFailureKind {
    const mongo = mongoose.mongo;

    if (
        error instanceof mongo.MongoNetworkError ||
        error instanceof mongo.MongoServerSelectionError ||
        error instanceof mongo.MongoNotConnectedError ||
        error instanceof mongo.MongoTopologyClosedError ||
        error instanceof mongoose.Error.MongooseServerSelectionError
    ) {
        return "RETRYABLE";
    }

    if (
        error instanceof mongo.MongoError &&
        RETRYABLE_LABELS.some((label) => error.hasErrorLabel(label))
    ) {
        return "RETRYABLE";
    }

    if (
        error instanceof mongo.MongoServerError &&
        typeof error.code === "number" &&
        RETRYABLE_SERVER_CODES.has(error.code)
    ) {
        return "RETRYABLE";
    }

    // Mongoose gives up on commands buffered while disconnected.
    if (error instanceof Error && /buffering timed out/i.test(error.message)) {
        return "RETRYABLE";
    }

    // Errors caused by the data itself (invalid IDs, schema rejections,
    // duplicate keys) never succeed on a retry.
    if (error instanceof Error && PERMANENT_DATA_ERROR_NAMES.has(error.name)) {
        return "PERMANENT";
    }

    const code = (error as { code?: unknown } | null)?.code;

    if (typeof code === "number" && PERMANENT_SERVER_CODES.has(code)) {
        return "PERMANENT";
    }

    // Any other database or driver failure (unknown server code, driver
    // error class, connection text) is treated as temporary. The attempt
    // limit (MAX_SYNC_ATTEMPTS) still stops it from being retried forever.
    if (isInternalError(error)) {
        return "RETRYABLE";
    }

    // Business validation errors (plain Error from the services), Mongoose
    // validation errors and anything unexpected need a person.
    return "PERMANENT";
}

/**
 * The text of a processing failure that may be stored on the operation and
 * returned to the client: readable business messages and our own
 * SyncProcessError messages are kept; database, driver and programming
 * error text is replaced by SYNC_INTERNAL_ERROR_MESSAGE.
 */
export function publicSyncErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
    if (error instanceof SyncProcessError) {
        return error.message;
    }

    if (error instanceof Error && PROGRAMMING_ERROR_NAMES.has(error.name)) {
        return SYNC_INTERNAL_ERROR_MESSAGE;
    }

    const message = publicErrorMessage(error, SYNC_INTERNAL_ERROR_MESSAGE);

    return message === GENERIC_ERROR_MESSAGE ? SYNC_INTERNAL_ERROR_MESSAGE : message;
}

/**
 * A message already stored on an operation (records saved before messages
 * were sanitized may hold raw database text), made safe to return.
 */
export function publicStoredSyncMessage(message: string | undefined): string | undefined {
    if (!message) {
        return undefined;
    }

    return publicSyncErrorMessage(new Error(message));
}

/** Status after a failed attempt (attempts already includes this attempt). */
export function statusAfterFailure(
    kind: SyncFailureKind,
    attempts: number
): { status: "FAILED" | "NEEDS_ATTENTION"; failureKind: "RETRYABLE" | "PERMANENT" | "RETRIES_EXHAUSTED" } {
    if (kind === "PERMANENT") {
        return { status: "NEEDS_ATTENTION", failureKind: "PERMANENT" };
    }

    if (attempts >= MAX_SYNC_ATTEMPTS) {
        return { status: "NEEDS_ATTENTION", failureKind: "RETRIES_EXHAUSTED" };
    }

    return { status: "FAILED", failureKind: "RETRYABLE" };
}

import {
    SalesService,
    type CreateSaleInput,
    type SalePolicy,
} from "@/lib/services/SalesService";

import {
    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS,
    SALE_DATE_RULES,
    getGrantedBusinessPermissions,
    getSyncEntityPermission,
} from "@/lib/auth/businessPermissions";

import {
    PurchaseService,
    type CreatePurchaseInput,
} from "@/lib/services/PurchaseService";

import {
    SalesReturnService,
    type CreateSalesReturnInput,
} from "@/lib/services/SalesReturnService";

import {
    PurchaseReturnService,
    type CreatePurchaseReturnInput,
} from "@/lib/services/PurchaseReturnService";

import {
    PaymentService,
    type CreatePaymentInput,
} from "@/lib/services/PaymentService";

import { StockService } from "./StockService";
import { Device } from "../models/Device";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
import { SyncOperation } from "../models/SyncOperation";
import { randomUUID } from "crypto";
import {
    MAX_SYNC_ATTEMPTS,
    SYNC_LEASE_MS,
    SyncProcessError,
    classifySyncError,
    publicStoredSyncMessage,
    publicSyncErrorMessage,
    statusAfterFailure,
} from "./SyncErrors";

// ============================================================
// TYPES
// ============================================================

export interface SyncUserContext {
    tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    // Identity of the authenticated session processing the operation.
    userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    deviceId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
    // The session user's CURRENT role (from the database, not the token).
    roleId?: string;
}

// ============================================================
// PAYLOAD VALIDATION HELPERS
// ============================================================

function getPayloadObject(
    value: unknown
): Record<string, unknown> {
    if (
        value === null ||
        typeof value !== "object" ||
        Array.isArray(value)
    ) {
        throw new Error("Sync payload must be an object");
    }

    return value as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function requiredString(
    value: unknown,
    field: string
): string {
    if (
        typeof value !== "string" ||
        !value.trim()
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `Invalid or missing field: ${field}`
        );
    }

    return value.trim();
}

function optionalString(
    value: unknown,
    field: string
): string | undefined {
    if (
        value === undefined ||
        value === null
    ) {
        return undefined;
    }

    if (typeof value !== "string") {
        throw new Error(
            `Invalid field: ${field}`
        );
    }

    return value;
}

function requiredNumber(
    value: unknown,
    field: string
): number {
    if (
        typeof value !== "number" ||
        !Number.isFinite(value)
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `Invalid or missing number: ${field}`
        );
    }

    return value;
}

function optionalNumber(
    value: unknown,
    field: string
): number | undefined {
    if (
        value === undefined ||
        value === null
    ) {
        return undefined;
    }

    return requiredNumber(value, field);
}

function optionalDate(
    value: unknown,
    field: string
): Date | undefined {
    if (
        value === undefined ||
        value === null ||
        value === ""
    ) {
        return undefined;
    }

    if (
        typeof value !== "string" &&
        !(value instanceof Date)
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            `Invalid date: ${field}`
        );
    }

    const date = new Date(value);

    if (!Number.isFinite(date.getTime())) {
        throw new Error(
            `Invalid date: ${field}`
        );
    }

    return date;
}

function requiredWarehouse(
    value: unknown
): {
    name: string;
    location?: string;
} {
    const warehouse = getPayloadObject(value);

    return {
        name: requiredString(
            warehouse.name,
            "warehouse.name"
        ),
        location: optionalString(
            warehouse.location,
            "warehouse.location"
        ),
    };
}

function requiredItems<T>(
    value: unknown,
    parser: (
        item: unknown,
        index: number
    ) => T
): T[] {
    if (
        !Array.isArray(value) ||
        value.length === 0
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "At least one item is required"
        );
    }

    return value.map(parser);
}

// ============================================================
// SALE PAYLOAD
// ============================================================

function parseSalePayload(
    value: unknown
): CreateSaleInput {
    const p = getPayloadObject(value);

    const allowedPaymentMethods = [
        "cash",
        "bank",
        "upi",
        "card",
        "credit",
    ] as const;

    if (
        p.paymentMethod !== undefined &&
        !allowedPaymentMethods.includes(
            p.paymentMethod as
            (typeof allowedPaymentMethods)[number]
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Invalid sale payment method"
        );
    }

    return {
        invoiceNo: requiredString(
            p.invoiceNo,
            "invoiceNo"
        ),

        customerId: optionalString(
            p.customerId,
            "customerId"
        ),

        saleDate: optionalDate(
            p.saleDate,
            "saleDate"
        ),

        warehouse: requiredWarehouse(
            p.warehouse
        ),

        items: requiredItems(
            p.items,
            (value, index) => {
                const item =
                    getPayloadObject(value);

                return {
                    productId: requiredString(
                        item.productId,
                        `items[${index}].productId`
                    ),

                    quantity: requiredNumber(
                        item.quantity,
                        `items[${index}].quantity`
                    ),

                    unitPrice: optionalNumber(
                        item.unitPrice,
                        `items[${index}].unitPrice`
                    ),

                    discountRate: optionalNumber(
                        item.discountRate,
                        `items[${index}].discountRate`
                    ),

                    taxRate: optionalNumber(
                        item.taxRate,
                        `items[${index}].taxRate`
                    ),
                };
            }
        ),

        paidAmount: optionalNumber(
            p.paidAmount,
            "paidAmount"
        ),

        paymentMethod:
            p.paymentMethod as
            CreateSaleInput["paymentMethod"],

        notes: optionalString(
            p.notes,
            "notes"
        ),
    };
}

// ============================================================
// PURCHASE PAYLOAD
// ============================================================

function parsePurchasePayload(
    value: unknown
): CreatePurchaseInput {
    const p = getPayloadObject(value);

    const allowedPaymentStatuses = [
        "unpaid",
        "partial",
        "paid",
    ] as const;

    if (
        p.paymentStatus !== undefined &&
        !allowedPaymentStatuses.includes(
            p.paymentStatus as
            (typeof allowedPaymentStatuses)[number]
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Invalid purchase payment status"
        );
    }

    return {
        purchaseNo: requiredString(
            p.purchaseNo,
            "purchaseNo"
        ),

        supplierId: requiredString(
            p.supplierId,
            "supplierId"
        ),

        purchaseDate: optionalDate(
            p.purchaseDate,
            "purchaseDate"
        ),

        supplierInvoiceNo: optionalString(
            p.supplierInvoiceNo,
            "supplierInvoiceNo"
        ),

        supplierInvoiceDate: optionalDate(
            p.supplierInvoiceDate,
            "supplierInvoiceDate"
        ),

        dueDate: optionalDate(
            p.dueDate,
            "dueDate"
        ),

        warehouse: requiredWarehouse(
            p.warehouse
        ),

        items: requiredItems(
            p.items,
            (value, index) => {
                const item =
                    getPayloadObject(value);

                return {
                    productId: requiredString(
                        item.productId,
                        `items[${index}].productId`
                    ),

                    quantity: requiredNumber(
                        item.quantity,
                        `items[${index}].quantity`
                    ),

                    unitPrice: requiredNumber(
                        item.unitPrice,
                        `items[${index}].unitPrice`
                    ),

                    discountRate: optionalNumber(
                        item.discountRate,
                        `items[${index}].discountRate`
                    ),

                    taxRate: optionalNumber(
                        item.taxRate,
                        `items[${index}].taxRate`
                    ),
                };
            }
        ),

        shippingCost: optionalNumber(
            p.shippingCost,
            "shippingCost"
        ),

        paidAmount: optionalNumber(
            p.paidAmount,
            "paidAmount"
        ),

        paymentStatus:
            p.paymentStatus as
            CreatePurchaseInput["paymentStatus"],

        notes: optionalString(
            p.notes,
            "notes"
        ),
    };
}

// ============================================================
// SALES RETURN PAYLOAD
// ============================================================

function parseSalesReturnPayload(
    value: unknown
): CreateSalesReturnInput {
    const p = getPayloadObject(value);

    const allowedRefundStatuses = [
        "pending",
        "refunded",
        "not_applicable",
    ] as const;

    if (
        p.refundStatus !== undefined &&
        !allowedRefundStatuses.includes(
            p.refundStatus as
            (typeof allowedRefundStatuses)[number]
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Invalid sales return refund status"
        );
    }

    return {
        salesReturnNo: requiredString(
            p.salesReturnNo,
            "salesReturnNo"
        ),

        saleId: requiredString(
            p.saleId,
            "saleId"
        ),

        customerId: optionalString(
            p.customerId,
            "customerId"
        ),

        returnDate: optionalDate(
            p.returnDate,
            "returnDate"
        ),

        warehouse: requiredWarehouse(
            p.warehouse
        ),

        items: requiredItems(
            p.items,
            (value, index) => {
                const item =
                    getPayloadObject(value);

                return {
                    productId: requiredString(
                        item.productId,
                        `items[${index}].productId`
                    ),

                    quantity: requiredNumber(
                        item.quantity,
                        `items[${index}].quantity`
                    ),
                };
            }
        ),

        refundAmount: optionalNumber(
            p.refundAmount,
            "refundAmount"
        ),

        refundStatus:
            p.refundStatus as
            CreateSalesReturnInput["refundStatus"],

        reason: optionalString(
            p.reason,
            "reason"
        ),

        notes: optionalString(
            p.notes,
            "notes"
        ),
    };
}

// ============================================================
// PURCHASE RETURN PAYLOAD
// ============================================================

function parsePurchaseReturnPayload(
    value: unknown
): CreatePurchaseReturnInput {
    const p = getPayloadObject(value);

    const allowedRefundStatuses = [
        "pending",
        "refunded",
        "not_applicable",
    ] as const;

    if (
        p.refundStatus !== undefined &&
        !allowedRefundStatuses.includes(
            p.refundStatus as
            (typeof allowedRefundStatuses)[number]
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Invalid purchase return refund status"
        );
    }

    return {
        purchaseReturnNo: requiredString(
            p.purchaseReturnNo,
            "purchaseReturnNo"
        ),

        purchaseId: requiredString(
            p.purchaseId,
            "purchaseId"
        ),

        returnDate: optionalDate(
            p.returnDate,
            "returnDate"
        ),

        warehouse: requiredWarehouse(
            p.warehouse
        ),

        items: requiredItems(
            p.items,
            (value, index) => {
                const item =
                    getPayloadObject(value);

                return {
                    productId: requiredString(
                        item.productId,
                        `items[${index}].productId`
                    ),

                    quantity: requiredNumber(
                        item.quantity,
                        `items[${index}].quantity`
                    ),
                };
            }
        ),

        refundAmount: optionalNumber(
            p.refundAmount,
            "refundAmount"
        ),

        refundStatus:
            p.refundStatus as
            CreatePurchaseReturnInput["refundStatus"],

        reason: optionalString(
            p.reason,
            "reason"
        ),

        notes: optionalString(
            p.notes,
            "notes"
        ),
    };
}

// ============================================================
// PAYMENT PAYLOAD
// ============================================================

function parsePaymentPayload(
    value: unknown
): CreatePaymentInput {
    const p = getPayloadObject(value);

    const allowedTypes = [
        "receipt",
        "payment",
        "sales_refund",
        "purchase_refund",
    ] as const;

    const allowedMethods = [
        "cash",
        "upi",
        "card",
        "bank",
        "credit",
        "other",
    ] as const;

    const allowedStatuses = [
        "pending",
        "completed",
        "failed",
        "cancelled",
    ] as const;

    if (
        !allowedTypes.includes(
            p.type as
            (typeof allowedTypes)[number]
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Invalid payment type"
        );
    }

    if (
        !allowedMethods.includes(
            p.paymentMethod as
            (typeof allowedMethods)[number]
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Invalid payment method"
        );
    }

    if (
        p.status !== undefined &&
        !allowedStatuses.includes(
            p.status as
            (typeof allowedStatuses)[number]
        )
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            "Invalid payment status"
        );
    }

    return {
        paymentNo: requiredString(
            p.paymentNo,
            "paymentNo"
        ),

        type: p.type as
            CreatePaymentInput["type"],

        amount: requiredNumber(
            p.amount,
            "amount"
        ),

        paymentMethod: p.paymentMethod as
            CreatePaymentInput["paymentMethod"],

        paymentDate: optionalDate(
            p.paymentDate,
            "paymentDate"
        ),

        customerId: optionalString(
            p.customerId,
            "customerId"
        ),

        supplierId: optionalString(
            p.supplierId,
            "supplierId"
        ),

        saleId: optionalString(
            p.saleId,
            "saleId"
        ),

        purchaseId: optionalString(
            p.purchaseId,
            "purchaseId"
        ),

        salesReturnId: optionalString(
            p.salesReturnId,
            "salesReturnId"
        ),

        purchaseReturnId: optionalString(
            p.purchaseReturnId,
            "purchaseReturnId"
        ),

        referenceNo: optionalString(
            p.referenceNo,
            "referenceNo"
        ),

        notes: optionalString(
            p.notes,
            "notes"
        ),

        status: p.status as
            CreatePaymentInput["status"],
    };
}

// ============================================================
// STOCK ADJUSTMENT PAYLOAD
// ============================================================

function parseStockAdjustmentPayload(
    value: unknown
): {
    productId: string;
    quantity: number;
    warehouse: {
        name: string;
        location?: string;
    };
    reason: string;
} {
    const p = getPayloadObject(value);

    return {
        productId: requiredString(
            p.productId,
            "productId"
        ),

        quantity: requiredNumber(
            p.quantity,
            "quantity"
        ),

        warehouse: requiredWarehouse(
            p.warehouse
        ),

        reason: requiredString(
            p.reason,
            "reason"
        ),
    };
}

// ============================================================
// SYNC SERVICE
// ============================================================

export class SyncService {
    /**
     * Process one uploaded operation for the CURRENT session.
     *
     * Safety rules:
     * - Only the uploading user on the uploading device may process it.
     * - Claims are atomic and leased: a crashed worker's claim expires and
     *   can be reclaimed. Every business operation below is idempotent per
     *   operationId, so re-running a reclaimed operation never duplicates
     *   sales, stock movements or payments.
     * - Temporary failures may be retried (bounded); permanent business
     *   failures go to NEEDS_ATTENTION and are never retried automatically.
     */
    static async processOperation(
        user: SyncUserContext,
        operationId: string
    ) {
        const normalizedOperationId = operationId?.trim();

        if (!user.tenantId || !user.userId || !user.deviceId) {
            throw new SyncProcessError(401, "INVALID_REQUEST", "Session context is required", false);
        }

        if (!normalizedOperationId) {
            throw new SyncProcessError(400, "INVALID_REQUEST", "Operation ID is required", false);
        }

        // 1. Find the operation inside the caller's tenant only.
        const existingOperation = await SyncOperation.findOne({
            tenantId: user.tenantId,
            operationId: normalizedOperationId,
        });

        if (!existingOperation) {
            throw new SyncProcessError(404, "NOT_FOUND", "Sync operation not found", true);
        }

        // 2. Only the uploading user on the uploading device may process it.
        //    Legacy records have no userId: then the session's device must
        //    be the operation's device (checked below against its owner).
        if (
            !existingOperation.deviceId.equals(user.deviceId) ||
            (existingOperation.userId && !existingOperation.userId.equals(user.userId))
        ) {
            throw new SyncProcessError(
                403,
                "OWNER_MISMATCH",
                "This operation belongs to another user or device",
                false
            );
        }

        // 3. Terminal states.
        if (existingOperation.status === "SYNCED") {
            return { success: true, alreadySynced: true, operation: existingOperation };
        }

        if (existingOperation.status === "NEEDS_ATTENTION" || existingOperation.status === "CANCELLED") {
            throw new SyncProcessError(
                422,
                "NEEDS_ATTENTION",
                // Stored text may predate sanitizing: never return it raw.
                existingOperation.errorMessage
                    ? `Operation needs attention: ${publicStoredSyncMessage(existingOperation.errorMessage)}`
                    : "Operation needs attention and will not be retried automatically",
                false
            );
        }

        // 4. Too many attempts already: stop retrying.
        if (existingOperation.status === "FAILED" && existingOperation.attempts >= MAX_SYNC_ATTEMPTS) {
            await SyncOperation.updateOne(
                { _id: existingOperation._id, tenantId: user.tenantId, status: "FAILED" },
                { $set: { status: "NEEDS_ATTENTION", failureKind: "RETRIES_EXHAUSTED" } }
            );
            throw new SyncProcessError(
                422,
                "NEEDS_ATTENTION",
                "Operation failed too many times and needs attention",
                false
            );
        }

        // 4b. Deny by default: an operation type without an explicit
        //     permission mapping is rejected BEFORE it is claimed, so no
        //     business code runs for it. It is flagged for review (status
        //     only; ownership was verified in step 2).
        const mappedPermission = getSyncEntityPermission(existingOperation.entityType);

        if (!mappedPermission) {
            const message = "This type of operation cannot be synchronized; it needs review";

            await SyncOperation.updateOne(
                {
                    _id: existingOperation._id,
                    tenantId: user.tenantId,
                    status: { $in: ["PENDING", "FAILED"] },
                },
                { $set: { status: "NEEDS_ATTENTION", failureKind: "PERMANENT", errorMessage: message } }
            );

            throw new SyncProcessError(403, "PERMISSION_DENIED", message, false);
        }

        // 5. Claim atomically with a lease. Claimable: PENDING, FAILED, or a
        //    PROCESSING claim whose lease expired (crashed/stalled worker).
        //    Legacy PROCESSING records without a lease are reclaimable once
        //    their last attempt is older than the lease duration.
        //    The ownership rules of step 2 are part of the claim itself, so a
        //    record whose owner changed after step 2 is never claimed: the
        //    session's device, and the session's user (or no user at all on a
        //    legacy record; that device's owner is checked in step 6).
        const now = new Date();
        const leaseToken = randomUUID();
        const operation = await SyncOperation.findOneAndUpdate(
            {
                _id: existingOperation._id,
                tenantId: user.tenantId,
                deviceId: user.deviceId,
                attempts: { $lt: MAX_SYNC_ATTEMPTS },
                $and: [
                    // userId: null matches a missing or null userId (legacy).
                    { $or: [{ userId: user.userId }, { userId: null }] },
                    {
                        $or: [
                            { status: { $in: ["PENDING", "FAILED"] } },
                            { status: "PROCESSING", leaseExpiresAt: { $lt: now } },
                            {
                                status: "PROCESSING",
                                leaseExpiresAt: { $exists: false },
                                lastAttemptAt: { $lt: new Date(now.getTime() - SYNC_LEASE_MS) },
                            },
                        ],
                    },
                ],
            },
            {
                $set: {
                    status: "PROCESSING",
                    lastAttemptAt: now,
                    leaseToken,
                    leaseExpiresAt: new Date(now.getTime() + SYNC_LEASE_MS),
                },
                $unset: { errorMessage: "", failureKind: "" },
                $inc: { attempts: 1 },
            },
            { returnDocument: "after" }
        );

        if (!operation) {
            const current = await SyncOperation.findById(existingOperation._id).lean();

            // Not claimed because the owner changed after step 2.
            if (
                current &&
                (!current.deviceId?.equals(user.deviceId) ||
                    (current.userId && !current.userId.equals(user.userId)))
            ) {
                throw new SyncProcessError(
                    403,
                    "OWNER_MISMATCH",
                    "This operation belongs to another user or device",
                    false
                );
            }

            if (current?.status === "SYNCED") {
                return { success: true, alreadySynced: true, operation: current };
            }

            if (current?.status === "PROCESSING") {
                const waitMs = current.leaseExpiresAt
                    ? current.leaseExpiresAt.getTime() - Date.now()
                    : SYNC_LEASE_MS;
                throw new SyncProcessError(
                    409,
                    "IN_PROGRESS",
                    "Operation is already being processed",
                    true,
                    Math.max(1, Math.ceil(waitMs / 1000))
                );
            }

            if (current?.status === "NEEDS_ATTENTION" || (current && current.attempts >= MAX_SYNC_ATTEMPTS)) {
                throw new SyncProcessError(422, "NEEDS_ATTENTION", "Operation needs attention", false);
            }

            throw new SyncProcessError(409, "IN_PROGRESS", "Operation could not be claimed; try again", true, 5);
        }

        try {
            // 6. The device must still be active in this tenant and, for
            //    legacy records, belong to the session's user.
            const device = await Device.findOne({
                _id: operation.deviceId,
                tenantId: user.tenantId,
                status: "active",
            }).lean();

            if (!device) {
                throw new Error("Sync device not found, inactive, or does not belong to this tenant");
            }

            if (!operation.userId && !device.userId.equals(user.userId)) {
                throw new SyncProcessError(
                    403,
                    "OWNER_MISMATCH",
                    "This operation belongs to another user or device",
                    false
                );
            }

            // 7. Business effects are attributed to the uploading user.
            //    (Steps 2 and 6 guarantee this is the session's user.)
            const businessUser = {
                userId: operation.userId ?? device.userId,
                tenantId: user.tenantId,
                deviceId: operation.deviceId,
            };

            // 7b. The user's CURRENT role must allow this operation. A
            //     refusal is permanent: the operation needs review.
            //     (Unmapped types never get here, see step 4b; re-checked
            //     on the claimed record anyway, denying by default.)
            const requiredPermission = getSyncEntityPermission(operation.entityType);

            if (!requiredPermission) {
                throw new SyncProcessError(
                    403,
                    "PERMISSION_DENIED",
                    "This type of operation cannot be synchronized; it needs review",
                    false
                );
            }

            const granted = await getGrantedBusinessPermissions(
                {
                    userId: user.userId.toString(),
                    tenantId: user.tenantId.toString(),
                    roleId: user.roleId,
                },
                [
                    requiredPermission,
                    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesPriceOverride,
                    BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesBackdate,
                ]
            );

            if (!granted.has(requiredPermission)) {
                throw new SyncProcessError(
                    403,
                    "PERMISSION_DENIED",
                    "You no longer have permission for this operation; it needs review",
                    false
                );
            }

            // Sale rules for offline sales: the date window is measured from
            // when the server first received the operation (server time).
            const salePolicy: SalePolicy = {
                allowPriceOverride: granted.has(BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesPriceOverride),
                allowBackdate: granted.has(BUSINESS_PERMISSIONS.salesBackdate),
                referenceTime: operation.createdAt ?? new Date(),
                maxAgeMs: SALE_DATE_RULES.syncMaxAgeMs,
            };

            const payload = getPayloadObject(operation.payload);

            // 8. Idempotent business operation (see the per-entity services).
            await SyncService.runBusinessOperation(businessUser, operation.entityType, payload, operation.operationId, salePolicy);

            // 9. Mark SYNCED. Normally only the lease holder may do this. If
            //    the lease expired meanwhile and another worker reclaimed the
            //    operation, the effect is already committed (idempotent), so
            //    it is still safe to mark it SYNCED.
            const synced =
                (await SyncOperation.findOneAndUpdate(
                    { _id: operation._id, tenantId: user.tenantId, status: "PROCESSING", leaseToken },
                    {
                        $set: { status: "SYNCED", syncedAt: new Date() },
                        $unset: { errorMessage: "", failureKind: "", leaseToken: "", leaseExpiresAt: "" },
                    },
                    { returnDocument: "after" }
                )) ??
                (await SyncOperation.findOneAndUpdate(
                    { _id: operation._id, tenantId: user.tenantId, status: { $in: ["PROCESSING", "SYNCED"] } },
                    {
                        $set: { status: "SYNCED", syncedAt: new Date() },
                        $unset: { errorMessage: "", failureKind: "", leaseToken: "", leaseExpiresAt: "" },
                    },
                    { returnDocument: "after" }
                ));

            if (!synced) {
                // The business effect is committed, but the status could not
                // be recorded. Report a temporary failure: a later retry
                // reclaims the expired lease, finds the existing record
                // (idempotent) and marks it SYNCED.
                throw new SyncProcessError(
                    503,
                    "RETRYABLE_FAILURE",
                    "Operation was applied but its sync status could not be saved; retry later",
                    true
                );
            }

            return { success: true, alreadySynced: false, operation: synced };
        } catch (error: unknown) {
            if (error instanceof SyncProcessError && error.code === "RETRYABLE_FAILURE") {
                // Leave the lease in place; it expires and the retry recovers.
                throw error;
            }

            const kind =
                error instanceof SyncProcessError
                    ? error.retryable ? "RETRYABLE" : "PERMANENT"
                    : classifySyncError(error);
            const next = statusAfterFailure(kind, operation.attempts);

            // Only sanitized text is stored on the operation and returned to
            // the client (no database, driver or programming error detail).
            const errorMessage = publicSyncErrorMessage(error);

            if (!(error instanceof SyncProcessError)) {
                // Server log: error class and code only, never the message
                // (it may hold key values or infrastructure detail).
                const errorCode = (error as { code?: unknown } | null)?.code;
                console.error(
                    "Sync processing error:",
                    error instanceof Error ? error.name : "unknown",
                    typeof errorCode === "number" || typeof errorCode === "string" ? errorCode : ""
                );
            }

            // Release the lease (only if we still hold it).
            await SyncOperation.updateOne(
                { _id: operation._id, tenantId: user.tenantId, status: "PROCESSING", leaseToken },
                {
                    $set: { status: next.status, failureKind: next.failureKind, errorMessage },
                    $unset: { leaseToken: "", leaseExpiresAt: "" },
                }
            ).catch(() => {
                // The lease simply expires; a later attempt recovers.
            });

            if (error instanceof SyncProcessError) {
                throw error;
            }

            if (next.status === "NEEDS_ATTENTION") {
                throw new SyncProcessError(
                    422,
                    kind === "PERMANENT" ? "PERMANENT_FAILURE" : "NEEDS_ATTENTION",
                    kind === "PERMANENT"
                        ? `Sync failed permanently: ${errorMessage}`
                        : "Sync failed too many times and needs attention",
                    false
                );
            }

            throw new SyncProcessError(
                503,
                "RETRYABLE_FAILURE",
                "Temporary problem while syncing; it will be retried",
                true
            );
        }
    }

    private static async runBusinessOperation(
        businessUser: {
            userId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
            tenantId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
            deviceId: mongoose.Types.ObjectId;
        },
        entityType: string,
        payload: Record<string, unknown>,
        operationId: string,
        salePolicy: SalePolicy
    ) {
        const operation = { entityType, operationId };

        switch (operation.entityType) {
            case "Sale": {
                await SalesService.createSale(
                    businessUser,
                    parseSalePayload(payload),
                    operation.operationId,
                    salePolicy
                );
                break;
            }

            case "Purchase": {
                await PurchaseService.createPurchase(
                    businessUser,
                    parsePurchasePayload(payload),
                    operation.operationId
                );
                break;
            }

            case "SalesReturn": {
                await SalesReturnService.createSalesReturn(
                    businessUser,
                    parseSalesReturnPayload(payload),
                    operation.operationId
                );
                break;
            }

            case "PurchaseReturn": {
                await PurchaseReturnService.createPurchaseReturn(
                    businessUser,
                    parsePurchaseReturnPayload(payload),
                    operation.operationId
                );
                break;
            }

            case "Payment": {
                await PaymentService.createPayment(
                    businessUser,
                    parsePaymentPayload(payload),
                    operation.operationId
                );
                break;
            }

            case "StockAdjustment": {
                const stockAdjustment =
                    parseStockAdjustmentPayload(payload);

                await StockService.changeStock(
                    businessUser,
                    {
                        productId:
                            stockAdjustment.productId,

                        quantity:
                            stockAdjustment.quantity,

                        type: "adjustment",

                        warehouse: {
                            name:
                                stockAdjustment.warehouse.name,

                            location:
                                stockAdjustment.warehouse.location,
                        },

                        reason:
                            stockAdjustment.reason,
                    },
                    undefined,
                    operation.operationId
                );

                break;
            }

            case "Customer": {
                throw new Error(
                    "Customer synchronization is not implemented yet"
                );
            }

            case "Supplier": {
                throw new Error(
                    "Supplier synchronization is not implemented yet"
                );
            }

            case "Product": {
                throw new Error(
                    "Product synchronization is not implemented yet"
                );
            }

            default: {
                throw new Error(
                    `Unsupported sync entity: ${operation.entityType}`
                );
            }
        }
    }
}
